\DLine{AB01-PDF0343-body-L035}{}{}{verum est, in codice et in Platone multa exciderunt, quorum reliquiae sunt verba Italicis lit-}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0343-body-L036}{}{}{teris impressa; qua re finis prioris solutionis et initium posterioris desiderantur. — Cognito}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0343-body-L037}{}{}{radio epicycli, seu NK $=$ 5\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 15′ e partibus quarum AE 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} complectitur, ex angulo NEK nu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0343-body-L038}{}{}{per invento statim NE deducitur, scilicet distantia Terrae ab epicycli centro; per quam, data}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0343-body-L039}{}{}{anomalia HNG, problema resolvitur ut antea pro syzygiis mediis (v. adn. \textbf{\textit{d}}) factum est; pro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0343-body-L040}{}{}{venit distantia GE Lunae a centro Terrae.}
\DSource{0344}{255}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0344-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent Lacuna hoc modo restitui potest: « sinus huius summae est semidiametrus epicycli de-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L002}{}{}{« clivis; [\textit{per eum divide 5}\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} \textit{15′, et quod exibit in 60}\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} \textit{multiplica; proveniet distantia}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L003}{}{}{« \textit{centri epicycli a Terra.}] Ea utere eodem modo quo supra usus es [\textit{distantia apogea eius-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L004}{}{}{« \textit{dem centri in syzygiis;}] invenies hypotenusam Lunae aequatam ad declivitatem epicycli » (1).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0344-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L005}{}{}{\Indent « [\textit{Si aliter vis, absque tabularum usu, eandem distantiam supputare, distantiam}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L006}{}{}{« \textit{apogeam centri epicycli et semidiametrum epicycli 5}\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} \textit{15′ sume, ut supra fecisti; hinc}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L007}{}{}{« \textit{eodem quem diximus modo hypotenusam Lunae quaere.}] Postea sume duplam elonga-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L008}{}{}{« tionem ..... ».}
\par\vspace{-0.12mm}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{152.45mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0344-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L009}{}{}{\Indent Haec nova solutio in hoc consistit, quod, loco supputandi distantiam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L010}{}{}{Lunae quando epicycli centrum a Terra quantitate NE distat, calculus inci-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L011}{}{}{pitur ponendo AE pro NE; postea quaeritur correctio ei afferenda. Sit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L012}{}{}{AE $=$ 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}, distantia apogea centri epicycli; eadem via quae in adn. \textbf{\textit{d}} ex-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L013}{}{}{ponitur, et semper radio AG′ $=$ 5\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} $\qfrac{1}{4}$ adhibendo, supputatur EG′ scilicet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L014}{}{}{\textit{hypotenusa Lunae.} Sit postea EN vera distantia centri epicycli a Terra;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L015}{}{}{vera distantia Lunae erit EG. Cum anguli in E valde sint parvi, et NA}
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L016}{}{}{quoque parva respectu AE, triangulum EGG′ longissimum erit, qua re,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L017}{}{}{multum proxime, EG′ $=$ EG $+$ GG′ $=$ EG $+$ NA. Et quoniam NA $=$ AE $-$}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L018}{}{}{NE $=$ 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} $-$ NE $=$ \textit{portio duplae excentricitatis} (p. 79, l. 6), erit proxime}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L019}{}{}{distantia Lunae quaesitae EG $=$ EG′ $-$ NA $=$ (\textit{hypotenusa Lunae ad de-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L020}{}{}{\textit{clivitatem epicycli aequata}) $-$ (\textit{portio duplae excentricitatis}). Adproxi-}
\par\vspace{-0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L021}{}{}{matio semper sufficit, nam angulus in G′, nempe EG′G $=$ H′EG′ nunquam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L022}{}{}{5° 1′ superat; angulus G′EG minor illo erit, quia in theoria Lunae semper}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L023}{}{}{GG′ $<$ EG. Quoad p. 79, l. 7, vide adn. \textbf{\textit{g}}, — Cum centrum N epicycli ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L024}{}{}{punctum apogeum excentrici pervenit, distantia NA nulla fit. Cum vero}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L025}{}{}{centrum epicycli ad punctum perigeum pervenit, eadem distantia fit ma-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L026}{}{}{xima, et quidem duplo excentricitatis circuli excentrici aequalis. Centro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L027}{}{}{epicycli alios locos, quam perigeum et apogeum, occupante, distantia NA}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{33.55mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0344-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0344-F01}\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{-0.43mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L028}{}{}{semper est minor dupla excentricitate, ita ut \textit{portio} vel fractio quaedam \textit{duplae excentrici-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L029}{}{}{\textit{tatis} appellari possit; quo nomine, ut vides, Albatenius revera usus est. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0344-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L030}{}{}{\Indent Formula trigonometrica quam \Name{al–Battānī} p. 78–79 ad supputandam NE praebet, facile}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L031}{}{}{derivatur a constructionibus geometricis \Name{Ptolemaei}, \textit{Alm.} V, 13 (ed. Halma t. I, p. 334–36).}
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.372\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0344-body-F02}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0344-F02}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.608\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.392\FullSourceWidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L032}{}{}{Producatur NE usque quo in L rectam FL ad angulos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L033}{}{}{rectos secet; erit AEN arcus duplae elongationis 2 (\MoonSym{} $-$ \SunSym{}),}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L034}{}{}{et FE excentricitas \textgreek{ε} ($=$ 10\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 19′). Manifeste in triangulo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L035}{}{}{rectangulo FEL}
\par\vspace{1.58mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L036}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{56.75mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$FL = \dfrac{FE \times \sin FEL}{R} = \dfrac{\text{\textgreek{ε}} \sin FEL}{R}\,,$}}}
\par\vspace{1.39mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L037}{}{}{et, quoniam sin \textit{FEL} $=$ sin [180° $-$ 2 (\MoonSym{} $-$ \SunSym{})] $=$ sin 2}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L038}{}{}{(\MoonSym{} $-$ \SunSym{}), erit}
\par\vspace{-0.29mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L039}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{57.40mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$FL = \dfrac{\text{\textgreek{ε}} \sin 2\,(\MoonSym{} - \SunSym{})}{R}\,.$}}}
\par\vspace{1.88mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-body-L040}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}Similiter}\rlap{\hspace*{56.76mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$EL = \dfrac{\text{\textgreek{ε}} \cos 2\,(\MoonSym{} - \SunSym{})}{R}\,.$}}}
\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}{\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{5.07mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0344-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0344-notes-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) I. e. distantiam quaesitam Lunae a centro Terrae; cfr. p. 78, l. 24.}
}
\DSource{0345}{256}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr -0.09mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L001}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}Qua re}\rlap{\hspace*{95.13mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$NL =$ latus aequatum $= \sqrt{\text{radius}^{2}\ \text{excentrici} - \left(\dfrac{\text{\textgreek{ε}} \sin 2\,(\MoonSym{} - \SunSym{})}{R}\right)^{2}}\,.$}}}
\par\vspace{-0.95mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L002}{}{}{At radius excentrici \textit{FN} $=$ \textit{AE} $-$ \textgreek{ε} $=$ 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} $-$ \textgreek{ε}; unde}
\par\vspace{-1.44mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L003}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{94.41mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$NL = \sqrt{(60^{p} - \text{\textgreek{ε}})^{2} - \left(\dfrac{\text{\textgreek{ε}} \sin 2\,(\MoonSym{} - \SunSym{})}{R}\right)^{2}}$}}}
\par\vspace{-2.06mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L004}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(\textgreek{α})}\rlap{\hspace*{94.20mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$NE = NL - EL = \sqrt{(60^{p} - \text{\textgreek{ε}})^{2} - \left(\dfrac{\text{\textgreek{ε}} \sin 2\,(\MoonSym{} - \SunSym{})}{R}\right)^{2}} - \dfrac{\text{\textgreek{ε}} \cos 2\,(\MoonSym{} - \SunSym{})}{R}\,.$}}}
\par\vspace{4.20mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0345-H05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L005}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{g}}) — \textit{Pag. 79, l. 7.}}}
\par\vspace{4.49mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0345-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L006}{}{}{\Indent [Repetenda hic sunt quae p. 254, sub fine adn. \textbf{\textit{d}} huius capitis dixi. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{5.31mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0345-H07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L007}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{h}}) — \textit{Pag. 79, l. 9-12.}}}
\par\vspace{3.36mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0345-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L008}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Distantiam geocentricam Solis} supputare.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0345-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L009}{}{}{\Indent Regula ad hoc inserviens eadem est qua distantia Lunae in syzygiis (adn. \textbf{\textit{d}}) supputatur,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L010}{}{}{cum pro sinu aequationis centri Lunae (5\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 15′) adhibeatur sinus aequationis Solis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0345-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L011}{}{}{\Indent Numerus ita proveniens indicat distantiam Solis cum distantia media 60 partium, ut pro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L012}{}{}{Luna, sumatur. Oportet nunc eam in radios terrestres convertere, quorum media Solis distantia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L013}{}{}{(iuxta cap. XXX, p. 61) 1108 complectitur; itaque numerus praecedenti supputatione repertus}
\par\vspace{-0.93mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L014}{}{}{multiplicandus videri potest in $\dfrac{1108}{60} =$ 18\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 28′ 0″. At probabiliter consideravit \Name{al–Battānī}}
\par\vspace{-0.49mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L015}{}{}{distantiam \textit{lunarem} 60 partium \textit{mediam} esse respectu epicycli, sed \textit{maximam} respectu ex-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L016}{}{}{centrici; qua re non iniuria loco mediae distantiae Solis (1108 radiorum) maluit numerum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L017}{}{}{medium inter mediam et maximam (1146) Solis distantiam sumere, scilicet 1127. Tunc 1127 : 60}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L018}{}{}{$=$ 18\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 47′ 0″ erit factor quo opus est uti ad nuper dictam reductionem conficiendam.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0345-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L019}{}{}{\Indent Partes 38 \ArabicRun{لح} codicis manifestus sunt error amanuensium pro \ArabicRun{ىح} seu \ArabicRun{يح} 18; in minutis, tam}
\par\vspace{0.47mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L020}{}{}{in cod. quam apud Platonem, perperam 46′ \ArabicRun{مو} loco 47′ \ArabicRun{مر} exstant. Quo modo autem secundae}
\par\vspace{0.03mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L021}{}{}{ortae sint (24″ in cod., 50″ apud Plat.) non intelligo; falsae omnino videntur.}
\par\vspace{5.29mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0345-H22}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L022}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{i}}) — \textit{Pag. 79, l. 34-39.}}}
\par\vspace{3.38mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0345-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L023}{}{}{\Indent Luna in ecliptica exstante, eius \textit{distantiam zenithalem} \textgreek{ζ} et \textit{altitudinem h} supputare.}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.340\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0345-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0345-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.640\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.360\FullSourceWidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0345-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent Sit ABGD meridianus, BED horizon, ZHT ecliptica, A}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L025}{}{}{zenith, H Luna, Z gradus eclipticae culminans scilicet me-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L026}{}{}{dium Caeli; erit ZA distantia zenithalis gradus culminantis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L027}{}{}{vel complementum eius altitudinis; HE $=$ \textit{h} $=$ 90° $-$ \textgreek{ζ}; T $=$}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L028}{}{}{gradus ascendens. \Name{Ptolemaeus} II, 12 (ed. Halma t. I,}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L029}{}{}{p. 130–31) demonstrat}
\par\vspace{0.33mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L030}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{60.69mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\dfrac{\text{chorda } 2ZB}{\text{chorda } 2BA} = \dfrac{\text{chorda } 2ZT}{\text{chorda } 2TH} \times \dfrac{\text{chorda } 2HE}{\text{chorda } 2EA}$}}}
\par\vspace{0.24mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L031}{}{}{Si pro chordis adhibeantur sinus:}
\par\vspace{0.35mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L032}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{60.27mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\dfrac{\text{sin alt. grad. culm.}}{R} = \dfrac{\text{sin dist. gr. culm. ab ascend.}}{\text{sin dist. \MoonSym{} ab ascend.}} \times \dfrac{\sin h}{R}\,,$}}}
\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L033}{}{}{unde regula \Name{al–Battānī}:}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0345-body-L034}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(\textgreek{α})}\rlap{\hspace*{94.78mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\cos \text{\textgreek{ζ}} = \sin h = R\,\dfrac{\text{sin dist. \MoonSym{} ab ascend.}}{\text{sin dist. gr. culm. ab asc.}} \times \dfrac{\text{sin alt. gr. culm.}}{R}\,.$}}}
\DSource{0346}{257}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 4.62mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0346-H01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L001}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{k}}) — \textit{Pag. 79, l. 40—pag. 80, l. 7.}}}
\par\vspace{4.27mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0346-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L002}{}{}{\Indent Luna in ecliptica exstante (1), ex data distantia zenithali \textgreek{ζ} Lunae, nempe complemento}
\par\vspace{0.04mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L003}{}{}{altitudinis \textit{h}, \textit{angulos quos ecliptica cum circulo verticali} per Lunam transeunti efficit co-}
\par\vspace{0.12mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L004}{}{}{gnoscere.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0346-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L005}{}{}{\Indent Angulus ecliptica et verticali interceptus appellatur \textit{angulus latitudinis}; eius comple-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L006}{}{}{mentum est \textit{angulus longitudinis}, interceptus circulo verticali et circulo per gradum longi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L007}{}{}{tudinis Lunae et per eclipticae polos transeunti.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0346-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L008}{}{}{\Indent Angulus latitudinis est rectus cum Luna 90° ab ascendente distet. Si longinquitas maior}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L009}{}{}{vel minor est 90°, et altitudo australis, regula \Name{al-Battānī} est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L010}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(\textgreek{α})}\rlap{\hspace*{94.07mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{sin ang. long. $=$ cos ang. lat. $= \dfrac{\text{cos dist. \MoonSym{} ab asc.}}{\text{sin dist. \MoonSym{} ab asc.}} \times \dfrac{\sin h}{\cos h} \times R.$}}}
\par\vspace{2.36mm}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{124.30mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0346-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L011}{}{}{\Indent Hoc e constructione geometrica \Name{Ptolemaei} II, 12 (ed.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L012}{}{}{Halma t. I, p. 131–32) deducitur: Sit ABGD meridianus, BED}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L013}{}{}{horizon, A zenith, ZHTN ecliptica, AHEG verticalis, ZHA an-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L014}{}{}{gulus latitudinis. Ex II, tanquam polo sumpto, arcus KLM circuli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L015}{}{}{maximi describatur; AE, EM, KM erunt quadrantes maximo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L016}{}{}{rum circulorum, et KL arcus qui metitur angulum latitudinis.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L017}{}{}{Ptolemaeus demonstrat}
\par\vspace{0.41mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L018}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{62.52mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\dfrac{\text{chorda } 2HE}{\text{chorda } 2EK} = \dfrac{\text{chorda } 2HT}{\text{chorda } 2TL} \times \dfrac{\text{chorda } 2LM}{\text{chorda } 2KM}\,;$}}}
\par\vspace{0.23mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L019}{}{}{sinibus pro chordis positis}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{61.70mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0346-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0346-F01}\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{-0.42mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L020}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{93.90mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\dfrac{\sin h}{\cos h} = \dfrac{\text{sin dist. \MoonSym{} ab asc.}}{\text{cos dist. \MoonSym{} ab asc.}} \times \dfrac{\text{cos ang. lat.}}{R}\,,$}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L021}{}{}{unde regula \Name{Albateniana}. — De iis quae sequuntur cfr. adn. \textbf{\textit{l}}.}
\par\vspace{5.76mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0346-H22}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L022}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{l}}) — \textit{Pag. 80, l. 7-9.}}}
\par\vspace{3.31mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0346-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L023}{}{}{\Indent [« \textit{At si altitudo est supra horizontem borealem} » (i. e. cum gradus eclipticae culmi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L024}{}{}{nans est a septentrionibus zenith) « \textit{res invertitur; nam arcus} ..... ». — Falsum, nam arcus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L025}{}{}{semper erit angulus longitudinis ut antea. Quod mutatur est signum parallaxis in latitudine.}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L026}{}{}{— \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0346-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L027}{}{}{\Indent Error ab \Name{al–Battānī} non provenit; eum esse ineptissimam interpolationem illius lectoris}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L028}{}{}{qui, ut vidimus videbimusque, alia quoque stultissime vitiavit, satis patet ex iis quae statim}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L029}{}{}{infra sequuntur: « sed hoc non evenit in ceteris (2) regionibus ..... », ubi manifeste de signo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L030}{}{}{parallaxis lunaris agitur. — Formulam quoque pag. 81, l. 31–33, interpolator similiter corrupit.}
\par\vspace{4.59mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0346-H31}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L031}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{m}}) — \textit{Pag. 80, l. 37-39.}}}
\par\vspace{3.35mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0346-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L032}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Parallaxis Solis}, cum e tabulis sumitur, \textit{augenda ipsius decima octava parte} « ob}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L033}{}{}{« diversitatem quae distantiae Solis a Terra contigit ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0346-P08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L034}{}{}{\Indent Melius dici poterat: « ob mutatam a nobis distantiam Ptolemaicam Solis a centro Terrae ».}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-body-L035}{}{}{In XXX cap., p. 61, maximam distantiam 1146 radiorum terrestrium, minimam 1070 statuit;}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{1.76mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0346-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Cum extra eclipticam sit, correctio neces-}
\par\vspace{0.06mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-notes_left-L002}{}{}{saria est quae p. 82, l. 8-26 et adn. \textbf{\textit{r}} exponitur.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{1.80mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0346-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (2) Exspectandum erat « nostris »; vide p. 81,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0346-notes_right-L002}{}{}{l. 15-18, ubi res clarius exponitur.}
\end{minipage}
\par\vspace{5mm}\hfill{\fontsize{8}{10}\selectfont 33}\hspace{10mm}
\DSource{0347}{258}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L001}{}{}{\Name{Ptolemaeus} maximam 1210 invenerat, nempe 64 radiis maiorem quam Albatenianam. Et}
\par\vspace{-0.96mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L002}{}{}{cum $\dfrac{1210}{64} = 18\dfrac{29}{32}$ sit, \Name{al–Battānī} autem tabulam parallaxium Solis ex \textit{Almagesto} sumpse-}
\par\vspace{-0.43mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L003}{}{}{rit, manifeste numeris ibi repertis $\qfrac{1}{18}$ ipsorum addi debet. Lectio igitur codicis $\qfrac{1}{8}$ falsa est.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0347-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L004}{}{}{\Indent Distantiae diminutio \Name{Albateniana} est parva mutatio in peius, nam quantitas \Name{Ptole-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L005}{}{}{\Name{maica} iam multo minor erat vera. Astronomi hodierni mediam Solis distantiam 23430 radio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L006}{}{}{rum circiter faciunt, parallaxim 8″,80.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0347-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L007}{}{}{\Indent Parallaxis iuxta \Name{Ptolemaeum} est 2′ 51″; mutatis distantiis, \Name{al–Battānī} invenit:}
\par\vspace{0.26mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L008}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{78.27mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{sin parall. \SunSym{} apogei $= 60^{p}\,\dfrac{1}{1146} =$ 3′ 8″ 29‴;}}\rlap{\hspace*{130.07mm}parall. 3′}\rlap{\hspace*{155.47mm}\llap{0″}}}
\par\vspace{1.17mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L009}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{77.51mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{sin parall. \SunSym{} perigei $= 60^{p}\,\dfrac{1}{1070} =$ 3′ 21″ 52‴;}}\rlap{\hspace*{129.93mm}parall. 3′ 13″,}}
\par\vspace{0.64mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L010}{}{}{unde differentia tredecim secundarum ab apogeo ad perigeum quae p. 80. l. ult., memoratur.}
\par\vspace{4.53mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0347-H11}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L011}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{n}}) — \textit{Pag. 81, l. 4-6.}}}
\par\vspace{3.32mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0347-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L012}{}{}{\Indent Regulae facile deducuntur e constructionibus geometricis \Name{Ptolemaei}; cfr. adn. \textbf{\textit{a}}, p. 251.}
\par\vspace{4.57mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0347-H13}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L013}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{o}}) — \textit{Pag. 81, l. 23-34.}}}
\par\vspace{3.32mm}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.249\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0347-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0347-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.731\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.269\FullSourceWidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0347-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L014}{}{}{\Indent Luna in ecliptica exstante, supputare \textit{angulum ecliptica et}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L015}{}{}{\textit{circulo verticali} per Lunam transeunti \textit{interceptum}, cum Luna in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L016}{}{}{ascendente sit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0347-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L017}{}{}{\Indent Iubet antea \textit{latitudinem aequatam} climatis deprehendere, scili-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L018}{}{}{cet latitudinem loci terrestris dati $\mp$ declinationem gradus eclipticae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L019}{}{}{culminantis. Quo facto formula est:}
\par\vspace{0.41mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L020}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(\textgreek{α})}\rlap{\hspace*{68.21mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{sin ang. long. $=$ cos ang. lat. $= R\,\dfrac{\text{cos latit. aequatae}}{\text{sin dist. \MoonSym{} a gr. culm.}}\,.$}}}
\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{-0.29mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0347-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L021}{}{}{\Indent Regulam probabiliter hoc modo reperit. Sit H ascendens et Luna, G gradus eclipticae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L022}{}{}{culminans; manifeste ZHG erit angulus latitudinis, cuius complementum GHB angulus longi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L023}{}{}{tudinis; GB altitudo gradus culminantis, quae (ob hoc ipsum cap., p. 79, et cap. XV, p. 30)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L024}{}{}{est summa aut differentia complementi latitudinis \textgreek{φ} urbis et declinationis \textgreek{δ} gradus culminantis.}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L025}{}{}{In triangulo sphaerico rectangulo GBH est (cfr. p. 181, adn. 1)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L026}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{93.15mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{sin \textit{GH} : sin \textit{GB} $=$ sin \textit{HBG} : sin \textit{GHB}.}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L027}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}At}\rlap{\hspace*{31.05mm}sin \textit{GB} $=$ sin $\bigl[$(90° $-$ \textgreek{φ}) $\pm$ \textgreek{δ}$\bigr]$ $=$ cos (\textgreek{φ} $\mp$ \textgreek{δ}) $=$ cos latit. aequatae;}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L028}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}ergo}\rlap{\hspace*{40.47mm}sin dist. \MoonSym{} a gr. culm. : cos lat. aeq. $= R$ : sin ang. long.,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L029}{}{}{unde formula \Name{al-Battānī}.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0347-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L030}{}{}{\Indent [Quae Italicis litteris impressa sequuntur, falsa sunt, et ab eodem interpolatore prove-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L031}{}{}{niunt qui similem errorem p. 80, l. 7–9 addidit (cfr. adn. \textbf{\textit{l}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0347-P08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L032}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Ptolemaeus} hoc problema neglexerat, nam ad eius solutionem regula generalis, in adn. \textbf{\textit{k}},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L033}{}{}{p. 257 enunciata, sufficere poterat. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{4.49mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0347-H34}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L034}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{p}}) — \textit{Pag. 81, l. 38—pag. 82, l. 4.}}}
\par\vspace{3.32mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0347-P09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L035}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Angulum latitudinis} supputare cum Luna sit in meridiano et in ecliptica.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0347-P10}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L036}{}{}{\Indent Aut regula generalis (p. 80 et adn. \textbf{\textit{k}}) est adhibenda, aut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0347-body-L037}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(\textgreek{α})}\rlap{\hspace*{93.95mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{sin ang. long. $=$ cos ang. lat. $= R\,\dfrac{\text{sin decl. gr. culm.} \times \text{cos dist. \MoonSym{} ab Ariete vel Libra}}{\text{cos decl. gr. culm.} \times \text{sin dist. \MoonSym{} ab Ariete vel Libra}}\,.$}}}
\DSource{0348}{259}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr -3.34mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\par\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{123.09mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0348-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent In \textit{Almagesto} hoc peculiare problema non considera-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L002}{}{}{tur. Solutio \Name{Albateniana} probabiliter auxilio theorematis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L003}{}{}{\Name{Menelai} (p. 192) inventa est, iuxta Graecorum morem. Sit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L004}{}{}{AEB meridianus, {\MoonFont ♈} initium Arietis, H gradus eclipticae cul-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L005}{}{}{minans et gradus Lunae. Ex H, tanquam polo sumpto, arcus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L006}{}{}{KLC circuli maximi describatur; erunt HK, EC, KC qua-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L007}{}{}{drantes circulorum maximorum; ergo KL metietur angulum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L008}{}{}{LHE $=$ AHG $=$ ang. latit., cum sit nunc meridianus ipse cir-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L009}{}{}{culus verticalis. Theoremate Menelai, sinibus pro chordis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L010}{}{}{positis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L011}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{59.67mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\dfrac{\sin HE}{\sin EK} = \dfrac{\sin H\text{{\MoonFont ♈}}}{\sin \text{{\MoonFont ♈}}L} \times \dfrac{\sin LC}{\sin KC}\,,$}}}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{62.91mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0348-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0348-F01}\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{0.95mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L012}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{93.30mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\dfrac{\text{sin decl. gr. culm.}}{\text{cos decl. gr. culm.}} = \dfrac{\text{sin dist. \MoonSym{} ab {\MoonFont ♈}}}{\text{cos dist. \MoonSym{} ab {\MoonFont ♈}}} \times \dfrac{\text{sin (90°} - \text{ang. lat.)}}{R}\,.$}}}
\par\vspace{4.13mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0348-H13}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L013}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{q}}) — \textit{Pag. 82, l. 5-8.}}}
\par\vspace{3.31mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0348-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L014}{}{}{\Indent Locus procul dubio corruptus aut interpolatus. Quae nunc e codice et Platone colligun-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L015}{}{}{tur falsa sunt, scilicet arcum horizontis, ascendente et directione (azimuth) Lunae interceptum,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L016}{}{}{aequari angulo latitudinis. Nam si Luna in horizonte consistat, arcus ille horizontis manifeste}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L017}{}{}{in nihilum redigetur, quod e contra angulo latitudinis minime continget. [Fortasse scripserat}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L018}{}{}{\Name{al–Battānī} angulum illum supputandum esse inter partem eclipticae a Luna ad medium Cae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L019}{}{}{lum progredientem et partem verticalis a Luna ad zenith; arcum horizontis inter eclipticam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L020}{}{}{(vel ascendens) et verticalem, ab austro aut a septentrionibus eclipticae exstare, prout angu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L021}{}{}{lus latitudinis a borea aut ab austro eclipticae sit. Tamen haec restitutio summopere est dubia.}
\par\vspace{0.12mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L022}{}{}{— \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0348-H23}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L023}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{r}}) — \textit{Pag. 82, l. 8-26.}}}
\par\vspace{3.38mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0348-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Correctio distantiae zenithalis} Lunae et \textit{correctio angulorum,} cum Luna extra ecli-}
\par\vspace{-0.14mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L025}{}{}{pticam sit.}
\par\vspace{0.15mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0348-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L026}{}{}{\Indent I. \textit{Correctio distantiae zenithalis.} — Sit \textgreek{ζ} distantia zenithalis gradus longitudinis Lunae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L027}{}{}{quae formula adn. \textbf{\textit{h}}, p. 256, cognoscitur; sit \textit{z} distantia zenithalis Lunae, \textgreek{β} Lunae latitudo;}
\par\vspace{0.18mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L028}{}{}{regula \Name{al–Battānī} est}
\par\vspace{-1.41mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L029}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(\textgreek{α})}\rlap{\hspace*{93.46mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\sin z = \sqrt{\sin^{2}\left[\text{\textgreek{ζ}} \mp \text{arc}\left(\dfrac{\text{sin \textgreek{β} sin ang. latit.}}{R}\right)\right] + \left(\dfrac{\text{sin \textgreek{β} sin ang. long.}}{R}\right)^{2}}\,.$}}}
\par\vspace{2.36mm}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.279\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0348-body-F02}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0348-F02}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.701\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.299\FullSourceWidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0348-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L030}{}{}{\Indent Quo modo auctor hanc formulam repererit investigemus. Ea-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L031}{}{}{dem figura utamur quam \textit{Almag.} V, 19 (ed. Halma t. I, p. 370–72)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L032}{}{}{habet; est GA ecliptica, Q eius polus, Z zenith, BE et BD duae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L033}{}{}{Lunae latitudines (borealis nempe et australis, prout alterutra su-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L034}{}{}{mitur), B gradus longitudinis Lunae, demum ZT, ZK, ZD arcus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L035}{}{}{circulorum verticalium. Ducantur arcus EL, KD perpendiculares}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L036}{}{}{ad ZK; erit ABZ angulus latitudinis, cuius complementum EBL}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L037}{}{}{angulus longitudinis.}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0348-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L038}{}{}{\Indent In triangulo sphaerico rectangulo ELB ($=$ BKD) est (cfr.}
\par\vspace{0.13mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L039}{}{}{p. 181, adn. 1):}
\par\vspace{-0.92mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0348-body-L040}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{66.26mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\sin EL = \sin DK = \dfrac{\sin EB \times \sin EBL}{R} = \dfrac{\text{sin \textgreek{β} sin ang. long.}}{R}\,.$}}}
\end{minipage}\par
\DSource{0349}{260}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0349-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent Si, ob parvitatem triangulorum, figuram tanquam rectilineam consideremus, debet esse}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L002}{}{}{ang. LEB $=$ 90° $-$ EBL $=$ 90° $-$ ang. long. $=$ ang. latit., qua re in triangulo sphaerico ELB vel}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L003}{}{}{BKD \textit{proxime} habetur:}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L004}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{93.83mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\sin BL = \sin KB = \dfrac{\sin EB \times \sin LEB}{R} = \dfrac{\text{sin \textgreek{β} sin ang. latit.}}{R}\,.$}}}
\par\vspace{0.60mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0349-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L005}{}{}{\Indent Si Luna est borealis, ZL $=$ ZB $-$ BL $=$ \textgreek{ζ} $-$ BL; si australis, ZK $=$ ZB $+$ KB $=$ \textgreek{ζ} $+$ BL;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L006}{}{}{ergo, ob aequationes nuper inventas, \textit{proxime} saltem erit}
\par\vspace{-0.21mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L007}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}$\sin ZL = \sin\left[\text{\textgreek{ζ}} - \text{arc}\left(\dfrac{\text{sin \textgreek{β} sin ang. latit.}}{R}\right)\right],$}\rlap{\hspace*{187.68mm}\llap{$\sin ZK = \sin\left[\text{\textgreek{ζ}} + \text{arc}\left(\dfrac{\text{sin \textgreek{β} sin ang. latit.}}{R}\right)\right].$}}}
\par\vspace{-0.46mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0349-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L008}{}{}{\Indent Adproximatio paulo maior est quam quae \textit{Almagestum} habet; ibi enim simplicius ponitur}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L009}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{45.44mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$BL = \dfrac{\text{\textgreek{β} sin ang. latit.}}{R}\,,$}}\rlap{\hspace*{141.71mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$EL = \dfrac{\text{\textgreek{β} sin ang. long.}}{R}\,,$}}}
\par\vspace{1.13mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L010}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{44.39mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$ZL = \text{\textgreek{ζ}} - \dfrac{\text{\textgreek{β} sin ang. latit.}}{R}\,,$}}\rlap{\hspace*{142.92mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$ZK = \text{\textgreek{ζ}} + \dfrac{\text{\textgreek{β} sin ang. latit.}}{R}\,.$}}}
\par\vspace{0.44mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0349-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L011}{}{}{\Indent Restat ut ZE $=$ \textgreek{ζ} supputetur. \Name{Ptolemaeus} (cfr. quoque ed. Halma t. I, p. 369–370)}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L012}{}{}{triangulum ZEL rectilineum ponit, invenitque}
\par\vspace{-1.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L013}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{93.72mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$z^{2} = \overline{ZL}^{2} + \overline{EL}^{2} = \left(\text{\textgreek{ζ}} \mp \dfrac{\text{\textgreek{β} sin ang. latit.}}{R}\right)^{2} + \left(\dfrac{\text{\textgreek{β} sin ang. long.}}{R}\right)^{2}.$}}}
\par\vspace{-0.39mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0349-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L014}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Al–Battānī} manifeste noluit simplices lineas rectas pro arcubus ponere; arcubus sub-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L015}{}{}{stituit eorum sinus, et tunc, triangulum ZEL tanquam rectilineum ducens, statuit:}
\par\vspace{-0.94mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L016}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{93.75mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\sin^{2} z = \sin^{2} ZL + \sin^{2} EL = \sin^{2}\left[\text{\textgreek{ζ}} \mp \text{arc}\left(\dfrac{\text{sin \textgreek{β} sin ang. latit.}}{R}\right)\right] + \left(\dfrac{\text{sin \textgreek{β} sin ang. long.}}{R}\right)^{2}.$}}}
\par\vspace{0.66mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0349-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L017}{}{}{\Indent [In resolvendo triangulo sphaerico ELZ rectangulo in L, vera formula quae \textit{z} (vel ZE)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L018}{}{}{praebet est}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L019}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{93.75mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\cos ZE = \cos EL \cos ZL$}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L020}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}unde oritur}\rlap{\hspace*{93.65mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\cos^{2} ZE = \cos^{2} EL \cos^{2} ZL$}}}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L021}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}scilicet}\rlap{\hspace*{93.45mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$1 - \sin^{2} ZE = (1 - \sin^{2} EL)\;(1 - \sin^{2} ZL)$}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L022}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}vel etiam}\rlap{\hspace*{93.97mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\sin^{2} ZE = \sin^{2} EL + \sin^{2} ZL - \sin^{2} EL \sin^{2} ZL.$}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L023}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}\Name{Al–Battānī} contra:}\rlap{\hspace*{95.27mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\sin^{2} ZE = \sin^{2} EL + \sin^{2} ZL\;\cdot$}}}
\par\vspace{1.11mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L024}{}{}{Unde patet eius sin\textsuperscript{2} ZE veritatem excedere quantitate sin\textsuperscript{2} EL sin\textsuperscript{2} ZL. Cum EB $=$ \textgreek{β} summum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L025}{}{}{5° attingat, et angulus EBZ summum 23° 35′, invenitur EL summum esse 2° 0′; qua re ma-}
\par\vspace{-0.93mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L026}{}{}{ximus valor sin\textsuperscript{2} EL est $\dfrac{1}{821}$, radio $=$ 1 sumpto. Apud \Name{Albatenium} igitur error quantitatis}
\par\vspace{-2.20mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L027}{}{}{sin\textsuperscript{2} ZE summum est $\dfrac{1}{821}$ sin\textsuperscript{2} ZL; et argumentatione quam referre nunc opus non est, invenio}
\par\vspace{-2.16mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L028}{}{}{maximum errorem contingentem parallaxi altitudinis ob ZE ita imperfecte supputatum, $\dfrac{1}{1642}$}
\par\vspace{-0.42mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L029}{}{}{ipsius parallaxis non posse excedere, summum ergo 3″ vel 4″, etiam si exaggerata parallaxis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L030}{}{}{\Name{Ptolemaei} adhibeatur cum Luna est in perigeo excentrici. Formula igitur \Name{Albateniana},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L031}{}{}{licet tantum adproximata, optime inservit. Formula rudior \Name{Ptolemaei} quoque sufficit; nam}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L032}{}{}{differentia inter arcus ZE, EL nunquam multum sensibilis est, et alicuius momenti fit tantum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L033}{}{}{cum Luna zenith appropinquet, scilicet cum ZE haud multos gradus complectatur. Tunc e}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L034}{}{}{methodo \Name{Albatenii} et methodo \Name{Ptolemaei} eadem fere quantitas colligitur, cum pro sinibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L035}{}{}{possint arcus poni absque multa variatione in eo quod provenit. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0349-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L036}{}{}{\Indent II. \textit{Correctio angulorum.} — \Name{Ptolemaeus}, l. l., angulum longitudinis correctum putat}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L037}{}{}{ATZ esse, vel AHZ si Luna ab austro eclipticae consistit. \Name{Al–Battānī} similiter, sed falso ut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L038}{}{}{infra dicemus. Ambo postea, perperam triangulis sphaericis tribuentes proprietatem rectilineo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0349-body-L039}{}{}{rum, faciunt}
\DSource{0350}{261}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr -3.34mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\par\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{131.08mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L001}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{20.38mm}ang. long. correctus $= ATZ = ABZ - BZT,$}}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L002}{}{}{et, pro Luna australi,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L003}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{19.93mm}ang. long. correctus $= AHZ = ABZ + DZK.$}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0350-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L004}{}{}{\Indent Hactenus \Name{Ptolemaeus}. \Name{Al-Battānī} supputationem \textit{varia-}}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L005}{}{}{\textit{tionis anguli} i. e. BZT docet hoc modo:}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L006}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{65.83mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\sin BZT = \dfrac{\text{sin \textgreek{β} sin ang. long.}}{R}\,,$}}}
\par\vspace{-3.17mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L007}{}{}{unde}
\par\vspace{-2.41mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L008}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(\textgreek{α})}\rlap{\hspace*{8.10mm}ang. long. correctus $=$ ang. long. $\pm$ arc $\left(\dfrac{\text{sin \textgreek{β} sin ang. long.}}{R}\right)$}}
\par\vspace{-0.42mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L009}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(\textgreek{β})}\rlap{\hspace*{7.16mm}ang. latit. correctus $=$ ang. latit. $\pm$ arc $\left(\dfrac{\text{sin \textgreek{β} sin ang. long.}}{R}\right).$}}
\par\vspace{-1.60mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0350-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L010}{}{}{\Indent Sed calculus quoque « variationis anguli » haud rectus est.}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{54.92mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0350-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0350-F01}\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{-0.95mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L011}{}{}{Nam sin BZT $=$ R $\dfrac{\text{sin EL}}{\text{sin ZE}}$; qua re, ob valores horum sinuum antea repertos:}
\par\vspace{3.35mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L012}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{93.73mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\sin BZT = \dfrac{R\,\dfrac{\text{sin \textgreek{β} cos }ABZ}{R}}{\sin z} = \dfrac{\text{sin \textgreek{β} sin ang. long.}}{\sin z}$ (1).}}}
\par\vspace{-4.04mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0350-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L013}{}{}{\Indent Ad veritatem restituendam in hoc calculo variationis anguli oporteret igitur aliquid in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L014}{}{}{l. 22–23 addere: « postea quod e divisione anguli longitudinis provenerat (2) rursus sume [\textit{et}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L015}{}{}{« \textit{in semidiametrum multiplica; productum per sinum distantiae zenithalis aequatae}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L016}{}{}{« \textit{Lunae divide; quod invenies}] in arcum converte; exibit variatio anguli ». Sed hanc cor-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L017}{}{}{rectionem in animo \Name{al–Battānī} habuisse vehementer dubitandum est, ut p. 262 ostendetur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0350-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L018}{}{}{\Indent Procul dubio ad hunc angulorum correctorum calculum pertinent censurae quas \Name{Ibn}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L019}{}{}{\Name{Yūnus}, introd. (ap. \Name{Caussin} p. 72) infert: « Item falsum est quod Abū ’l–‘Abbās al–Faḍl}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L020}{}{}{« ibn Ḥātim \Name{an–Nayrīzī} (3), Abū ‘Abd Allāh Muḥammad ibn Ǵābir ibn Sinān \Name{al–Battānī}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L021}{}{}{« aliique dicunt de angulis quibus opus est ad cognoscendam parallaxim lunarem in longitu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L022}{}{}{« dine et latitudine, in calculo eclipsium Solis; id enim ad veram cognitionem loci apparentis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L023}{}{}{« Lunae non ducit, cum non adhibeantur methodi quabus ad veritatem perveniatur ». Sed hae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L024}{}{}{censurae (quas \Name{Caussin} perperam ad XLIV cap. al–Battānī pertinere censet) \Name{Ptolemaeum},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L025}{}{}{magis etiam quam \Name{Albatenium} attingunt; et re vera \Name{Tannery}, \textit{Recherches sur l’hist.}}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L026}{}{}{\textit{de l’astron. ancienne}, Paris 1893, p. 221, adn., haud paulum ob has correctiones Graecum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L027}{}{}{astronomum obiurgat.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0350-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L028}{}{}{\Indent [Solutio problematis nullo modo calculum anguli in T requirit, ut \Name{Ptolemaeus} et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L029}{}{}{\Name{al–Battānī} habent; verus \textit{angulus longitudinis}, qui respectu B est ZBQ, respectu Lunae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L030}{}{}{E est ZEQ; hic autem facillime deducitur per proportionem sinuum, triangulo ZEB considerato.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L031}{}{}{— Falsissima, ut antea dictum est, aequatio BZT $+$ ATZ $=$ ABZ, quae tantum constaret si}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L032}{}{}{triangula essent rectilinea. — Demum in calculo anguli ATZ ambo scriptores erraverunt. Parva}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L033}{}{}{tamen mutatione, supputatio \Name{Albateniana} potest satis idonee corrigi.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0350-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L034}{}{}{\Indent Sit AG ecliptica, Z zenith, E Luna, EB circulus latitudinis, ZB et ZT circuli verticales}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L035}{}{}{per B et E ducti. Loco arcus perpendicularis EL ducendi, arcus paralleli describatur, ita ut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L036}{}{}{L, E pariter a Z distent; quoniam EL 2° summum attingit, licet alterum alteri arcui surrogare.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-body-L037}{}{}{— Ex centro O sphaerae ducantur tria plana quae in sphaera tres arcus vel latera trianguli}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{-0.51mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0350-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) [\Name{Ptolemaeus} per \textit{z} dividit, faciens}
\par\vspace{2.54mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0350-H02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\CenterLine{$\sin BZT = \dfrac{\text{\textgreek{β} sin ang. long.}}{z}\,.$}}
\par\vspace{2.74mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-notes_left-L003}{}{}{Sed \textit{z} pro sin \textit{z} ponere semper falsum est; proxime}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-notes_left-L004}{}{}{tantum fieri potest cum Luna paulum a zenith di-}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{-0.59mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-notes_right-L001}{}{}{stet, ita ut sin \textit{z} haud multum a \textit{z} differat. — \textsc{Schia-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\textsc{parelli}.]}
\par\vspace{0.49mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0350-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (2) I. e.\quad $\dfrac{\text{sin \textgreek{β} sin ang. long.}}{R}$.}
\par\vspace{0.72mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0350-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (3) Obiit circa annum 310 heg. (922-23 Chr );}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0350-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Name{Suter} nr. 88, p. 45.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0351}{262}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr -3.51mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\par\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.359\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0351-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0351-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.621\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.379\FullSourceWidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L001}{}{}{sphaerici ZBT determinent; in E et L tangentes \textgreek{ζτ, ζβ} du-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L002}{}{}{cantur, quae manifeste concurrent in puncto \textgreek{ζ} rectae OZ}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L003}{}{}{productae. Per has duas tangentes imaginemur transire pla-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L004}{}{}{num \textgreek{ζτβ}; in quod proiiciamus quadrilaterum sphaericum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L005}{}{}{ELTB, cuius proiectio igitur quadrilaterum rectilineum}
\par\vspace{0.12mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L006}{}{}{EL\textgreek{τβ} erit. Ob amborum quadrilaterorum parvitatem, an-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L007}{}{}{guli et latera alterius ab alterius different quantitate pror-}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L008}{}{}{sus in hoc problemate negligenda, et pro sphaerico quadri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L009}{}{}{laterum planum considerari poterit; tunc, si loco anguli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L010}{}{}{sphaerici in Z utamur angulo plano in \textgreek{ζ}, verae erunt omnes}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L011}{}{}{Ptolemaei et al–Battānī considerationes. Differentia angulo-}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L012}{}{}{rum \textgreek{ζβα, ζτα} eadem circiter erit quae inter ZBA, ZTA:}
\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{-0.19mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L013}{}{}{differentia illa non angulus sphaericus in Z, sed planus in \textgreek{ζ} erit, quo pro Z posito quantitas}
\par\vspace{-0.98mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L014}{}{}{satis bona et veritati proxima colligetur. Angulus in \textgreek{ζ} est R $\dfrac{\text{EL}}{\text{\textgreek{ζ}L}}$; manifeste \textgreek{ζ}L $=$ tg ZL $=$}
\par\vspace{-2.24mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L015}{}{}{tg ZE $=$ tg \textit{z}; ergo \textit{proxime}}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L016}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{93.09mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{ang. \textgreek{ζ} $= R\,\dfrac{EL}{\text{tg}\,z} = \dfrac{\text{sin \textgreek{β} sin ang. long.}}{\text{tg}\,z} = \dfrac{\cos z\ \text{sin \textgreek{β} sin ang. long.}}{R \sin z}\,.$}}}
\par\vspace{-0.48mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L017}{}{}{Qua re regula \Name{Albateniana} fiet vera, si locus p. 82, l. 22–23 ita restituatur: « postea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L018}{}{}{« quod e divisione anguli longitudinis provenerat rursus sume [\textit{et in cosinum distantiae zeni-}}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L019}{}{}{« \textit{thalis aequatae Lunae multiplica; productum per sinum eiusdem distantiae divide;}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L020}{}{}{« \textit{quod invenies}] in arcum converte; exibit variatio anguli ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0351-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L021}{}{}{\Indent Itaque \Name{Ptolemaeus} et \Name{al–Battānī} culpam negligentiae vitavissent si in calculis suis}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L022}{}{}{angulo \textgreek{ζ} (loco anguli Z) et arcus L\textgreek{ζ} tangente (loco arcus LZ) usi essent. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{3.43mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0351-H23}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L023}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{s}}) — \textit{Pag. 82, l. 27-36.}}}
\par\vspace{1.83mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0351-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Tabulae parallaxium lunarium} in climatibus, e tabulis \Name{Theonis} sumptae.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0351-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L025}{}{}{\Indent [\Name{Theon} tabulas supputavit obliquitate eclipticae 23° 51′ sumpta, 16′ igitur maiore quam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L026}{}{}{obliquitate \Name{Albateniana}. Ex hoc parva differentia in calculo parallaxis oriri debet; maxi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L027}{}{}{mam quantitatem attingit in parallaxe latitudinis cum Luna, in Cancro et in termino boreali}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L028}{}{}{exstans, in circulo meridiano culminat. Tunc Luna a zenith urbis ar–Raqqah 7° $\qfrac{1}{2}$ tantum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L029}{}{}{abest; parallaxis (posita parallaxe horizontali 60 minutorum) 7′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$ circiter complectitur; va-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L030}{}{}{riatio 16′ in distantia zenithali tunc variationem 16″ circiter in parallaxe gignit, quae erat}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L031}{}{}{quantitas eo tempore negligenda.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0351-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L032}{}{}{\Indent Recte tamen \Name{al–Battānī} monet tabulas illas, licet commodiores supputatione directa,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L033}{}{}{aliquot parvos errores gignere. Id ob varias causas. Prima diversa obliquitas qua Theon usus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L034}{}{}{est. Secunda interpolationes magno faciendae intervallo 15° graduum anguli horarii, et dimi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L035}{}{}{diae horae in climate datae urbis; talibus interpolationibus in accurata computatione uti non}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L036}{}{}{licet. Tertia demum has tabulas nonnisi cum latitudo Lunae parva sit (ut semper in eclipsi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L037}{}{}{bus Solis evenit) valere. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{3.44mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0351-H38}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L038}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{t}}) — \textit{Pag. 84, l. 3-18.}}}
\par\vspace{1.83mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0351-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L039}{}{}{\Indent [Docet nunc quando parallaxis longitudinis addenda est longitudini, quando demenda.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0351-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-body-L040}{}{}{\Indent Manifeste parallaxis inter ascendens et nonagesimum (1) longitudinem auget, inter nona-}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}{\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{1.04mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0351-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-notes-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Appellatur \textit{nonagesimus} punctum eclipticae quod ab ascendente 90° distat; est punctum eclipti-}
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0351-notes-L002}{}{}{cae omnium altissimum supra horizontem.}
}
\DSource{0352}{263}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L001}{}{}{gesimum et descendens longitudinem minuit. At nonagesimus in meridiano non est nisi quando}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L002}{}{}{initium Cancri et initium Capricorni culminant; aliter ecliptica collocata, nonagesimus ab ortu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L003}{}{}{vel ab occasu meridiani consistit, et, iuxta diversa climata, eius angulus horarius 1 horae vel}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L004}{}{}{magis etiam esse potest. In tabulis parallaxium \Name{Theonis} semper igitur investigare oportet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L005}{}{}{quo loco parallaxes positivae (in longitudine) desinant et negativae incipiant.}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{131.49mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0352-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L006}{}{}{\Indent Sit Z zenith, AD ecliptica, A ascendens, D descendens, N no-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L007}{}{}{nagesimus, M medium Caelum; si Luna inter M et N reperitur,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L008}{}{}{est in horis orientalibus, attamen, ob parallaxim. in occasum de-}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L009}{}{}{clinat et parallaxis longitudinis negativa fit. Res contraria evenire}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L010}{}{}{potest cum N ab occasu sit meridiani. Angulus horarius puncti}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L011}{}{}{N, nempe (quoniam P est polus) angulus ZPN, horam excedit in-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L012}{}{}{terdum in climatibus quae tabulae \Name{Theonis} complectuntur; duas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L013}{}{}{horas attingere potest in climatibus borealioribus.}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0352-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L014}{}{}{\Indent In tabellis Cancri et Capricorni signum parallaxis longitudinis}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{54.51mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0352-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0352-F01}\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{-0.33mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L015}{}{}{mutatur in meridiano. In reliquis tabellis oportet minimum numerum quaerere et numerum e}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L016}{}{}{duobus ei finitimis qui altero minor sit; inter hos punctum incidit in quo parallaxis nulla}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L017}{}{}{habetur. — Ut haec demonstremus, in linea AD horas scribamus, dextrorsum matutinas, si-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L018}{}{}{nistrorsum vespertinas. Lineae verticales I, II, III, tot millimetrorum ducantur quot sunt mi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L019}{}{}{nuta parallaxium longitudinis correspondentium in tabella qualibet \Name{Theonis}; extremitates}
\par\vspace{0.14mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L020}{}{}{harum linearum lineam continuam obliquam efficient. Punctum X, in quo lineam horizontalem}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.461\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0352-body-F02}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0352-F02}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.519\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.481\FullSourceWidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L021}{}{}{secat, respondet tempori quo parallaxis longitudi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L022}{}{}{nis nulla est. Hora cui minima parallaxis respon-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L023}{}{}{det manifeste est I matutina; ex ei finitimis 0, II,}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L024}{}{}{minima parallaxis ad horam 0 pertinet; ergo X}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L025}{}{}{inter I et 0 incidit, non inter I et II ut primo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L026}{}{}{aspectu censeri poterat. — Interdum X inter I}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L027}{}{}{et II reperitur; tunc parallaxis horae I minor est}
\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{2.76mm}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{115.73mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L028}{}{}{parallaxe meridiei, sicut e nova figura apparet; sed regula}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L029}{}{}{investigandi semper eadem est. Interdum X etiam inter II}
\par\vspace{0.15mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L030}{}{}{et III reperitur, ut non raro evenit in tabellis septimi cli-}
\par\vspace{-0.54mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L031}{}{}{matis, t. II, p. 101.}
\par\vspace{0.53mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0352-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L032}{}{}{\Indent « \textit{Quod cum eveniat et horae} ..... ». I. e. cum contin-}
\par\vspace{0.11mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L033}{}{}{gat parallaxim longitudinis nullam fieri inter medium Cae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L034}{}{}{lum et unam e duobus primis finitimis horis; minus obscure}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{70.27mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0352-body-F03}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0352-F03}\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{-0.22mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L035}{}{}{dici poterat: \textit{Quod cum eveniat inter medium Caelum et unius horae distantiam ab ea}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L036}{}{}{\textit{parte in qua minuta longitudinis desinunt,} [\textit{aut inter primam et secundam horam}].}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L037}{}{}{\textit{minuta longitudinis} etc. Hoc praecepto docere vult quod, cum necessaria sit interpolatio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L038}{}{}{inter duas parallaxes longitudinales contrarii signi, tanquam differentia duarum parallaxium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L039}{}{}{non debet sumi \textit{differentia} numerorum qui minuta parallaxium indicant, sed numerorum}
\par\vspace{-0.26mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L040}{}{}{\textit{summa.} Nam manifeste\quad $(+\,a) - (-\,b) = a + b$.}
\par\vspace{0.32mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0352-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L041}{}{}{\Indent l. 30: « \textit{Idque, Deo volente, propius erit veritati si Luna in ecliptica exstiterit} ».}
\par\vspace{-0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L042}{}{}{Recte, nam si Luna extra eclipticam consistit, parallaxes hoc modo repertae possunt errore}
\par\vspace{-0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0352-body-L043}{}{}{etiam 4′ et 5′ affici. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\DSource{0353}{264}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 8.57mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\par\fontsize{15}{17}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0353-H01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L001}{}{}{\CenterLine{A\textsc{d} \textsc{caput} XL.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{4.24mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0353-H02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L002}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{a}}) — \textit{Pag. 84, l. 38-40.}}}
\par\vspace{4.82mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0353-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L003}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Parallaxibus correctis} Lunae longitudinis et latitudinis, e tabulis Theonis sumptis, \textit{quan-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L004}{}{}{\textit{titas addenda}, quae iuxta Platonem $\qfrac{1}{18}$, iuxta cod. $\qfrac{1}{38}$ ipsarum est.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0353-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L005}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Theon}, \textit{Tables manuelles,} t. I, p. 68, ait parallaxibus e tabulis correctionis sumptis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L006}{}{}{addendam esse $\qfrac{1}{20}$ ipsarum, propter parallaxim Solis. Nam tabulas parallaxium lunarium sup-}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L007}{}{}{putavit ac si de parallaxe Lunae iam parallaxim Solis detraxisset. — Si parallaxis Solis 3′ mi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L008}{}{}{nutorum rotunde sumatur (cfr. p. 252 et 258), haec 3′ sunt decima octava pars 54′, quae,}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L009}{}{}{iuxta \Name{Ptolemaeum} et \Name{Albatenium}, parallaxim Lunae in primo termino (53′ 34″, cfr.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L010}{}{}{tabulas t. II, p. 94) conficiunt. Ergo numerus Platonis $\qfrac{1}{18}$ bonus est.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0353-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L011}{}{}{\Indent Ob diversam obliquitatem eclipticae qua \Name{Theon} utitur, differentia sensibilis in parallaxe}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L012}{}{}{non oritur; cfr. p. 262, adn. \textbf{\textit{s}}.}
\par\vspace{4.67mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0353-H13}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L013}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{b}}) — \textit{Pag. 85, l. 1-27.}}}
\par\vspace{5.28mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0353-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L014}{}{}{\Indent I. — \textit{Parallaxim lunarem altitudinis observationibus deprehendere} (l. 1–18).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0353-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L015}{}{}{\Indent Optime \Name{al–Battānī} docet Lunam in nonagesimo (cfr. p. 262, adn.) observare; nam tunc}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L016}{}{}{circulus latitudinis per centrum Lunae et nonagesimum transiens evadit circulus verticalis seu}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L017}{}{}{altitudinis, qua re parallaxis longitudinis in nihilum redigitur, et parallaxis latitudinis est ipsa}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L018}{}{}{parallaxis altitudinis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0353-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L019}{}{}{\Indent Eadem methodo \Name{Tycho Brahe} quoque interdum usus est: « Nunc ternas alias [paral-}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L020}{}{}{« laxes] extra Meridianum observatas adiungam, quando Luna iuxta 90 Eclipticae ab Hori-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L021}{}{}{« zonte gradum erat, tunc nullam differentiam Longitudinis visae et verae insinuans, sed to-}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L022}{}{}{« tam in Latitudinem vertens; collata itaque Latitudine vera facilima [sic] erit Parallaxeos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L023}{}{}{« investigatio » (1).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0353-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Ptolemaeus} V, 13 (ed. Halma t. I, p. 332–38) aliam viam secutus erat, meridianum}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L025}{}{}{loco nonagesimi deligens. Nam XIII die mensis athyr anni 20 Hadriani, vel 882 Nabonassaris,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L026}{}{}{5 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ $+$ $\qfrac{1}{3}$ horis aequinoctialibus post meridiem, Lunam prope tropicum Capricorni (exacte in}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L027}{}{}{3° 10′ Capric.) observaverat, ut circulus latitudinis proxime fieret meridianus (\textgreek{ἔγγιστα ἦν τότε τῷ}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L028}{}{}{\textgreek{μεσημβρινῷ}) et parallaxis altitudinis a parallaxe latitudinis non differret. Cfr. etiam \Name{Delam-}}
\par\vspace{0.16mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L029}{}{}{\Name{bre}, \textit{Astron. ancienne,} II, 209 et 210.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0353-P08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L030}{}{}{\Indent II. — Distantia zenithali et parallaxe cognitis, \textit{distantiam Lunae a centro Terrae} sup-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L031}{}{}{putare, radio Terrae unius partis sumpto (l. 19–27).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0353-P09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L032}{}{}{\Indent Sit \textgreek{ζ} distantia zenithalis apparens Lunae, \textit{p} summa parallaxium Solis et Lunae in circulo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L033}{}{}{verticali; \textit{a} semidiametrus Terrae; \Name{al–Battānī} duas problematis solutiones praebet, quae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-body-L034}{}{}{ob multa in codice et apud Platonem omissa, perperam in unam coaluerunt.}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{4.76mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0353-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) \Name{Tychonis Brahe Dani}, \textit{Astronomiae}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\textit{instauratae progymnasmata}, Uraniburgi et Pra-}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-notes_left-L003}{}{}{gae 1610, t. I, p. 461. Cfr. quoque \Name{Riccioli}, \textit{Alma-}}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\textit{gestum novum}, Bononiae 1651, t. I, p. 221. — In aliis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-notes_left-L005}{}{}{problematibus iam \Name{Hipparchus} Lunam in nona-}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{4.76mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-notes_right-L001}{}{}{gesimo consulto observaverat, ut parallaxim lon-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-notes_right-L002}{}{}{gitudinis vitaret; cfr. \textit{Almag.} V, 3 (ed. Halma t. I,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-notes_right-L003}{}{}{293-94, 295), et V, 5 (ed. Halma I, 304); \Name{Delambre},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\textit{Astr. anc.}, II, 188, 189, 194; et \Name{Pappi} considera-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0353-notes_right-L005}{}{}{tiones apud \Name{Delambre}, \textit{Astr. anc.}, II, 579.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0354}{265}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr -3.43mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\par\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{126.26mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0354-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent I. Sit E centrum Terrae, F locus observationis, Z zenith,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L002}{}{}{L Luna; erunt ang. ZFL $=$ \textgreek{ζ}, ang. FLE $=$ \textit{p}, EL $=$ distantia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L003}{}{}{geocentrica quaesita. Producatur FL usque quo in Q cum re-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L004}{}{}{cta EQ angulum rectum efficiat. Manifeste:}
\par\vspace{0.15mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L005}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{37.61mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\sin FLE = R\,\dfrac{EQ}{EL}\,,$}}\rlap{\hspace*{88.48mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\sin QFE = R\,\dfrac{EQ}{EF}\,,$}}}
\par\vspace{0.85mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L006}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}id est}\rlap{\hspace*{40.92mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\sin p = R\,\dfrac{EQ}{\text{dist.}}\,,$}}\rlap{\hspace*{85.45mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\sin \text{\textgreek{ζ}} = R\,\dfrac{EQ}{a}\,,$}}}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{59.74mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0354-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0354-F01}\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{0.69mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L007}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}unde}\rlap{\hspace*{91.61mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{distantia geocentr. $\times \sin p = a \sin \text{\textgreek{ζ}}$.}}}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L008}{}{}{Et quoniam \textit{a} $=$ 1 sumitur, statim prior formula \Name{al–Battānī} oritur:}
\par\vspace{0.26mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L009}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(\textgreek{α})}\rlap{\hspace*{94.35mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{distantia geocentrica $= \dfrac{\sin \text{\textgreek{ζ}}}{\sin p}\,.$}}}
\par\vspace{2.09mm}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.374\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0354-body-F02}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0354-F02}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.606\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.394\FullSourceWidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0354-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L010}{}{}{\Indent II. [Altera regula, cuius maxima pars desideratur,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L011}{}{}{procul dubio haec erat: Sit \textit{z} distantia zenithalis \textit{vera},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L012}{}{}{nempe ZEL; perpendicularis FQ ducatur; eodem quo}
\par\vspace{-0.90mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L013}{}{}{antea pro EL modo facillime invenitur FL $= a\,\dfrac{\sin z}{\sin p}$. Ob}
\par\vspace{-1.23mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L014}{}{}{parvitatem anguli FLE, sine errore sensibili potest QL $=$}
\par\vspace{-1.00mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L015}{}{}{FL $= a\,\dfrac{\sin z}{\sin p}$ statui; est autem EQ $= \dfrac{a \cos z}{R}$; ergo}
\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{-0.47mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L016}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{94.06mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{distantia geocentrica $= EL = QL + EQ = a\,\dfrac{\sin z}{\sin p} + a\,\dfrac{\cos z}{R}\,.$}}}
\par\vspace{-0.24mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L017}{}{}{Si \textit{a} $=$ 1 sumatur, et in secundo termino altitudo vera \textit{h} pro eius complemento \textit{z}, oritur}
\par\vspace{-0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L018}{}{}{altera formula \Name{al–Battānī}:}
\par\vspace{-0.93mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L019}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(\textgreek{β})}\rlap{\hspace*{94.46mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{distantia geocentrica $= \dfrac{\sin z}{\sin p} + \dfrac{\sin h}{R}\,.$}}}
\par\vspace{-0.16mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0354-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L020}{}{}{\Indent Pag. 85, l. 23–24, verba « \textit{in semidiametrum multiplica, et productum} » delenda sunt.}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L021}{}{}{— \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{4.46mm}
\par\fontsize{15}{17}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0354-H22}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L022}{}{}{\CenterLine{A\textsc{d} \textsc{caput} XLI.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.73mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0354-H23}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L023}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{a}}) — \textit{Pag. 85, l. 37–39.}}}
\par\vspace{4.48mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0354-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Novilunii cognitio} maximi est momenti apud nationes Muslimicas, non solum ob chro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L025}{}{}{nologiam Arabicam, sed etiam ob praecepta religionis de ieiunio mensis ramaḍān. Incipit enim}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L026}{}{}{cum Luna nova mensis ramaḍān apparet; cessat cum Luna nova mensis shawwāl cernitur.}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L027}{}{}{Oportet igitur ut Muslimi novam Lunam explorent ultima die (vicesima nona) mensis sha‘bān (1),}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L028}{}{}{et, si viderint illam, ieiunium incipiant. Prima dies ramaḍān « dies dubitationis » (\textit{yawm}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L029}{}{}{\textit{ash–shakk}) vocatur; in ea enim fieri potest ut, licet aer serenus sit, Luna nova maneat invi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L030}{}{}{sibilis, qua re incertum utrum debeant homines ieiunare an non. Hoc die elapso ieiunium}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L031}{}{}{semper est incipiendum, etiamsi ob aeris condiciones novilunium haud possit cerni.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0354-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L032}{}{}{\Indent Eo « dubitationis die », si in aere aliquod impedimentum sit quo Luna apparere nequeat,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L033}{}{}{ieiunium incipi debet cum iudex religiosus (\textit{qāḍī}) urbis susceperit testimonium unius hominis}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L034}{}{}{veridici, qui asserat se novam Lunam vidisse, sive vir est, sive mulier, sive liber, sive ser-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-body-L035}{}{}{vus (2). Si autem in aere nullum sit impedimentum, nullius recipitur testimonium, donec vide-}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.59mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0354-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Qui ramaḍān praecedit. Non obliviscendum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-notes_left-L002}{}{}{apud Muslimos diem civilem ab occasu Solis incipere.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.59mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0354-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (2) Si de novilunio in fine ramaḍān et initio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0354-notes_right-L002}{}{}{shawwāl agitur, necessarium est testimonium duo-}
\end{minipage}
\par\vspace{5mm}\hfill{\fontsize{8}{10}\selectfont 34}\hspace{10mm}
\DSource{0355}{266}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 0.11mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L001}{}{}{rint novam Lunam multi homines e quorum testimonio res certissime constet (1). — Nihili}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L002}{}{}{faciunt supputationes astronomicas novilunii, praeter paucos quosdam theologos et iuriscon-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L003}{}{}{sultos scholae shāfi‘iticae, e quibus \Name{Ibn Surayǵ} (m. 306 heg., 918 Chr.).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0355-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L004}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Thābit ibn Qurrah}, m. 288 heg., 901 Chr., composuit epistulam « de supputanda}
\par\vspace{0.13mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L005}{}{}{« apparitione Lunae novae » (2), cuius exemplar in Musaeo Britannico asservatur; eius me-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L006}{}{}{thodus exponetur in fine adn. \textbf{\textit{c}}. \Name{Al–Bīrūnī}, \textit{Chron.} p. 196 text. $=$ 176–77 vers., haec scri-}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L007}{}{}{bit: « Calculus apparitionis Lunae novae est longissima et difficillima quaestio, cuius expositio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L008}{}{}{« longos calculos multasque tabulas requirit. De hoc sufficiunt quae in opere astronomico Mu-}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L009}{}{}{« ḥammad ibn Ǵābir \Name{al–Battānī}, aut in aliquo operum \Name{Ḥabash} calculatoris (3) scripta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L010}{}{}{« leguntur ».}
\par\vspace{0.50mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0355-H11}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L011}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{b}}) — \textit{Pag. 86, l. 2-8.}}}
\par\vspace{4.52mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0355-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L012}{}{}{\Indent Qui hoc modo errabant probabiliter erant scriptores iuris (nam apud Muslimos ritus etiam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L013}{}{}{religionis, ut orandi, ieiunii agendi, peregrinandi, sunt pars iuris), aut viri qui, rudimentis ali-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L014}{}{}{quot astronomicis imbuti, auxilio paucorum instrumentorum quotidie in sacris aedibus horas}
\par\vspace{0.13mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L015}{}{}{canonicarum orationum statuunt pro diversa dierum longitudine in ea urbe, et solariis aliis-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L016}{}{}{que horologiis templi inspiciendis praepositi sunt. Appellantur \textit{muwaqqit} « tempora determi-}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L017}{}{}{nantes »; eorum scientia (\textit{‘ilm al–mīqāt} « scientia temporum definiendorum ») complectitur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L018}{}{}{supputationem horarum temporalium et aequinoctialium, usum astrolabii et constructionem}
\par\vspace{0.12mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L019}{}{}{quadrantum atque solariorum.}
\par\vspace{5.28mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0355-H20}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L020}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{c}}) — \textit{Pag. 86, l. 17—pag. 87, l. ult.}}}
\par\vspace{4.52mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0355-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L021}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Albatenii} theoria \textit{apparitionum vespertinarum Lunae.}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0355-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L022}{}{}{\Indent [Praeter aerem limpidum, tres condiciones auctor statuit quibus praecipue possit falx}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L023}{}{}{Lunae cerni tempore occasus post novilunium.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0355-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent I. \textit{Distantia angularis Lunae a Sole,} quae metitur magnitudinem falcis visibilis. Sit T}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L025}{}{}{observator in superficie Terrae, TS directio Solis, L Luna, LS recta quae Lunam Soli con-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L026}{}{}{iungit. Ob maximam Solis distantiam possunt TS, LS rectae parallelae censeri. Hemisphaerium}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L027}{}{}{Lunae illuminatum erit CED, quod determinatur plano CD perpendiculari ad TS et LS; hemi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L028}{}{}{sphaerium quod potest a spectatore conspici est AFB, determinatum plano AB perpendiculari}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-body-L029}{}{}{ad TL; pars Lunae visibilis ad imaginem falcis erit fusus CLA, cuius amplitudo in gradibus}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.64mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_left-L001}{}{}{rum virorum veridicorum. — Muslimi qui iuris doc-}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_left-L002}{}{}{trinam ḥanafiticam, mālikitam et ḥanbaliticam profi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_left-L003}{}{}{tentur, cum quadam urbe die dubitationis testimo-}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_left-L004}{}{}{nium unius viri iudex receperit putant « ubique ter-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_left-L005}{}{}{rarum » (\ArabicRun{على جميع الدنيا}) ieiunium esse agendum; quod}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_left-L006}{}{}{tantum hodie in imperio Turcarum, telegraphi gra}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_left-L007}{}{}{tia, re vera fit. Qui contra doctrinam shāfi‘iticam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_left-L008}{}{}{sequuntur, ieiunium tunc nonnisi in locis haud nimis}
\par\vspace{0.12mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_left-L009}{}{}{longinquis agendum existimant; celeberrimus \Name{al-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_left-L010}{}{}{\Name{Ghazālī}, \textit{Iḥyā’ ‘ulūm ad-dīn}, Kahirae 1302, t I,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_left-L011}{}{}{p. 209, maximam distantiam statuit esse bidui iter}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_left-L012}{}{}{(\ArabicRun{مرحلتان}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0355-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_left-L013}{}{}{\Indent (1) Quanta possit esse exspectatio novilunii et}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_left-L014}{}{}{quam incerta res apud Muslimos, satis apparet ex}
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_left-L015}{}{}{\Name{al-Ǵabartī}, \textit{‘Aǵā’ib al-āthār fī ’t-tarāǵim wa}}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.60mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\textit{’l-akhbār}, Kahirae 1301-2 heg. (in marg. Annalium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_right-L002}{}{}{Ibn al-Athīr), t. VIII, p. 171 (ultimo die ramaḍān}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_right-L003}{}{}{1217 heg., in quo aer nubilus in Aegypto erat), et}
\par\vspace{0.20mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_right-L004}{}{}{t. XII, p. 216 (ineunte mense shawwāl anni 1235 heg.,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_right-L005}{}{}{in quo Lunae visus perdifficilis et magnae de appa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_right-L006}{}{}{ritione disputationes). — \Name{Ibn al-Fāriḍ} poeta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_right-L007}{}{}{(m. 632 heg., 1235 Chr.) de viro quem nimius exte-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_right-L008}{}{}{nuaverat amor ait:}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_right-L009}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{64.61mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{\ArabicRun{كهلال الشكّ لولا انه}}}\rlap{\hspace*{25.26mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{\ArabicRun{أنّ عيني عينَهُ لم تتأيّ}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_right-L010}{}{}{« [extenuatus est] ut Luna nova dubitationis; nisi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_right-L011}{}{}{« ingemuisset, oculus meus corpus eius cernere non}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_right-L012}{}{}{« potuisset » (\textit{Dīwān}, Kahirae 1310 heg., t. I, p. 19).}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0355-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_right-L013}{}{}{\Indent (2) Ita titulus corrigatur apud \Name{Suter}, \textit{Die}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_right-L014}{}{}{\textit{Mathematiker}, p. 35, l. 1, et p. 36.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0355-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0355-notes_right-L015}{}{}{\Indent (3) \Name{Ḥabash}, cod. Berol., f. 150,v.–151,v.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0356}{267}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr -3.26mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\par\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{148.25mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L001}{}{}{est angulus CLA $=$ STL, nempe angulus quem centra Solis et Lunae in}
