\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L002}{}{}{oculo observatoris T efficiunt. — \Name{Al–Battānī} ait, datis mediis condi-}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L003}{}{}{cionibus in quibus hae observationes fiant, primo falcem lunarem visi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L004}{}{}{bilem esse cum angulus STL 12 sit graduum. Addit hos 12° perpaulo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L005}{}{}{differre a motu medio quo Luna in die elongetur a Sole, nempe 12° 11′;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L006}{}{}{id ergo circiter confirmare sententiam eorum qui dicant Lunam nonnisi}
\par\vspace{0.11mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L007}{}{}{24\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} fere post veram coniunctionem conspici posse. Tanquam calculi fun-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L008}{}{}{damentum 12° 11′ deligit.}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0356-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L009}{}{}{\Indent II. \textit{Lumen imi Caeli eo loco quo Luna tempore occasus repe-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L010}{}{}{\textit{ritur,} quod autem determinatur profunditate Solis sub horizonte eo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L011}{}{}{temporis momento. Luna et Sole in aequatore positis (quod plerumque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L012}{}{}{non evenit, sed est media rerum condicio), ex observatione nuper ex-}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{37.75mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0356-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0356-F01}\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{-0.29mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L013}{}{}{posita apparet eam profunditatem sufficere quotiescumque ab occasu Solis ad occasum Lunae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L014}{}{}{tot minuta elapsa sint quot respondeant arcui 12° interpositi; ergo $\qfrac{4}{5}$ horae. His $\qfrac{4}{5}$ horae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L015}{}{}{Luna a Sole $\qfrac{2}{5}$ gradus seu 24′ recedit; itaque si, occidente Luna, elongatio duorum lumi-}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L016}{}{}{narium 12° 11′ fuerit, occidente Sole erit 24′ minor, nempe 11° 47′. \Name{Al–Battānī} rotunde}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L017}{}{}{11° 45′ scribit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0356-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L018}{}{}{\Indent Sed quando linea duo luminaria coniungens non est ad aequatorem parallela, arcus 12° 11′}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L019}{}{}{nequit esse limes apparitionis. In ipso climate urbis ar-Raqqah, linea illa potest esse fere ad}
\par\vspace{0.02mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L020}{}{}{horizontem parallela, ob eclipticae obliquitatem et latitudinem Lunae; interdum quoque per}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L021}{}{}{pendicularis fit ad horizontem. Tunc magnitudo illa non est quantitas profunditatis Solis sub}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L022}{}{}{horizonte, et luminis Caeli. — Itaque \Name{al–Battānī} tanquam regulam generalem statuit $\qfrac{4}{5}$}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L023}{}{}{horae esse arcum paralleli solaris qui horizontem transgressus sit inter occasum Solis occa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L024}{}{}{sumque Lunae; ponit postea, tanquam regulam generalem, inter ambos occasus, datis mediis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L025}{}{}{condicionibus, praeteriisse 12° aequatoris vel $\qfrac{4}{5}$ horae, quod, ut dixi, respondet differentiae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L026}{}{}{11° 45′ tempore occasus Solis. Tunc Solis profunditas sub horizonte fere constans erit; in cli-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L027}{}{}{mate ar–Raqqah, iuxta diversa anni tempora, inter 10° et 8° circiter variare poterit.}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0356-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L028}{}{}{\Indent III. \textit{Longinquitas Lunae a Terra.} \Name{Al–Battānī} apparitionem Lunae ponit etiam ab}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L029}{}{}{apparenti latitudine falcis luminosae pendere; quae, aequalibus datis condicionibus, est propor-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L030}{}{}{tionalis ad parallaxim horizontalem Lunae, et easdem variationis leges sequitur. In his varia-}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L031}{}{}{tionibus supputandis considerat mutationes distantiae Lunae iuxta eius situs in epicyclo; et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L032}{}{}{ponit medium statum rerum haberi cum Luna sit in distantia media, scilicet a Terra tanto}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L033}{}{}{distet quanto epicycli centrum, et sexagesimae partes correctionis anomaliae propter paralla-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L034}{}{}{xim sint 30′ in tertia columna (1) tabulae t. II, p. 89. Ponit quoque quantitates constantes 12°}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L035}{}{}{et $\qfrac{4}{5}$ horae (de quibus supra dixi) respondere mediae distantiae lunari.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0356-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L036}{}{}{\Indent His positis, \Name{al–Battānī} hoc modo ratiocinatur. Condicio qua Luna visibilis est provenit}
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L037}{}{}{a tribus factoribus variabilibus, quorum alter crescendo potest deminutionem alterius compen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L038}{}{}{sare. Ut exemplum praebeam, si Lunae elongatio a Sole maior fuerit, falx erit proportionaliter}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L039}{}{}{amplior; qua re ad Lunam visibilem efficiendam minor Solis sub horizonte profunditas neces-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L040}{}{}{saria erit, et ea proportione poterit \textit{arcus apparitionis} fundamentalis (11° 45′) minui. Si Luna}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L041}{}{}{vicinior fuerit Terrae quam distantia media, magnitudo apparens proportionaliter crescet, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-body-L042}{}{}{minor profunditas Solis erit ad apparitionem Lunae necessaria. Si demum elongatio et propin-}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}{\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{7.40mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0356-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-notes-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Ita \Name{al-Battānī} p. 87, nam binas numerorum series pro duabus columnis supputat. Alioquin}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0356-notes-L002}{}{}{II; inscribitur: « Portiones longinquitatum Lunae ».}
}
\DSource{0357}{268}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 0.08mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L001}{}{}{quitas simul maiores fuerint, correctiones inferendae \textit{arcui apparitionis} medio in unum col-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L002}{}{}{ligentur. — Hinc \Name{al–Battānī} regulas suas deducit, quae ita exponi possunt:}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0357-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L003}{}{}{\Indent I. Die data, tempore occasus Solis deducantur e tabulis situs Solis et Lunae (in hac in-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L004}{}{}{cludenda est parallaxis), et quaeratur quanto temporis intervallo occasus situs Lunae ita sup-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L005}{}{}{putati sequatur occasum Solis. Sit A hoc intervallum, gradibus expressum. — Manifeste si}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L006}{}{}{tunc Luna falcem regularem haberet, quae respondet 12° 11′ elongationis, et mediam distan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L007}{}{}{tiam a Terra, problema solveretur comparando A cum fundamentali apparitionis arcu 11° 45′.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L008}{}{}{Luna erit eo vespere visibilis si A maior 11° 45′ fuerit.}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0357-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L009}{}{}{\Indent II. Si tunc temporis elongatio Lunae a Sole differat a 12° 11′, elongatio (parallaxi inclusa)}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L010}{}{}{supputetur qualis apparet observatori in superficie Terrae (1); eius ratio ad elongationem fun-}
\par\vspace{0.01mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L011}{}{}{damentalem 12° 11′ supputetur. Si ratio 1 superat, arcus fundamentalis apparitionis 11° 45′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L012}{}{}{proportionaliter minuatur; si contra ratio minor 1, arcus proportione inversa augeatur. Exibit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L013}{}{}{\textit{arcus apparitionis} reductus ad elongationem fundamentalem 12° 11′. Nam si vera elongatio}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L014}{}{}{maior est fundamentali, falx Lunae proportionaliter latior debet esse, Luna igitur visibilis sub}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L015}{}{}{arcu apparitionis minore. Hic arcus B ita reductus ille est qui cum numero A comparari de-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L016}{}{}{beret si Luna mediam distantiam a Terra occuparet.}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0357-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L017}{}{}{\Indent III. At si Luna mediam distantiam non tenet, auxilio anomaliae aequatae lunaris intran-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L018}{}{}{dum est in tertiam (2) columnam tabulae t. II, p. 89, quae ad analogam reductionem parallaxis}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L019}{}{}{inservit; parallaxis horizontalis et amplitudo apparens falcis lunaris eadem decrescunt propor-}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L020}{}{}{tione qua distantia. Falx media respondet numero 30 columnae III, minima numero 0, ma-}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L021}{}{}{xima numero 60. Variatio 30 partium efficit $\qfrac{1}{12}$ longitudinis apparentis ipsius falcis; qua re,}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L022}{}{}{si \textit{n} vocetur numerus tertiae columnae respondens anomaliae datae, cum fuerit $n < 30$ falx mi-}
\par\vspace{-0.93mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L023}{}{}{nuetur iuxta rationem 1 ad $1 - \dfrac{1}{12}\cdot\dfrac{30 - n}{30}$; et cum $n > 30$, falx augebitur iuxta rationem}
\par\vspace{-2.22mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L024}{}{}{1 ad $1 + \dfrac{1}{12}\cdot\dfrac{n - 30}{30}$. Si falx est augenda (nempe si \textit{n} maior 30), eadem ratione minuatur}
\par\vspace{-0.38mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L025}{}{}{arcus apparitionis B antea supputatus; si falx est minuenda ($n < 30$), ratione inversa augeatur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L026}{}{}{arcus B. Proveniet quantitas C, quae erit \textit{arcus} correctus propter ambas variationes, elonga-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L027}{}{}{tionis scilicet et distantiae a Terra. Cum C comparandus postremo erit numerus A; si hic}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L028}{}{}{maior fuerit, Luna visibilis erit in caelo sereno et puro; si A minor, diem posterum exspe-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L029}{}{}{ctare oportebit.}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0357-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L030}{}{}{\Indent Tota haec theoria ingeniosissime et elegantissime condita est. Proportiones equidem quibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L031}{}{}{nititur haud ita simplices fortasse sunt ut auctor ponit; exempli gratia non est certum dupli-}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L032}{}{}{catione amplitudinis falcis arcum apparitionis fieri dimidium absque eo quod visibilitas Lunae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L033}{}{}{mutetur. Tamen hodie etiam difficillimum est melius facere; et multis observationibus opus fo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L034}{}{}{ret ad veras rationes, quibus necesse est uti, bene determinandas. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0357-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L035}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Al–Farghānī} cap. XXV, ed. Golii p. 93–96, ait Lunam novam post coniunctionem con-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L036}{}{}{spici cum 12° circiter aequatoris a Sole iam distet, elapso nempe nychthemero fere a coniun-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L037}{}{}{ctione (3). Similiter \Name{al–Ǵaghmīnī} p. 256–57. — \Name{Ḥabash}, f. 150,v.–151,v., supputationes}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-body-L038}{}{}{ab ovo incipit, nempe a correctione mediorum motuum Solis et Lunae, et « tabulam paral-}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.01mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0357-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Quod satis proxime fit ponendo hanc elonga-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-notes_left-L002}{}{}{tionem quasi hypotenusam trianguli rectanguli, cu-}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-notes_left-L003}{}{}{ius catheti sint latitudo Lunae et eius differentia lon-}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-notes_left-L004}{}{}{gitudinis a Sole. Eos qui calculum exactiorem cu-}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-notes_left-L005}{}{}{piunt \Name{al-Battānī} iubet (p. 87, l. 11-13) aliis prae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-notes_left-L006}{}{}{ceptis uti.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0357-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-notes_left-L007}{}{}{\Indent (2) Aut « secundam »; cfr. p. 267, adn. 1.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.03mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0357-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (3) Haec iam ante Arabum aetatem \Name{Indi} co-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-notes_right-L002}{}{}{gnoscebant; cfr. \textit{Sūrya-Siddhānta}, X, 1 (vers. \Name{Bur-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Name{gess-Whitney} p. 236), et \textit{Pañcasiddhāntikā}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-notes_right-L004}{}{}{V, 1-3, pag. 38 versionis (titulum indicavi p. 206,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-notes_right-L005}{}{}{adn. 2). In hoc libro dicitur distantia « correcta » So-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-notes_right-L006}{}{}{lis et Lunae (i. e. distantia in aequatore) 2 \textit{nāḍikā}}
\par\vspace{-0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0357-notes_right-L007}{}{}{complecti debere; ergo 48\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} $=$ 12°.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0358}{269}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L001}{}{}{« laxis lunaris propter visionem Lunae novae » praebet; sed eius methodus ab \Name{Álbateniana}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L002}{}{}{differt et multo ea imperfectior videtur. Statuit Lunam novam conspici posse cum arcus de-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L003}{}{}{pressionis Solis sub horizonte sit 10° aut amplius; nunquam apparere cum hic arcus sit 9° aut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L004}{}{}{paulo minus. Tunc quantitatem luminis Lunae hoc modo inveniri:}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L005}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{93.53mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{sin luminis \MoonSym{} $= R\,\dfrac{\text{sin differ. longit. }(\MoonSym{} - \SunSym{})}{\sqrt{\text{sin}^{2}\ \text{latit. }\MoonSym{} + \text{sin}^{2}\ \text{differ. longit. }(\MoonSym{} - \SunSym{})}}\,.$}}}
\par\vspace{-0.33mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0358-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L006}{}{}{\Indent Iuvat nunc multa referre ex iis quae \Name{al–Khāzinī}, \textit{az–zīǵ as–sanǵarī}, cod. Vatic.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L007}{}{}{fol. 87,r.–90,v. (tractat. IX, pars I) narrat: « \textit{Caput II:} de iis qui [in calculo novilunii] ni-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L008}{}{}{« tuntur arcubus simplicibus, inter se non compositis. — Arcus, cum aliis non compositis, qui}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L009}{}{}{« adhibentur in supputanda visione Lunae novae quatuor sunt: \textit{arcus lucis, arcus morae,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L010}{}{}{« \textit{arcus depressionis, arcus altitudinis} (1). — Dicunt multi e \Name{gente libri} (2): [Cum fue-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L011}{}{}{« rit] inter 10 et 12, et amplius, Luna erit intra limites visionis et poterit cerni. — Dicunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L012}{}{}{« \Name{Indi}, \Name{Sinenses} et \Name{Muḥammad ibn Mūsà al–Khuwārizmī}: Si arcus morae fuerit}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L013}{}{}{« 12 et amplius, Luna nova conspicietur; secus non. — Dicit Aḥmad ibn ‘Abd Allāh \Name{Ḥabash}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L014}{}{}{« calculator: Cum fuerit Solis depressio, tempore occasus Lunae, 10° et amplius, conspicietur;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L015}{}{}{« secus non. Et secundum eundem auctorem narrat \Name{as–Sa‘īdī} (3) quod si arcus fuerit 8° $\qfrac{2}{3}$}
\par\vspace{-0.25mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L016}{}{}{« et amplius conspicietur; si minus, non. — Dicit \Name{Kūshiyār} (4): Quod verisimile duco (\ArabicRun{المعتبر}}
\par\vspace{0.34mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L017}{}{}{« \ArabicRun{عندي}) est arcum lucis 10°, arcum morae 8°, arcum altitudinis apparentis 6°, arcum depressio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L018}{}{}{« nis 8° usque ad 7° $\qfrac{1}{2}$ complecti. Si hi arcus ita fuerint aut maiores, Luna nova erit visibilis;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L019}{}{}{« si minores, non. Dicit quoque: Si summa arcuum lucis et depressionis fuerit 18° vel amplius,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L020}{}{}{« Luna nova conspicietur ». — In III capite \Name{al–Khāzinī} docet quo modo arcus illi depre-}
\par\vspace{0.11mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L021}{}{}{hendantur (f. 87,v.–88,r.); postea haec habet (f. 88,r.–89,r.): « \textit{Caput IV:} de principiis quibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L022}{}{}{« \Name{al-Battānī} nititur (\ArabicRun{في ذكر ما اعتمد عليه البتاني}). — Inter calculos [variis arcubus] compositos}
\par\vspace{-0.28mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L023}{}{}{« (\ArabicRun{ومن المركبات}) est quod \Name{al-Battānī} memorat, qui arcu morae nititur eumque corrigit (\ArabicRun{وعدله})}
\par\vspace{-0.37mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L024}{}{}{« duplici modo per arcum lucis. — \textit{Prior methodus.} Dicit: Nocte trigesimi diei (5) mensis Ara-}
\par\vspace{-0.39mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L025}{}{}{« bici verum locum Solis et Lunae determinemus (\ArabicRun{قوَمنا النيرين}) tempore occasus Solis; locum Lu-}
\par\vspace{0.41mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L026}{}{}{« nae in longitudine et latitudine corrigamus (\ArabicRun{عدلنا}), et deprehendamus arcum lucis et arcum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L027}{}{}{« morae tunc exstantes. Arcum lucis observemus; si 12° $\qfrac{1}{6}$ fuerit, arcus medius apparitionis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L028}{}{}{« qui 11° 45′ complectitur, iam erit aequatus. Si arcus lucis maior aut minor fuerit 12° 10′, ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L029}{}{}{« eum debebimus arcum medium apparitionis, seu 11° 45′, corrigere. Aequatio haec est: differen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L030}{}{}{« tiam sumamus inter arcum lucis repertum et 12° 10′, et cum hac differentia intrando in pri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L031}{}{}{« mam tabulam aequationis visionis ab \Name{al–Battānī} confectam, gradus et minuta ei respondentia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L032}{}{}{« accipiamus, quae servabimus quia nondum erunt aequata. Postea in eandem tabulam cum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L033}{}{}{« anomalia Lunae [aequata] etiam intrando, sumamus minuta proportionalia (\ArabicRun{دقائق النسب}), quae}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-body-L034}{}{}{« erunt crescentia aut decrescentia prout differentia antea sumpta fuerit super 12° 10′ cre-}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}

\par\vspace{4.77mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0358-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) \ArabicRun{الباب الثاني في ذكر من اعتمد على القسي المفردة}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{البسيطة. القسي المفردة المستعملة في عمل معرفة رؤية الهلال اربع}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\smash{\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\textarabic{احداها قوس النور والثانية قوس المكث والثالثة قوس الانحطاط}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\ArabicRun{والرابعة قوس الارتفاع}. — Ex f. 87,v.-88,r. patet arcum}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-notes_left-L005}{}{}{morae esse moram Lunae post occasum Solis, ar-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-notes_left-L006}{}{}{cum depressionis depressionem Solis sub horizonte}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-notes_left-L007}{}{}{tempore occasus Lunae, arcum altitudinis altitudi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-notes_left-L008}{}{}{nem Lunae tempore quo Sol occidit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0358-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-notes_left-L009}{}{}{\Indent (2) \ArabicRun{قالت طائفة من اهل الكتاب}. \textit{Gentem libri} ap-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-notes_left-L010}{}{}{pellant Muslimi Christianos, Iudaeos et Ṣābios (non}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-notes_left-L011}{}{}{Sabaeos), qui a Deo revelationem scriptam accepe-}
\end{minipage}

\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{0.28mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-notes_right-L001}{}{}{runt. — Hoc loco probabiliter Iudaei intelliguntur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-notes_right-L002}{}{}{quorum calendarium est lunisolare.}
\par\vspace{1.45mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0358-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (3) Cod. \ArabicRun{السعدى}. Fortasse ut \Name{Suter} mihi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-notes_right-L004}{}{}{litteris proposuit, est Muḥammad ibn Aḥmad al-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-notes_right-L005}{}{}{–Ḥāzimī as-Sa‘īdī, ignotae hucusque aetatis, qui Al-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-notes_right-L006}{}{}{magestum in epitomem redegit; cfr. \Name{Suter}, \textit{Die}}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-notes_right-L007}{}{}{\textit{Mathem. u. Astronomen der Araber}, p. 202, nr. 520.}
\par\vspace{1.48mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0358-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-notes_right-L008}{}{}{\Indent (4) Floruit ineunte XI Chr. saec.; \Name{Suter}, p 83–}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-notes_right-L009}{}{}{-84, nr. 192.}
\par\vspace{1.50mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0358-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-notes_right-L010}{}{}{\Indent (5) Iuxta usum civilem Muslimorum dies ab}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0358-notes_right-L011}{}{}{occasu Solis incipit; nox igitur diem praecedit.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0359}{270}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L001}{}{}{« scens aut ab his decrescens. Minuta proportionalia reperta multiplicemus in [gradus et mi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L002}{}{}{« nuta] servata; productum per 60 dividamus; exibit \textit{aequatio,} quam addamus arcui medio}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L003}{}{}{« apparitionis (scilicet 11° 45′) si differentia erat decrescens, aut de arcu 11° 45′ demamus si}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L004}{}{}{« differentia crescens erat. Proveniet \textit{arcus apparitionis correctus} (\ArabicRun{قوس الرؤية المصححة}). Si arcus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L005}{}{}{« morae fuerit huic arcui apparitionis correcto aequalis aut maior, Luna nova apparebit; si}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L006}{}{}{« minor non. — \textit{Altera eius} [Albatenii] \textit{methodus.} Dicit: Locum verum Solis et Lunae nocte}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L007}{}{}{« trigesimi diei mensis Arabici determinemus, atque ambos arcus lucis et morae tunc exstan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L008}{}{}{« tes. Si arcus lucis 13° 40′ fuerit, arcus medius apparitionis, qui 10° 50′ complectitur, iam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L009}{}{}{« erit aequatus. At si arcus lucis maior aut minor fuerit quam 13° 40′, ad eum debebimus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L010}{}{}{« arcum apparitionis corrigere. Correctio haec est: Differentiam inter arcum lucis inventum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L011}{}{}{« et 13° 40′ sumamus; cum ea in secundam tabulam \Name{Albatenianam} aequationis visionis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L012}{}{}{« intrando, gradus et minuta ei respondentia accipiamus et servemus. Postea cum anomalia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L013}{}{}{« aequata [Lunae] in eandem secundam tabulam intrando, sumamus minuta proportionalia ei}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L014}{}{}{« respondentia; erunt crescentia aut decrescentia prout ad arcum lucis inventum aut ad ar-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L015}{}{}{« cum 13° 40′ differentia pertinebat. Ea in [gradus minutaque] servata multiplicemus; produ-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L016}{}{}{« ctum per 60 dividamus; prodibit \textit{aequatio generalis} (\ArabicRun{تعديل مطلق}), quae erit in gradus ascen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L017}{}{}{« sionum convertenda. Hoc est eam addamus gradui cum quo Luna occidit, si differentia ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L018}{}{}{« arcum 13° 40′ pertinuerit; ascensionem obliquam nadir gradus ita provenientis sumamus,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L019}{}{}{« et ascensionem nadir gradus cum quo Luna occidit; minorem de maiore demamus; proveniet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L020}{}{}{« \textit{aequatio aequata} (\ArabicRun{التعديل المعدل}). Eam addamus 10° 50′, si differentia ad arcum 13° 40′ perti-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L021}{}{}{« nebat, aut de 10° 50′ demamus si ad arcum lucis spectabat; proveniet \textit{arcus apparitionis}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L022}{}{}{« \textit{correctus.} Postea si arcus morae fuerit arcui correcto apparitionis aequalis aut maior,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L023}{}{}{« Luna nova conspicietur; si minor, non apparebit ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0359-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent Exponit postea methodum Thābit ibn Qurrah (f. 89,r.–v.): « \textit{Caput V: de eo quod me-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L025}{}{}{« \textit{morat} \Name{Thābit ibn Qurrah} al–Ḥarrānī (1), qui arcum depressionis aequat per arcum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L026}{}{}{« lucis. Dicit: Locum verum Solis et Lunae determinemus (\ArabicRun{قومنا النيرين}) tempore occasus Lunae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L027}{}{}{« nocte trigesimi (2) diei mensis Arabici; item cognoscamus arcus lucis, morae, depressionis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L028}{}{}{« nec non distantiam Lunae a loco incidentiae perpendicularis (sive catheti) lucis (\ArabicRun{مسقط عمود النور}) (3).}
\par\vspace{0.32mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L029}{}{}{« — \textit{Sectio:} de limitibus totalibus (\ArabicRun{في الحدود الكلية}), e tabula [sumendis]. Cum eos quaerimus, in}
\par\vspace{-0.36mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L030}{}{}{« tabulam limitum visionis, a \Name{Thābit} confectam, cum anomalia Lunae intremus, et sumamus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L031}{}{}{« quod ei respondet ex arcubus totalibus generalibus (\ArabicRun{المطلقة}), [scilicet] arcu lucis, aequatione,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L032}{}{}{« extremo distantiae; haec omnia separatim servemus. — \textit{Sectio:} de consideratione facienda}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L033}{}{}{« (\ArabicRun{في الاعتبار}). Arcum lucis calculo repertum observemus; si fuerit minor arcu lucis e tabula}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L034}{}{}{« sumpto, ne speremus visionem Lunae ob parvitatem luminis in corpore Lunae; si aequalis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L035}{}{}{« fuerit extremo distantiae aut maior, Luna conspicietur die ante Solis occasum, nec erunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L036}{}{}{« necessariae supputationes; demum si fuerit maior arcu lucis e tabula sumpto, sed minor}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L037}{}{}{« extremo distantiae, necessaria erit aequatio. — \textit{Sectio:} de arcu lucis corrigendo. Si eum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L038}{}{}{« corrigere voluerimus, complementum arcus distantiae Lunae a loco incidentiae perpendicu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L039}{}{}{« laris lucis multiplicabimus in arcum lucis e tabula sumpto; productum per 360 dividemus;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L040}{}{}{« proveniet \textit{depressio correcta,} quam seorsum servabimus. Differentiam inter arcum lucis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L041}{}{}{« repertum [observatione] et arcum lucis e tabula provenientem sumamus et in depressionem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L042}{}{}{« correctam multiplicemus; productum dividamus per arcum aequationis tabulae; exibit \textit{se-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-body-L043}{}{}{« \textit{cunda aequatio.} Si differentia pertinebat ad arcum lucis repertum [observatione], aequa-}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{0.82mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0359-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Cfr. p. 266, sub fine adn. \textbf{\textit{a}}.}
\par\vspace{0.69mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0359-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Cfr. p. 269, adn. 5.}
\end{minipage}

\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{0.99mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0359-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (3) Ut ex fol. 87,r., apparet, supputatur formula:}
\par\vspace{-1.02mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0359-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{45.97mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{cos dist. \MoonSym{} a perpendic. lucis $= \dfrac{\text{cos arcus lucis}}{R\ \text{cos depress. Solis}}\,.$}}}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0360}{271}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 0.08mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L001}{}{}{« tionem secundam dememus a depressione correcta; si ad arcum lucis e tabula sumptum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L002}{}{}{« differentia pertinebat, aequationem secundam addemus depressioni correctae. Prodibit \textit{de-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L003}{}{}{« \textit{pressio adhibenda} (\ArabicRun{الانحطاط المحصى}). — \textit{Sectio:} De consideratione rursus facienda. Si depressio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L004}{}{}{« Solis quam calculo invenimus tempore occasus Lunae est aequalis depressioni adhibendae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L005}{}{}{« aut maior, Luna nova conspicitur; si minor, non conspicitur ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0360-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L006}{}{}{\Indent Postremo (cap. VI, f. 89,v.-90,v.) \Name{al–Khāzinī} methodum suam docet, quam, cum sit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L007}{}{}{Albatenio serior, nunc referre operis pretium non est.}
\par\vspace{4.48mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0360-H08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L008}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{d}}) — \textit{Pag. 88, l. 1–34.}}}
\par\vspace{4.02mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0360-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L009}{}{}{\Indent [« \textit{Ut nunc ad opinionem revertamur.....} ». — \Name{Al–Battānī} nunc aliam rationem eius-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L010}{}{}{dem calculi ducendi exponit; iisdem nititur principiis, sed alios numeros tanquam fundamen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L011}{}{}{tum sumit. Ponit Lunam, in maxima distantia a Terra i. e. minima diametro apparenti prae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L012}{}{}{ditam, visibilem fieri cum \textit{arcus apparitionis} sit 10° 50′ et distantia angularis a Sole 13° 40′.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L013}{}{}{Modo satis peregrino delectum horum numerorum explicat, dicens priorem esse \textit{minimum}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L014}{}{}{qui fieri possit motum relativum Solis et Lunae in ecliptica (nempe cum Sol maximum mo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L015}{}{}{tum horarium habeat, Luna minimum); alterum esse motum relativum \textit{maximum,} cum Sol}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L016}{}{}{quam tardissime, Luna quam celerrime moveatur. Sed quomodocumque res sit, condicio rerum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L017}{}{}{his numeris repraesentata haud multum differt a condicione in praecedenti solutione delecta.}
\par\vspace{-1.04mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L018}{}{}{Iuxta quae antea posuerat invenitur 11° 45′ $\dfrac{13}{12}\cdot\dfrac{\text{12° 11′}}{\text{13° 40′}} =$ 11° 21′ $=$ \textit{arcus apparitionis} res-}
\par\vspace{-0.52mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L019}{}{}{pondens maximae distantiae lunari et elongationi 13° 40′. Nunc pro 11° 21′ al–Battānī 10° 50′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L020}{}{}{tantum ponit; differentia parvi momenti est, attamen iuxta novum calculum interdum Luna}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L021}{}{}{nova potest visibilis iudicari in condicionibus in quibus iuxta priorem calculum apparere ne-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L022}{}{}{quibat. — Regula autem supputationis eadem est quae casus praecedentis, hoc tantum excepto}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L023}{}{}{quod in ratione ducenda distantiae Lunae a Terra tanquam fundamentum illius rationis sumi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L024}{}{}{tur distantia maxima pro media, et consequenter est operandum. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{4.60mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0360-H25}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L025}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{e}}) — \textit{Pag. 89, l. 31–32.}}}
\par\vspace{3.98mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0360-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L026}{}{}{\Indent [« \textit{Spatium inter duos arcus est novae Lunae imago} ». — Constructio auctoris incli-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L027}{}{}{nationem lineae cornuum respectu eclipticae satis exacte praebet, eo usque saltem quo diffe-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L028}{}{}{rentia longitudinis inter Solem et Lunam non fiat multo maior differentia quae solet primae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L029}{}{}{apparitionis die haberi. Animadvertendum etiam est GT esse \textit{veram latitudinem fusi} lunaris}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L030}{}{}{visibilis, non latitudinem \textit{falcis} quae fuso multo subtilior est, nam ibi superficies lunaris sub}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L031}{}{}{magna obliquitate conspicitur. Hoc modo intelligenda sunt verba « est novae Lunae imago ».}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L032}{}{}{Melius infra p. 90, l. 18–19: « Linea TG erit ..... quantitas digitorum lucis lunaris ». —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L033}{}{}{\textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{0.34mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0360-H34}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L034}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{f}}) — \textit{Pag. 91, l. 11 et seqq.}}}
\par\vspace{4.48mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0360-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L035}{}{}{\Indent I. \textit{Horizontis locum e quo Luna nova apparebit} cognoscere.}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0360-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L036}{}{}{\Indent [In figura huius paginae quaedam sunt cum textu minime convenientia; et in textu ipso}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L037}{}{}{quaedam, Italicis litteris impressa, haud recta. Quoniam EG est circulus verticalis per Lu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L038}{}{}{nam F transiens eiusque directionem G in horizonte indicans, punctum H eclipticae cum Luna}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L039}{}{}{culminans debebit non in linea a G ad E, sed in circulo maximo qui a Luna F ad polum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L040}{}{}{caelestem procedit esse. Attamen in textu codicis et Platonis linea KEHFG memoratur!}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0360-body-L041}{}{}{Figura male descripta, et textus ad eius errores accommodatus, ut alibi quoque vidimus,}
\DSource{0361}{272}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr -3.37mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\par\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.493\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0361-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0361-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.487\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.513\FullSourceWidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L001}{}{}{sunt. Veram \Name{Albatenii} imaginem eam fuisse}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L002}{}{}{existimo quam hoc loco describo; tunc quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L003}{}{}{p. 91 Italicis litteris sunt impressa deleri de-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L004}{}{}{bent. Ipsum punctum H omnino est inutile.}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L005}{}{}{Cur Luna debeat esse \textit{australis,} et nonnisi in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L006}{}{}{D haberi Libra, nescio; minus etiam intelligo}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L007}{}{}{cur H initium Scorpii esse debeat. — \textsc{Schia-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L008}{}{}{\textsc{parelli}.]}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0361-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L009}{}{}{\Indent II. \Name{Al–Battānī} p. 91, l. 1, 5, 9, 11, 34,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L010}{}{}{usum \textit{tuborum} in observationibus memorat,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L011}{}{}{quibus, ut notum est, magna pars radiorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L012}{}{}{lucis caelestis diffusae, qui visionem perturbant,}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L013}{}{}{impediuntur. Haec omnium antiquissima men-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L014}{}{}{tio tuborum apud Arabes est; nam qui historiam}
\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{-0.31mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L015}{}{}{opticae scripserunt nonnisi tubum cognoscunt aptatum magno sextanti, quo \Name{Naṣīr ad–dīn}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L016}{}{}{\Name{aṭ–Ṭūsī} in specula Marāghensi (anno 657 heg., 1259 Chr. condita) Solem observabat; loco}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L017}{}{}{alhidadae (dioptrae) tubus radios solares ad divisiones sextantis ducebat.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0361-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L018}{}{}{\Indent Apud \textit{astronomos Graecos} nulla exstat tuborum mentio, si \Name{Pseudo–Plutarchi} li-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L019}{}{}{bellus \textit{de facie in orbe Lunae} excipiatur, in quo de observanda Luna agitur. \Name{Aristoteles},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L020}{}{}{\textit{de generatione animalium,} V, 1, visionem comparat motui: « ita igitur potissimum res cer-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L021}{}{}{« nerentur longinquae, si illico ab oculo veluti fistula (\textgreek{αὐλός}) quaedam porrecta usque ad rem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L022}{}{}{« cernendam continuaretur; sic enim nulla ex parte dissolveretur motus, qui a re visibili pro-}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L023}{}{}{« ficeretur. Sed cum ita non sit, quo amplius porrigitur, eo longius cernatur, necesse est ».}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L024}{}{}{\Name{Polybius} X, 43 seqq. memorat dioptram, duabus instructam cannis, qua \Name{Cleoxenus} et}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L025}{}{}{\Name{Democletus} signa a longinquis militibus facta observabant. In \textit{Talmūd} narratur \Name{Rabbī}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L026}{}{}{\Name{Gamĕlī’ēl} tubum habuisse ad metiendas locorum distantias et profunditates vallium. In Eu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L027}{}{}{ropa demum \Name{Walafried} anno 825 in pago Reichenau usum tubi didicit. — Cfr. R. \Name{Wolf},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L028}{}{}{\textit{Handbuch der Astronomie, ihrer Geschichte und Litteratur,} Zürich 1890–93, t. I, p. 318–}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L029}{}{}{–19, § 133; et S. \Name{Günther}, \textit{Das gläserlose Sehrohr im Altertum und Mittelalter,} in Bi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L030}{}{}{bliotheca Mathematica hrsg. von G. Eneström, Neue Folge, t. VIII, Stockholm 1894, p. 15–23.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0361-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L031}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Schiaparelli} locum mihi indicat vetustissimi libri Sinici \textit{Shù–King} (1) inscripti, in quo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L032}{}{}{de Shun rege (2255–2205 ante Chr.) haec narrantur: « Observavit gemmatam (aut gem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L033}{}{}{« meam?) sphaeram et gemmis–ornatum tubum, et cognovit septem dirigentes [planetas] ».}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L034}{}{}{Sed e notitiis quas humanissime cl. sinologus L. \Name{Nocentini} mihi suppeditavit, patet hanc}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L035}{}{}{versionem non omni parte certam esse, et commentariis II saec. ante Chr. niti. Praeterea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L036}{}{}{e descriptione celeberrimi interpretis Ts‘ai C‘in (1167–1230 Chr.), qui vetustioribus illis com-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L037}{}{}{mentariis usus est, atque tubum transverse positum dicit, minime liquet utrum ad observan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-body-L038}{}{}{dum stellas an potius sphaeram ipsam armillarem tubus inserviisset.}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.89mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0361-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Pars II, lib. 1, nr. 3 iuxta versionem Angli-}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-notes_left-L002}{}{}{cam \Name{Legge} (\textit{The sacred books of the East,} t. III,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-notes_left-L003}{}{}{Oxford 1879, p. 38-39); vel pars I, cap. 2, nr. 5 iuxta}
\par\vspace{-0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-notes_left-L004}{}{}{vers. Gallicam \Name{Gaubil} a G. Pauthier recognitam}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.92mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-notes_right-L001}{}{}{(\textit{Les livres sacrés de l’Orient,} Paris 1840, p. 49). Si-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-notes_right-L002}{}{}{nice et Latine apud \Name{Zottoli}, \textit{Cursus litteraturae}}
\par\vspace{-0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0361-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\textit{Sinicae,} t. III, p. 333.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0362}{273}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 5.20mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\par\fontsize{15}{17}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0362-H01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L001}{}{}{\CenterLine{A\textsc{d} \textsc{caput} XLII.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.79mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0362-H02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L002}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{a}}) — \textit{Pag. 94, l. 5–11.}}}
\par\vspace{4.45mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0362-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L003}{}{}{\Indent [Praecepta pertinent ad secundam aequationem calculi evectionis. Si pro dupla elonga-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L004}{}{}{tione 180° radius apparens epicycli (ergo velocitas etiam qua aequatio anomaliae crescit) auge-}
\par\vspace{-0.99mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L005}{}{}{tur a 5° 1′ ad 7° 40′, quanto crescet pro dupla elongatione 2\textgreek{ε}°? Manifeste $\dfrac{\text{2° 39′}}{\text{5° 1′}}\cdot\dfrac{\text{2\textgreek{ε}°}}{\text{180°}}$ circiter.}
\par\vspace{-0.51mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L006}{}{}{Eadem proportione velocitas aequationis anomaliae crescet in segmento superiore epicycli contra}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L007}{}{}{ordinem signorum, in segmento inferiore ad signorum ordinem (ab occasu ad ortum). Haec velo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L008}{}{}{citas media horaria cum sit 33′ circumferentiae epicycli, in oculo observatoris, qui duodecies}
\par\vspace{-1.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L009}{}{}{longius consistit quam centrum epicycli, subtendet arcum duodecies minorem, seu $\dfrac{\text{33′}}{\text{12}} = \text{165″}$;}
\par\vspace{-0.40mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L010}{}{}{cuius quantitatis sumenda erit pars indicata ratione antea exposita. Ergo motus horarius ap-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L011}{}{}{parens anomaliae in epicyclo crescet (et decrescet motus horarius Lunae in ecliptica cum Luna}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L012}{}{}{fuerit apogea, crescet cum fuerit perigea) quantitate horaria:}
\par\vspace{2.43mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L013}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{94.88mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$\text{165″}\,\dfrac{\text{2° 39′}}{\text{5° 1′}}\cdot\dfrac{\text{2\textgreek{ε}°}}{\text{180°}} = \dfrac{\text{33″}}{\text{18}}\cdot\dfrac{\text{159}}{\text{301}}\cdot\dfrac{\text{\textgreek{ε}°}}{\text{1°}} = \dfrac{\text{5247″}}{\text{5418}}\cdot\dfrac{\text{\textgreek{ε}°}}{\text{1°}}\,.$}}}
\par\vspace{3.12mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L014}{}{}{Coefficiens tam paulum ab unitate differt ut fere 1 sumi possit; dicemus tunc \textit{ob appropin-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L015}{}{}{\textit{quationem epicycli ad observatorem cum Luna extra mediam coniunctionem sit, motum}}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L016}{}{}{\textit{eius horarium apparentem in parte apogea epicycli tot secundis singula hora retardari}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L017}{}{}{\textit{quot gradus contineantur elongatione} \textgreek{ε}°; \textit{totidemque hunc motum horarium accelerari}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L018}{}{}{\textit{in epicycli parte perigea.} Hic ponit \Name{Albatenius} Lunam esse prope perigeum aut apogeum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L019}{}{}{epicycli; effectus in maximis aequationibus anomaliae nullus est. At manifeste auctor rudem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L020}{}{}{adproximationem sufficere existimavit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0362-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L021}{}{}{\Indent Ex iis quae diximus patet usus tabellae in parte dextera t. II, p. 88 et cur in adpendice H}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L022}{}{}{(pag. 150) signum correctionis dicatur negativum esse a 265° ad 95° (in segmento epicycli supe-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L023}{}{}{riore), positivum a 95° ad 265° (in segmento inferiore). Tabella ultra 7° non procedit, quia elon-}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L024}{}{}{gatio media tempore verae coniunctionis 7° paulum excedere potest, et nunquam 8° attingit.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L025}{}{}{— \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{5.25mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0362-H26}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L026}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{b}}) — \textit{Pag. 95, l. 33—pag. 96, l. 2.}}}
\par\vspace{4.43mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0362-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L027}{}{}{\Indent [Causa qua \textit{anomaliae lunari} $\qfrac{1}{6}$ + $\qfrac{1}{8}$ differentiae longitudinis adduntur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0362-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L028}{}{}{\Indent Praecepto pag. 93, l. 38–40 et eius rudi demonstratione quam nunc praebet, \Name{al–Battānī}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L029}{}{}{aliquo modo vult calculum evectionis non negligere, cum tempus coniunctionis verae aliquot}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L030}{}{}{horis distet a tempore mediae. Primum casuum ab Albatenio datorum ponamus, nempe tem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L031}{}{}{pore mediae coniunctionis locum verum Solis in ecliptica praecedere verum locum Lunae;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L032}{}{}{\textgreek{ε} esse differentiam longitudinis in gradibus. Luna Soli appropinquabit uno gradu singulis dua-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L033}{}{}{bus horis fere exacte; ergo vera coniunctio eveniet fere 2\textgreek{ε} horas post mediam, elongatio me-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L034}{}{}{dia (quae tempore mediae coniunctionis nulla erat) in coniunctione vera \textgreek{ε} erit, at longitudo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L035}{}{}{\textbf{\textit{media}} Lunae \textgreek{ε} gradibus longitudinem mediam Solis \textit{excedet,} et elongatio media lunaris erit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L036}{}{}{2\textgreek{ε} gradibus in ortum versus, scilicet in directione positiva.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0362-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L037}{}{}{\Indent Sit AK circulus excentricus Lunae. O centrum, E Terra, N centrum aequantis seu pun-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0362-body-L038}{}{}{ctum circa quod motus centri epicycli semper uniformis apparet (cfr. p. 237), qua re EN $=$ EO.}
\par\vspace{5mm}\hfill{\fontsize{8}{10}\selectfont 35}\hspace{10mm}
\DSource{0363}{274}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr -3.47mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\par\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.402\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0363-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0363-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.578\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.422\FullSourceWidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L001}{}{}{Punctum S indicet directionem Solis; angulus AES sit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L002}{}{}{elongatio media, cuius duplum AEQ; erit Q centrum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L003}{}{}{epicycli, punctum P origo mediarum anomaliarum seu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L004}{}{}{apogeum medium, R apogeum verum, PR aequatio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L005}{}{}{anomaliae \textit{addenda} anomaliae mediae ut vera prove-}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L006}{}{}{niat. Oportet demonstrare in locis propinquis coniun-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L007}{}{}{ctioni angulum RPQ esse $\qfrac{1}{6}$ + $\qfrac{1}{8}$ $=$ $\qfrac{7}{24}$ elongationis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L008}{}{}{mediae QES, vel $\qfrac{7}{48}$ duplae elongationis QEA.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0363-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L009}{}{}{\Indent Cum anguli in E, N omnes sint multum parvi,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L010}{}{}{possumus noto opticae theoremate uti et affirmare an-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L011}{}{}{gulos QEA, QNA, sub quibus arcus QA e punctis E}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L012}{}{}{et N cernitur, inter se esse ratione inversa distantia-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L013}{}{}{rum horum punctorum ab arcu; ergo QEA : QNA $=$}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L014}{}{}{$=$ NA : EA, unde QEA $-$ QNA : QEA $=$ NA $-$ EA : NA,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L015}{}{}{vel NQE : QEA $=$ EN : NA. Et quoniam, iuxta Ptole-}
\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{-0.35mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L016}{}{}{maeum, si EA $=$ 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} sumatur est NE $=$ 10\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 19′, et quoniam NQE $=$ RQP $=$ \textit{portio} seu aequa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L017}{}{}{tio argumenti, erit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L018}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{93.35mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{aequatio argumenti $=$ dupla elongatio $\dfrac{10^{p}\,19'}{70^{p}\,19'} =$ elongatio $\dfrac{20^{p}\,38'}{70^{p}\,19'}\,.$}}}
\par\vspace{0.44mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L019}{}{}{Quod tanto erit veritati propius quanto minores fuerint anguli considerati; semper autem pro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L020}{}{}{usu huius calculi astronomici satis exactum.}
\par\vspace{-1.38mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0363-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L021}{}{}{\Indent Ratio $\dfrac{20^{p}\,38'}{70^{p}\,19'}$ in numeris decimalibus 0,2934 efficit; \Name{al–Battānī} a rudiore sua supputa-}
\par\vspace{-0.50mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L022}{}{}{tione $\qfrac{1}{6}$ + $\qfrac{1}{8}$ $=$ 0,2916 deducit. Concordantia optima. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{4.48mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0363-H23}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L023}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{c}}) — \textit{Pag. 96, l. 3–9.}}}
\par\vspace{3.35mm}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{137.25mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0363-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent [« \textit{In hoc Ptolemaeus} ..... ». — Satis obscure vult nunc \Name{al-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L025}{}{}{\Name{–Battānī} ostendere cur antea Lunam consideraverit in epicycli posi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L026}{}{}{tione cui respondet aequatio 3°, cum primo aspectu censeri possit casus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L027}{}{}{maioris momenti esse in positione respondenti aequationi 5°. Auctor ait,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L028}{}{}{aequatione fere 3° et correctione anomaliae 1° $\qfrac{1}{2}$ exstantibus, errorem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L029}{}{}{provenientem a neglecta nova correctione maximam suam quantitatem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L030}{}{}{attingere; posita vero aequatione $=$ 5°, correctionem nullam aut parvi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L031}{}{}{esse momenti. Haec vera esse e sequentibus patebit: Sit C centrum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L032}{}{}{epicycli, E Terra. Arcus \textit{ab} et \textgreek{αβ} sint $\qfrac{7}{24}$ elongationis; \textit{ab} in loco}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L033}{}{}{quo aequatio CE\textit{a} $=$ 3°, \textgreek{αβ} in loco quo aequatio CE\textgreek{α} $=$ 5°. Manifeste}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L034}{}{}{si correctio illarum $\qfrac{7}{24}$ elongationis negligitur, error in priore loco}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L035}{}{}{est angulus \textit{a}E\textit{b}, multo maior errore qui fit in loco posteriore ubi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L036}{}{}{nullus aut negligendus apparet.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0363-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L037}{}{}{\Indent Sed quis (1) poterit dicere: Cur tanquam locum maximi erro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L038}{}{}{ris partem AB epicycli non sumis? Nonne est angulus AEB (erroris}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L039}{}{}{quantitas) maior etiam quam in praecedentibus casibus? \Name{Al–Battānī}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0363-body-L040}{}{}{ita respondet: Si Luna in A aut B (vel etiam in A′ aut B′) consistit,}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{48.75mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0363-body-F02}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0363-F02}\end{minipage}\par

\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L001}{}{}{elongatio pendit a sola aequatione Solis, et summum 2° est; ergo eius $\qfrac{7}{24}$ paulo \textcolor{black!22}{\rule[-.2ex]{19.2mm}{1.6ex}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L002}{}{}{gradum excedunt, et saepe ne dimidium quidem attingunt. Unde consequitur re vera \textcolor{black!22}{\rule[-.2ex]{17.8mm}{1.6ex}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L003}{}{}{loca circa \textit{ab}) esse puncta in quibus, a neglectis iis $\qfrac{7}{24}$ elongationis, maximus proveni\textcolor{black!22}{\rule[-.2ex]{17.3mm}{1.6ex}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L004}{}{}{hic autem $\qfrac{1}{8}$ gradus vel $\qfrac{1}{4}$ horae circiter (1) est in motu relativo duorum luminari\textcolor{black!22}{\rule[-.2ex]{16.1mm}{1.6ex}}}
\par\vspace{0.58mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L005}{}{}{\textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L007}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{a}}) — \textit{Pag. 97, l. 35.}}}
\par\vspace{4.53mm}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{123.50mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0364-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L008}{}{}{\Indent [Vocem \textit{moram} \Name{al–Battānī} vario sensu usurpat. Sit O}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L009}{}{}{centrum umbrae, \textit{b} centrum Lunae in medio eclipsis, \textit{a} centrum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L010}{}{}{Lunae in fine totalitatis, \textit{c} in initio. Hoc loco et p. 98, l. 36,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L011}{}{}{\textit{minuta morae} sunt quantitas AK qua magnitudo eclipsis to-}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L012}{}{}{talis 12 digitos excedit; at p. 98, l. 6 et passim saepe, \textit{minuta}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L013}{}{}{\textit{morae} sunt $\qfrac{1}{2}$ longitudo \textit{bc} spatii quod Luna intra umbram}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L014}{}{}{percurrit ab initio ad finem totalitatis. \textit{Horae morae} vel \textit{tem-}}
\par\vspace{0.14mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L015}{}{}{\textit{pus morae} indicant temporis intervallum in quo Luna a \textit{b} ad \textit{a}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L016}{}{}{pervenit, nempe dimidium tempus totalitatis. Demum \textit{totam}}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{62.50mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0364-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0364-F01}\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{-0.30mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L017}{}{}{\textit{moram} appellat maximum valorem morae, qui eclipsi centrali respondet; est igitur dimidia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L018}{}{}{differentia diametrorum umbrae et Lunae. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{5.50mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0364-H19}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L019}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{b}}) — \textit{Pag. 98, l. 39—pag. 99, l. 3.}}}
\par\vspace{4.53mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0364-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L020}{}{}{\Indent [Verba Italicis litteris impressa aliquot erroribus scatent ab amanuensium negligentia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L021}{}{}{provenientibus. Probabilis restitutio, ut facile e figura apparet, haec est: « Ab hoc motu, ut}
\par\vspace{0.13mm}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.509\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0364-body-F02}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0364-F02}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.471\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.529\FullSourceWidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L022}{}{}{« vidimus, latitudinem Lunae ambobus tem-}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L023}{}{}{« poribus disce [BA vel DE], \textit{eiusque qua-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L024}{}{}{« \textit{dratum deme de quadratum semidiffe-}}
\par\vspace{0.11mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L025}{}{}{« \textit{rentiae} [OB vel OD] \textit{duarum diame-}}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L026}{}{}{« \textit{trorum quae est quantitas minutorum}}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L027}{}{}{« \textit{totius morae} (2). \textit{Quantitati residuae}}
\par\vspace{-1.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L028}{}{}{« [$\overline{\text{OA}}^{2}$ vel $\overline{\text{OE}}^{2}$, sive, quod idem est, $\overline{\text{HB}}^{2}$}
\par\vspace{-0.94mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L029}{}{}{« vel $\overline{\text{DC}}^{2}$] \textit{adde quadratum differentiae}}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L030}{}{}{« inter latitudines Lunae in medio eclipsis}
\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{-2.51mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L031}{}{}{« et eo tempore [$\overline{\text{IH}}^{2}$ vel $\overline{\text{IC}}^{2}$]; radicem huius summae [$\sqrt{\overline{\text{HB}}^{2} + \overline{\text{IH}}^{2}} = \text{IB}$, vel $\sqrt{\overline{\text{DC}}^{2} + \overline{\text{IC}}^{2}} = \text{ID}$]}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L032}{}{}{« divide per praecessionem Lunae. Quod e tempore initii provenit ..... ». Similiter demonstran-}
\par\vspace{-0.28mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L033}{}{}{tur praecedentes regulae p. 98. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{5.81mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0364-H34}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L034}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{c}}) — \textit{Pag. 99, l. 22—pag. 100, l. 8.}}}
\par\vspace{4.25mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0364-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L035}{}{}{\Indent [\textit{Calculus superficiei disci lunaris occultatae.}}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0364-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-body-L036}{}{}{\Indent Regulas auctoris ad supputationem \textit{sagittarum} Lunae et umbrae non memini me apud}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.32mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0364-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Pag. 96, l. 6, cod. et Plato $\qfrac{1}{8}$ horae habent.}
\par\vspace{-0.98mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0364-notes_left-L002}{}{}{Sed corrigendum videtur $\qfrac{1}{8}$ gradus, ut p. 96, l. 1.}

\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.78mm}
