\DSource{0365}{276}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr -3.22mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\par\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.552\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0365-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0365-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.428\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.572\FullSourceWidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L001}{}{}{alios scriptores invenisse; hoc modo pos-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L002}{}{}{sunt demonstrari. Ait \Name{al–Battānī} sa-}
\par\vspace{-0.99mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L003}{}{}{gittam LK umbrae $= \dfrac{\text{KA} \times \text{KN}}{2\,\text{SV}}$. Sit}
\par\vspace{-0.53mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L004}{}{}{\textit{a} semidiametrus umbrae, \textit{c} semidiame-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L005}{}{}{trus Lunae, \textit{b} $=$ VS distantia centrorum.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L006}{}{}{Haec distantia per chordam communem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L007}{}{}{BC dividitur in duas partes SL $=$ \textit{y},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L008}{}{}{LV $=$ \textit{x}; qua re \textit{x} + \textit{y} $=$ \textit{b}. Tunc no-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L009}{}{}{tum \Name{Euclidis} (II, 13) theorema docet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L010}{}{}{in triangulo SBV esse}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L011}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{40.21mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$a^{2} = b^{2} + c^{2} - 2b{\cdot}x$}}}
\par\vspace{0.44mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L012}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{40.21mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$c^{2} = b^{2} + a^{2} - 2b{\cdot}y$}}}
\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{-0.90mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L013}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}ergo}\rlap{\hspace*{57.84mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$x = \dfrac{b^{2} - a^{2} + c^{2}}{2b}$}}\rlap{\hspace*{130.20mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$y = \dfrac{b^{2} + a^{2} - c^{2}}{2b}\,.$}}}
\par\vspace{0.68mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L014}{}{}{Facile nunc est ambas sagittas LK $=$ \textit{a} $-$ \textit{y}, LN $=$ \textit{c} $-$ \textit{x} supputare. Valores nuper inventos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L015}{}{}{linearum \textit{y} et \textit{x} surrogando:}
\par\vspace{-0.45mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L016}{}{}{$LK = a - y = a - \dfrac{b^{2} + a^{2} - c^{2}}{2b} = \dfrac{2ab - b^{2} - a^{2} + c^{2}}{2b} = \dfrac{c^{2} - (a - b)^{2}}{2b} = \dfrac{1}{2b}\Bigl\{c + (b - a)\Bigr\}\Bigl\{c - (b - a)\Bigr\}\,,$}
\par\vspace{-0.66mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L017}{}{}{unde}
\par\vspace{-0.68mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L018}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(\textgreek{α})}\rlap{\hspace*{93.80mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$LK = \text{sagitta umbrae} = \dfrac{1}{2SV}\,KA \times KN.$}}}
\par\vspace{-0.72mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L019}{}{}{Similiter}
\par\vspace{-1.35mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L020}{}{}{$LN = c - x = c - \dfrac{b^{2} - a^{2} + c^{2}}{2b} = \dfrac{2bc - b^{2} + a^{2} - c^{2}}{2b} = \dfrac{a^{2} - (b - c)^{2}}{2b} = \dfrac{1}{2b}\Bigl\{a + (b - c)\Bigr\}\Bigl\{a - (b - c)\Bigr\}\,,$}
\par\vspace{1.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L021}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{94.19mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$LN = \text{sagitta disci lunaris} = \dfrac{1}{2SV}\,ZN \times KN.$}}}
\par\vspace{0.66mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L022}{}{}{\Name{Al–Battānī} formulam (\textgreek{α}) proponit; sagittam disci lunaris a sagitta umbrae directe deducit}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L023}{}{}{statuens}
\par\vspace{1.62mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L024}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(\textgreek{β})}\rlap{\hspace*{10.91mm}$LN = \text{sagitta disci lunaris} = NK - LK = \text{digiti eclipsis correcti} - \text{sagitta umbrae}.$}}
\par\vspace{1.39mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0365-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L025}{}{}{\Indent Solutio vere elegantissima.}
\par\vspace{0.11mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0365-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L026}{}{}{\Indent Postea \Name{al-Battānī} semichordam BL (\textit{dimidium sinum communem}) supputat, utens}
\par\vspace{-0.39mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L027}{}{}{nota regula BL $= \sqrt{\text{NL} \times \text{LA}}$:}
\par\vspace{0.88mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L028}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(\textgreek{γ})}\rlap{\hspace*{14.10mm}dimidius sinus communis $= \sqrt{\text{sag. disc. lun.} \times (\text{digit. diam. lun.} - \text{sag. disc. lun.})}$.}}
\par\vspace{1.58mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0365-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L029}{}{}{\Indent Aream trianguli BVC (\textit{trianguli Lunae}) invenit multiplicando dimidium sinum communem}
\par\vspace{0.11mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L030}{}{}{BL in distantiam LV; haec autem distantia est LV $=$ KV + LK $=$ (semidiam. \MoonSym{} $-$ digit. eclipsis}
\par\vspace{-0.11mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L031}{}{}{correcti) + sagitta umbrae (1). Similiter \textit{trianguli umbrae} BSC area supputatur. — Quo fa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L032}{}{}{cto \textit{aream arcus Lunae}, idest sectoris VBNC, deducit multiplicando semidiametrum Lunae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L033}{}{}{VN in \textit{arcum Lunae}, scilicet in arcum BN, e tabulis sumptum auxilio sinus BL. Similiter}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L034}{}{}{\textit{arcus umbrae} BK $\times$ semidiametrus SK umbrae $=$ \textit{area arcus umbrae}. — Demum}
\par\vspace{1.51mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L035}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}(\textgreek{δ})}\rlap{\hspace*{94.24mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{(area arcus \MoonSym{} + area arcus umbrae) $-$ (triang. \MoonSym{} + triang. umbrae) $=$}}}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L036}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{94.57mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{$=$ area partis occultatae disci lunaris,}}}
\par\vspace{1.40mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-body-L037}{}{}{quae est area figurae curvilineae NBKC. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}{\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{7.18mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0365-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0365-notes-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Cod. et Plato perperam « sagittam disci lunaris » habent (p. 99, l. 28-30).}
}
\DSource{0366}{277}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 2.55mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\par\fontsize{15}{17}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0366-H01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L001}{}{}{\CenterLine{A\textsc{d} \textsc{caput} XLIV.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.63mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0366-H02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L002}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{a}}) — \textit{Pag. 104–106.}}}
\par\vspace{3.80mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0366-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L003}{}{}{\Indent Calculus \textit{coniunctionis apparentis Solis et Lunae.}}
\par\vspace{-0.57mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0366-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L004}{}{}{\Indent [Ratio inter coniunctionem apparentem et coniunctionem veram intercedens in eclipsibus}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L005}{}{}{Solis, duo diversa problemata gignit. Cum tempus coniunctionis apparentis cognoscatur e directa}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L006}{}{}{eclipsis observatione, epocha verae coniunctionis facillime elicitur. Sufficit effectum parallaxis}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L007}{}{}{tempore coniunctionis verae supputare; tunc differentia verarum longitudinum Solis et Lunae}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L008}{}{}{invenietur, a qua differentia, cognito motu horario synodico vel relativo, facile tempus verae}
\par\vspace{0.55mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L009}{}{}{coniunctionis deducetur.}
\par\vspace{-0.67mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0366-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L010}{}{}{\Indent Sed cum datum sit tempus verae coniunctionis, ad coniunctionem apparentem deducendam}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L011}{}{}{necesse est cognoscere parallaxim tempore apparentis coniunctionis, quod ipsum tempus igno-}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L012}{}{}{tum est. Problema igitur directe non potest resolvi, sed tantum per adproximationes. \Name{Al–}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L013}{}{}{\Name{–Battānī} aliquot subsequentibus correctionibus adproximatis utitur, et hoc modo procedit.}
\par\vspace{0.22mm}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{89.92mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0366-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L014}{}{}{\Indent Sit AB ecliptica in regione sphaerae cae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L015}{}{}{lestis in qua Sol et Luna consistunt tempore}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L016}{}{}{eclipsis; tempore coniunctionis verae (qualis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L017}{}{}{nempe ex centro Terrae conspiceretur, et qua-}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L018}{}{}{lis e tabulis astronomicis deducitur) sit L′ locus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L019}{}{}{verus Lunae, S′ locus verus Solis, L′S′ per-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L020}{}{}{pendicularis ad AB. Sit L′Z′ directio circuli}
\par\vspace{-0.22mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L021}{}{}{verticalis Lunae eodem temporis momento; ob}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L022}{}{}{parallaxim altitudinis Luna observatori appa-}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L023}{}{}{rebit non in L′, sed in \textgreek{λ}′. Si ex \textgreek{λ}′ ducatur per-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L024}{}{}{pendicularis \textgreek{λ}′\textgreek{σ}′, arcus eclipticae S′\textgreek{σ}′ manifeste}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L025}{}{}{id erit quod \Name{al–Battānī} \textit{primam paralla-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L026}{}{}{\textit{xim} [longitudinis] vocat, nempe differentia lon-}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L027}{}{}{gitudinis quae re vera observatur inter Solem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L028}{}{}{S′ et Lunam \textgreek{λ}′ tempore verae coniunctionis;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L029}{}{}{quod temporis momentum nos \textgreek{ϑ} appellabimus.}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L030}{}{}{In casu quem figura repraesentat, Luna est}
\par\vspace{-0.12mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L031}{}{}{ab occasu nonagesimi (seu gradus qui 90° ab}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L032}{}{}{ascendente distat); et quoniam Luna in ecliptica}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L033}{}{}{retro est respectu Solis (scilicet \textgreek{σ}′ retro respe-}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{96.08mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0366-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0366-F01}\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{-0.48mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L034}{}{}{ctu S′), ei quodam tempore opus erit ut longitudinem Solis consequatur. Hoc temporis spa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L035}{}{}{tium est quod invenire debemus.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0366-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L036}{}{}{\Indent Si effectus parallaxis in longitudine semper unus idemque maneret, sufficeret arcum S′\textgreek{σ}′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L037}{}{}{per motum horarium relativum duorum luminarium dividere; tempus quaesitum proveniret. At}
\par\vspace{-0.11mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L038}{}{}{parallaxis celeri gressu mutatur; et quoniam intervallum inter coniunctionem veram et appa-}
\par\vspace{-0.14mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L039}{}{}{rentem potest duas horas fere interdum attingere, ne eam quidem uniformi modo mutari arbi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L040}{}{}{trari possumus. Si igitur, ut \Name{al–Battānī} iubet, parallaxim longitudinis S′\textgreek{σ}′ sumimus et per}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L041}{}{}{motum horarium relativum dividimus, non invenimus tempus quo coniunctio apparens respectu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L042}{}{}{verae retardatur, sed tantum quantitatem illi tempori vicinam. Sit \textgreek{ε}′ hoc tempus vero proxi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L043}{}{}{mum, nempe, ut \Name{al–Battānī} dicit, \textit{horae primae parallaxis}. Addatur tempori \textgreek{ϑ}; exibit}
\par\vspace{0.27mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0366-body-L044}{}{}{prima adproximatio problematis et temporis quaesiti, scilicet \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}′.}
\DSource{0367}{278}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0367-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent Postea arcus S′\textgreek{σ}′ addatur longitudini Lunae, anomaliae, et argumento latitudinis; hoc}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L002}{}{}{est ponatur Lunam in orbita sua caelesti a L′ ad L² progressam esse, percurrendo arcum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L003}{}{}{L′L² $=$ S′\textgreek{σ}′. Pro tempore correcto \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}′ rursus ascendens determinetur; et per novum locum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L004}{}{}{L² Lunae denuo parallaxis altitudinis L²\textgreek{λ}² supputetur; \textgreek{λ}² erit locus in quo Luna cernetur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L005}{}{}{tempore \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}′. Parallaxis longitudinis (\textit{parallaxis secunda} Albatenii) erit \textgreek{Σσ}², determinata}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L006}{}{}{perpendicularibus ex L² et \textgreek{λ}² ad eclipticam ductis. Sit S² locus Solis tempore \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}′. Si S²}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L007}{}{}{et \textgreek{σ}² inter se congruerint, problema resolutum erit. Secus nova correctio tempori \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}′ erit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L008}{}{}{inferenda, ut exeat tempus coniunctionis apparentis; similiter \textit{parallaxis secunda} denuo cor-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L009}{}{}{rigenda, licet semper multo vicinior sit veritati quam prima parallaxis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0367-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L010}{}{}{\Indent Qua re, \Name{al–Battānī} inquit, si \textit{secundam parallaxim} per motum horarium relativum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L011}{}{}{duorum luminarium diviserimus, inveniemus correctionem \textgreek{ε}² temporis meliorem correctione \textgreek{ε}′.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L012}{}{}{Hanc \textgreek{ε}² vocat \textit{horas secundae parallaxis}. Pro novo tempore exactiore \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}² rursus ascen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L013}{}{}{dens, medium Caelum, locus Lunae, anomalia Lunae inveniantur; ab iis nova parallaxis (\textit{ter-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L014}{}{}{\textit{tia parallaxis}) deducatur. Si haec fuerit aequalis secundae, problema resolutum erit, et tem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L015}{}{}{pus \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}² indicabit tempus coniunctionis apparentis; nam tunc certissime sciemus parallaxim}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L016}{}{}{secundam et tertiam exacte esse aequales differentiae verarum longitudinum Solis et Lunae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L017}{}{}{qua re luminarium longitudines apparentes inter se aequales exstare.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0367-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L018}{}{}{\Indent Sed ex hoc \Name{al–Battānī} quaedam \textit{falsa} concludit, Italicis litteris impressa p. 104, l. 41–}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L019}{}{}{–42, p. 105, l. 1–3, 24–25, 34, p. 110, l. 25–26. Dicit enim: Si tertia parallaxis aequalis est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L020}{}{}{secundae, etiam longitudines apparentes duorum luminarium sunt aequales. Qua re si tertia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L021}{}{}{parallaxis a secunda differt quadam quantitate, eadem differunt inter se longitudines apparen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L022}{}{}{tes correspondentes Solis et Lunae; id est ea quantitate tertia parallaxis excedere debet dif-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L023}{}{}{ferentiam verarum longitudinum Lunae et Solis. — Id verum non est; nam ex eo quod duae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L024}{}{}{differentiae inter se coaequantur cum nullae sint, minime consequitur eas etiam esse aequales}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L025}{}{}{quando nullae non sunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0367-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L026}{}{}{\Indent Sit \textgreek{Δ} differentia longitudinum apparentium, quam ut investigemus restat, verae longitudini}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L027}{}{}{Lunae applicando tertiam parallaxim: debemus hanc differentiam residuam in nihilum redigere,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L028}{}{}{aliquantum rursus corrigentes tempus \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}². Sufficit velocitatem motus apparentis relativi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L029}{}{}{Solis et Lunae in longitudine supputare; per hanc velocitatem dividatur \textgreek{Δ}, proveniet tempus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L030}{}{}{coniunctionis apparentis. — Pro quantitate \textgreek{Δ}, \Name{al–Battānī} perperam \textit{differentiam inter se-}}
\par\vspace{0.05mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L031}{}{}{\textit{cundam et tertiam parallaxim} adhibet. Si hic \textit{error} excipiatur, \Name{Albatenii} supputatio vera}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L032}{}{}{est, et hoc modo explicatur:}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0367-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L033}{}{}{\Indent Motus relativus apparens horarius Lunae et Solis deducitur a motu relativo horario vero}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L034}{}{}{quem tabulae praebent (\textit{praecessio Lunae respectu Solis,} ut Arabes dicunt), ab hoc demendo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L035}{}{}{(et nunquam ei \textit{addendo}) variationem horariam parallaxis, absolute consideratam sine distin-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L036}{}{}{ctione signi. Si ea uniformis esset, id est proportionalis ad variationem anguli horarii, variatio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L037}{}{}{parallaxis respondens minuto temporis facillime inveniretur differentiam inter duas parallaxes}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L038}{}{}{repertas dividendo per minuta temporis ab altera ad alteram elapsa. At variatio tam rapide}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L039}{}{}{mutatur, ut ne intra spatium quidem unius horae possit aequabilis censeri; calculus igitur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L040}{}{}{pro tam longo temporis intervallo duci nequit. Oportet altera saltem adproximatione uti, nempe}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L041}{}{}{alteram saltem parallaxim determinare et tempus \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}′ ei correspondens. Error huius tem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L042}{}{}{poris erit tunc parva fractio horae, in qua licebit variationem parallaxis existimare unifor-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L043}{}{}{mem, et uniformem quoque motum relativum apparentem longitudinis. Melius etiam si ter-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L044}{}{}{tiam parallaxim supputemus, et tempus \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}² ei respondens.— Problema nunc est cognoscere}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L045}{}{}{velocitatem horariam motus relativi apparentis in brevi temporis intervallo necessario ad redi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0367-body-L046}{}{}{gendam in nihilum differentiam residuam \textgreek{Δ} longitudinum apparentium.}
\DSource{0368}{279}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0368-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Al–Battānī} p. 105, l. 6–24, statuit breve temporis intervallum in quo variatio parallaxis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L002}{}{}{uniformis censenda sit non posse excedere $\qfrac{1}{6}$ horae, seu 10\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} anguli horarii in tempore. Dua-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L003}{}{}{rum parallaxium quibus opus est uti, una iam nota est, et angulo horario \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}² respondet,}
\par\vspace{-0.06mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L004}{}{}{ut nuper vidimus. Auctor duos distinguit casus:}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0368-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L005}{}{}{\Indent 1. Si angulus horarius seu tempus \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}² ab hora integra proxima magis $\qfrac{1}{6}$ horae dif-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L006}{}{}{fert, oportet nos parallaxim novam pro tempore \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}² + $\qfrac{1}{6}$ vel \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}² $-$ $\qfrac{1}{6}$ directe sup-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L007}{}{}{putare. Differentia duarum parallaxium erit variatio parallactica in $\qfrac{1}{6}$ horae; eam in 6 mul-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L008}{}{}{tiplicando inveniemus motum parallacticum horarium, qui demendus erit a « praecessione}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L009}{}{}{Lunae » (motu horario relativo longitudinis verae), ut exeat « \textit{motus aequatus} » Albatenii,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L010}{}{}{scilicet motus horarius relativus apparens longitudinis amborum luminarium. — Tabulis Theo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L011}{}{}{nis uti non possumus, quia proximum datum in his tabulis respondet intervallo maiori $\qfrac{1}{6}$ horae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L012}{}{}{ab angulo horario \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}².}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0368-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L013}{}{}{\Indent 2. At si fractio horae in tempore \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}² minor est $\qfrac{1}{6}$ aut maior $\qfrac{5}{6}$ (exempli gratia $\qfrac{1}{8}$,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L014}{}{}{$\qfrac{1}{10}$ ..... aut $\qfrac{7}{8}$, $\qfrac{9}{10}$ .....), calculum brevius ducere possumus utentes hora integra proxima,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L015}{}{}{scilicet tanquam intervallum sumentes $\qfrac{1}{8}$, $\qfrac{1}{10}$ ..... Limites intervalli tunc erunt angulus hora-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L016}{}{}{rius \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}² cuius parallaxis iam nota, et angulus horarius constans horis integris, cuius pa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L017}{}{}{rallaxis indicatur in tabulis Theonis. Hoc modo aliquid laboris vitatur. — Manifeste tale com-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L018}{}{}{modum haberi nequit nisi intervallum $\qfrac{1}{8}$, $\qfrac{1}{10}$ ..... horae sumatur; intervallum $\qfrac{1}{6}$ vitari debet,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L019}{}{}{nam extra horam integram ducit. — « \textit{Motus aequatus} » ut antea supputabitur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0368-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L020}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Al–Battānī} postea iure observat parallaxim longitudinis decrescere cum tempore et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L021}{}{}{angulo horario, si Luna inter ascendens et nonagesimum consistat; crescere, si Luna inter}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L022}{}{}{nonagesimum et descendens reperiatur. In priore casu docet (l. 27–33) $\qfrac{1}{6}$ horae \textit{demere} de}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L023}{}{}{\textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}² ad efficiendum intervallum inter angulos horarios \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}² et \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}² $-$ $\qfrac{1}{6}$; in casu po-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L024}{}{}{steriore \textit{addere} $\qfrac{1}{6}$ horae, ut intervallum inter angulos horarios \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}² et \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}² + $\qfrac{1}{6}$ pro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L025}{}{}{veniat. Hoc praeceptum dat ut nova parallaxis auxiliaria semper maior sit parallaxe anguli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L026}{}{}{\textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}². Res \textit{utilis} ad vitandos errores signi, at non \textit{necessaria}; praesertim cum praeceptum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L027}{}{}{ad casum 1. tantum pertineat, non ad 2.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0368-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L028}{}{}{\Indent Demum per \textit{motum aequatum} dividatur differentia residua \textgreek{Δ} longitudinum apparentium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L029}{}{}{Solis et Lunae; proveniet tempusculum \textgreek{τ}, quod tempori adproximato \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}² addendum aut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L030}{}{}{detrahendum erit, prout Luna eo tempore \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}² Solem in longitudine sequebatur aut prae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L031}{}{}{cedebat. Prodibit \textit{tempus quaesitum coniunctionis apparentis}.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0368-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L032}{}{}{\Indent Omnia auctoris praecepta ita breviter exponi possunt. Pro dato tempore \textgreek{ϑ} verae coniun-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L033}{}{}{ctionis supputetur prima parallaxis et per « praecessionem Lunae » dividatur; exibit tempus \textgreek{ε}′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L034}{}{}{qui primam adproximationem \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}′ dabit. Hoc novo tempore \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}′ rursus longitudines Solis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L035}{}{}{et Lunae, anomalia Lunae, ascendens supputentur; ab iis nova parallaxis deducatur, quae, per}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L036}{}{}{praecessionem Lunae divisa, tempus \textgreek{ε}² praebebit; hinc secunda adproximatio \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}². Hoc tertio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L037}{}{}{tempore denuo longitudines Solis et Lunae, anomalia Lunae, ascendens supputentur; tertia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L038}{}{}{parallaxis ab iis cognoscatur. Ea addatur verae longitudini Lunae; exibit longitudo apparens}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L039}{}{}{Lunae. Si haec fuerit longitudini Solis apparenti aequalis, problema resolutum erit. Si differen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L040}{}{}{tia ± \textgreek{Δ} exstiterit, motus horarius relativus apparens Solis et Lunae (« motus aequatus ») sup-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L041}{}{}{putetur ut supra docuimus; per eum dividatur \textgreek{Δ}: tempus ex divisione proveniens iuxta suum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L042}{}{}{signum applicetur ad \textgreek{ϑ} + \textgreek{ε}²; exibit tempus coniunctionis apparentis. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0368-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L043}{}{}{\Indent Ratio qua \Name{al–Battānī} operatur a ratione vetustiorum aliquantum differt. Iuvat quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L044}{}{}{\Name{al–Khāzinī}, \textit{az–zīǵ as–sanǵarī,} fol. 80,r.–81,r. (tractat. VIII, pars II, cap. II « de corrigendis}
\par\vspace{0.17mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L045}{}{}{horis medii eclipsis ») narrat referre.}
\par\vspace{-0.17mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0368-P08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0368-body-L046}{}{}{\Indent « \textit{Methodus prima,} iuxta \Name{Ptolemaeum}. — Dicit: Cognoscamus distantiam horarum}
\DSource{0369}{280}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L001}{}{}{« verae coniunctionis a meridie, altitudinem Lunae, parallaxim Lunae in circulo altitudinis.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L002}{}{}{« Ab hac parallaxe parallaxim Solis demamus; restat parallaxis apparens. Per has horas veras}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L003}{}{}{« angulum longitudinis deprehendamus, et ab eo parallaxim in longitudine, quam \textit{primam pa-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L004}{}{}{« \textit{rallaxim} vocabimus. Eam per praecessionem veram Lunae dividamus: exibunt \textit{horae pri-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L005}{}{}{« \textit{mae parallaxis,} quas dememus ab horis verae coniunctionis si Luna minus 90° ab ascen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L006}{}{}{« dente distaverit, secus addemus. Prodibunt \textit{horae secundae coniunctionis}. Iterum per has}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L007}{}{}{« horas deprehendamus altitudinem Lunae, parallaxim apparentem, angulum longitudinis, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L008}{}{}{« parallaxim longitudinis quam \textit{secundam parallaxim} vocabimus. Si aequalis fuerit primae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L009}{}{}{« parallaxi, horae secundae coniunctionis erunt ipsae horae medii eclipsis; si diversa, eam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L010}{}{}{« aequare nobis opus erit. Aequatio haec est: Cognoscamus differentiam inter duas parallaxes}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L011}{}{}{« illas; postea proportionem resolvendo}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0369-H12}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L012}{}{}{\CenterLine{« quantitas incognita : differentia $=$ differentia : parallaxis minor,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L013}{}{}{« nempe quadratum differentiae per minorem parallaxim dividendo, inveniemus quantitatem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L014}{}{}{« incognitam (\textit{al–ǵuz’ al–maǵhūl}). Hanc addamus differentiae, si parallaxis secunda fuerit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L015}{}{}{« maior prima, aut de differentia demamus, si prima maior; exibit differentia aequata, quae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L016}{}{}{« addita primae parallaxis longitudinis, \textit{parallaxim aequatam} efficiet. Hanc per veram}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L017}{}{}{« praecessionem Lunae dividamus; exibunt \textit{horae parallaxis apparentis}. Eas demamus ab}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L018}{}{}{« horis coniunctionis verae si Luna ab ascendente minus 90° distaverit, secus addamus; pro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L019}{}{}{« dibunt \textit{horae aequatae praecisae (murattabah) medii eclipsis}. — In his determinemus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L020}{}{}{« veros locos amborum luminarium et nodi, anomaliam Lunae, Lunae altitudinem, parallaxim}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L021}{}{}{« lunarem latitudinis eiusque plagam; a loco Lunae apparenti et a nodo cognoscemus latitu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L022}{}{}{« dinem veram Lunae, et deprehendemus eius latitudinem correctam.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0369-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L023}{}{}{\Indent « \textit{Methodus secunda,} e traditione accepta (\textit{‘amaluhu mursalan}). — Dicunt \Name{Thābit} (1),}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L024}{}{}{« auctores \textit{Tabulae Probatae} (2), et \Name{Indi}: Cognoscamus distantiam horarum coniunctionis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L025}{}{}{« verae a meridie, et parallaxim Lunae in longitudine; parallaxim per veram praecessionem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L026}{}{}{« Lunae dividamus, exibunt \textit{horae primae parallaxis}. Si Luna ab ascendente minus 90° di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L027}{}{}{« staverit, eas ab horis verae coniunctionis demamus ut exeant \textit{horae secundae coniunctionis};}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L028}{}{}{« item primam parallaxim longitudinis de gradu coniunctionis demamus, et proveniet \textit{locus}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L029}{}{}{« \textit{apparens Lunae}. Si contra amplius 90° distaverit Luna ab ascendente, horas primae pa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L030}{}{}{« rallaxis addemus horis coniunctionis verae ut exeant \textit{horae secundae coniunctionis,} at-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L031}{}{}{« que primam parallaxim adiiciemus gradui coniunctionis ut prodeat \textit{locus apparens Lunae}.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L032}{}{}{« — Postea his horis utendo rursus parallaxim longitudinis supputemus; si fuerit aequalis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L033}{}{}{« primae parallaxi, horae secundae coniunctionis erunt ipsae horae medii eclipsis, et Luna}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L034}{}{}{« apparens erit Luna medii eclipsis. Si autem duae illae parallaxes fuerint diversae, secundam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L035}{}{}{« per praecessionem veram Lunae dividamus; exibunt \textit{horae secundae parallaxis}. Eas ad-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L036}{}{}{« damus horis coniunctionis verae, parallaxim autem gradui coniunctionis, aut demamus ut su-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L037}{}{}{« pra diximus. Provenient \textit{horae tertiae coniunctionis}. Si haec fuerit aequalis secundae, erit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L038}{}{}{« ipsa quod quaeritur; si differens, quartum et quintum horas coniunctionis supputabimus, nec}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L039}{}{}{« desinemus calculos hoc modo pluries iterare usque quo numerum invenerimus numero prae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L040}{}{}{« cedenti aequalem. Horae quae postremo exibunt erunt \textit{horae medii eclipsis,} et Luna appa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L041}{}{}{« rens ipsa erit quae quaerebatur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0369-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L042}{}{}{\Indent « \textit{Methodus tertia,} iuxta \Name{al–Battānī}. — Dicit: Distantiam inter horas coniunctionis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-body-L043}{}{}{« verae et meridiem sumamus, atque parallaxim longitudinis aequatam investigemus, quae}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0369-notes_right-L002}{}{}{diderunt.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0370}{281}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L001}{}{}{« erit \textit{prima parallaxis}. Eam per praecessionem veram Lunae dividamus; provenient \textit{horae}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L002}{}{}{« \textit{primae parallaxis}. Si Luna minus 90° ab ascendente distaverit, eas ab horis verae coniun-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L003}{}{}{« ctionis, et primam parallaxim a gradu [eiusdem coniunctionis] dememus; si magis 90° di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L004}{}{}{« staverit, ambas res addemus; provenient \textit{horae secundae coniunctionis,} et \textit{locus apparens}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L005}{}{}{« \textit{Lunae}. Per has quantitates iterum parallaxim longitudinis cognoscamus; postea ut supra}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L006}{}{}{« operemur eam per praecessionem Lunae dividendo, et additione et subductione deinde}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L007}{}{}{« utendo. Si secunda parallaxis ipsa fuerit aequalis primae, et \textit{horae tertiae coniunctionis}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L008}{}{}{« aequales horis secundae, erunt hae horae medii eclipsis, atque Luna apparens erit quae quae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L009}{}{}{« rebatur. At si secunda parallaxis fuerit a prima diversa, differentiam inter eas (1) dividamus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L010}{}{}{« per praecessionem \textit{apparentem in ea hora} (2), \textit{et non per praecessionem veram} (\ArabicRun{وقسمناه}}
\par\vspace{0.29mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L011}{}{}{« \ArabicRun{على السبق المرئي لتلك الساعة خاصة دون سبقه الحقيقي}); quod proveniet erit fractio horae. Si secunda}
\par\vspace{-0.29mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L012}{}{}{« parallaxis fuerit maior prima, fractionem addemus horis secundae parallaxis ut fiant \textit{ae-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L013}{}{}{« \textit{quatae}; si autem secunda fuerit minor prima parallaxe, fractionem dememus, inveniemusque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L014}{}{}{« \textit{horas aequatas}. Postea si gradus coniunctionis ab ascendente minus 90° distaverit, has ho-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L015}{}{}{« ras aequatas de horis verae coniunctionis dememus; si distantia maior 90°, horas aequatas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L016}{}{}{« addemus. Quod a subductione aut additione proveniet erunt \textit{horae medii eclipsis}. — Postea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L017}{}{}{« has horas aequatas multiplicemus in motus Solis, Lunae, nodi, anomaliae Lunae; producta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L018}{}{}{« addamus locis correspondentibus tempore coniunctionis, si horae aequatae erant additivae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L019}{}{}{« aut demamus si horae erant subtractivae; nodo tantum excepto, cum quo operandum est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L020}{}{}{« contrarie (3). Exibunt \textit{loca praecisa} (\textit{al–murattabah}) ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0370-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L021}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Ḥabash}, f. 133,r.–134,v., methodum Ptolemaicam sequitur.}
\par\vspace{2.60mm}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0370-P03}{}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L025}{}{}{terminorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L026}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{28.83mm}\llap{maximus}}\rlap{\hspace*{30.32mm}$=$}\rlap{\hspace*{35.95mm}max.}\rlap{\hspace*{46.72mm}semidiam.}\rlap{\hspace*{66.93mm}\SunSym{}}\rlap{\hspace*{72.70mm}+}\rlap{\hspace*{78.77mm}max.}\rlap{\hspace*{89.10mm}semid.}\rlap{\hspace*{102.17mm}\MoonSym{}}\rlap{\hspace*{106.52mm}$=$}\rlap{\hspace*{112.46mm}$\qfrac{1}{2}$}\rlap{\hspace*{118.04mm}(33′}\rlap{\hspace*{126.46mm}40″}\rlap{\hspace*{134.66mm}+}\rlap{\hspace*{140.60mm}35′}\rlap{\hspace*{147.61mm}20″)}\rlap{\hspace*{157.49mm}$=$}\rlap{\hspace*{163.12mm}34′}\rlap{\hspace*{170.88mm}30″}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L027}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{28.95mm}\llap{minimus}}\rlap{\hspace*{30.41mm}$=$}\rlap{\hspace*{36.39mm}min.}\rlap{\hspace*{45.61mm}semidiam.}\rlap{\hspace*{65.83mm}\SunSym{}}\rlap{\hspace*{71.59mm}+}\rlap{\hspace*{77.66mm}min.}\rlap{\hspace*{86.92mm}semid.}\rlap{\hspace*{100.00mm}\MoonSym{}}\rlap{\hspace*{104.39mm}$=$}\rlap{\hspace*{110.11mm}$\qfrac{1}{2}$}\rlap{\hspace*{115.74mm}(31′}\rlap{\hspace*{124.16mm}20″}\rlap{\hspace*{132.45mm}+}\rlap{\hspace*{138.39mm}29′}\rlap{\hspace*{145.52mm}30″)}\rlap{\hspace*{155.14mm}$=$}\rlap{\hspace*{160.91mm}30′}\rlap{\hspace*{168.00mm}25″.}}
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L028}{}{}{Diametri Albatenianae Solis et Lunae sunt p. 58. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{-0.04mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0370-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L029}{}{}{\Indent Hunc locum fusius exponunt \textit{Tabulae Alphonsinae}: « Est ergo regula \Name{Albategni} de}
\par\vspace{-0.04mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L030}{}{}{« necessitate eclipsis Solis talis: quod inveniantur semidiameter Solis et semidiameter Lunae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L031}{}{}{« tempore verissimae coniunctionis: et iungentur simul. Et ad idem tempus inveniatur latitudo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L032}{}{}{« Lunae visa. et tunc si latitudo Lunae visa fuerit aequalis aggregato ex semidiametris Solis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L033}{}{}{« et Lunae: transibit Luna prope Solem: et non eclipsabitur Sol. Et si latitudo Lunae fuerit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L034}{}{}{« maior: impossibile est fieri eclipsim Solis. Sed si latitudo fuerit minor quam aggregatum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L035}{}{}{« ex semidiametris: necessario fit eclipsis Solis » (\textit{Tabulae astronomicae Divi} \Name{Alphonsi}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L036}{}{}{\textit{Regis Romanorum et Castellae, nuper quam diligentissime cum additionibus emendatae,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0370-body-L037}{}{}{Venetiis 1518, fol. 19,r., prop. XXXII).}
\par\vspace{2.64mm}

\par\vspace{5mm}\hfill{\fontsize{8}{10}\selectfont 36}\hspace{10mm}
\DSource{0371}{282}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L001}{}{}{supputandi diametrum apparentem Solis, sed ad eam nihil pertinent quae nunc in cod. et apud}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L002}{}{}{Platonem scripta leguntur. Quid in vero textu \Name{al–Battānī} esset, et qualis nova ratio, for-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L003}{}{}{tasse coniici potest e pag. 58, l. 31 et seqq., ubi auctor animadvertit diametros apparentes}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L004}{}{}{Solis esse proportionales ad velocitatem motus zodiacalis horarii. Hic motus est in tabella}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L005}{}{}{t. II, p. 88. Regula igitur haec erat: « Minimam diametrum apparentem Solis (31′ 20″) multi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L006}{}{}{« plica in motum horarium inaequalem Solis qui datae anomaliae Solis respondeat; productum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L007}{}{}{« per minimum motum horarium 2′ 23″ divide ». — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\fontsize{15}{17}\selectfont

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0371-H09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L009}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{a}}) — \textit{Pag. 115, l. 15 et 19.}}}
\par\vspace{6.01mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0371-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L010}{}{}{\Indent « \textit{Columna latitudinis borealis, afīǵiyūn septentrionum appellata} ..... \textit{Columna lati-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L011}{}{}{« \textit{tudinis australis, ferīǵiyūn austri appellata} ». — Plato: « \textit{effregion septentrionalis} »,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L012}{}{}{« \textit{effregio meridiana} »; item in tabula latitudinum planetarum superiorum, t. II, p. 140, quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L013}{}{}{ex \textit{Almagesto} ed. Halma t. II, p. 412–13 est sumpta, ad marginem ultimae columnae scrip-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L014}{}{}{tum legitur: \ArabicRun{افيثخون هو الشمال فرنجيون هو الجنوب} « \textgreek{ἀπόγειον} est septentriones, \textgreek{περίγειον} auster ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0371-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L015}{}{}{\Indent Quo modo Graeca haec vocabula ad plagas latitudinum superiorum planetarum pertineant,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L016}{}{}{primo aspectu non liquet. In tabula \Name{Ptolemaei} columna latitudinis borealis \textgreek{βόρειον πέρας}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L017}{}{}{« terminus borealis » inscribitur, columna latitudinis australis \textgreek{νότιον πέρας} « terminus austra-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L018}{}{}{« lis ». Sed in versione Latina Almagesti quam \Name{Gherardus Cremonensis} ex Arabico}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L019}{}{}{fecit (1), prior columna « \textit{Efegion–Septentrio} », posterior « \textit{Efregion–Meridies} » inscribitur.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L020}{}{}{Haec igitur ab Arabicis Almagesti exemplaribus \Name{al–Battānī} mutuatus est. Talia nomina ex}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L021}{}{}{eo orta puto, quod in Ptolemaei systemate latitudo trium superiorum planetarum borealis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L022}{}{}{fit quotiescumque centrum epicycli est in illo excentrici segmento in quo reperitur apogeum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L023}{}{}{excentrici; australis quando est in illo in quo perigeum consistit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0371-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent Eadem Graeca vocabula, in scriptura Arabica haud paucum deturpata, invenio apud \Name{al–}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L025}{}{}{\Name{-Khāzinī}, \textit{az–zīǵ as–sanǵarī,} at sensu contrario usui Albateniano. Nam (cod. Vatic., f. 167}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L026}{}{}{pro Saturno, 178 pro Iove, 176 pro Marte) in tabulis latitudinum planetarum superiorum}
\par\vspace{-0.13mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L027}{}{}{hos titulos semper habet: \ArabicRun{افرنجيون زحل وهو صاعد في الشمال} « \textgreek{περίγειον} Saturni [aut Iovis, aut Martis],}
\par\vspace{0.41mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L028}{}{}{« et id [est] ascendens in plaga boreali »; \ArabicRun{افحون زحل وهو هابط في الجنوب} « \textgreek{ἀπόγειον} Saturni, et}
\par\vspace{0.21mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L029}{}{}{« id [est] descendens in plaga australi ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0371-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L030}{}{}{\Indent E Platonis versione puto ea vocabula irrepsisse in librum Iohannis \Name{Schoneri} (2), qui}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L031}{}{}{\textit{Tabulae resolutae astronomicae} (Witenbergae 1587) inscribitur. Ibi enim, p. 27, haec inve-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L032}{}{}{nio: « et quod in communi angulo inveneris de latitudine septentrionali aut meridionali, extra}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L033}{}{}{« notabis cum parte sua latitudinis aut septentrionali, quae \textit{efregion septentrionale} dicitur,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L034}{}{}{« aut meridionali, quae \textit{efregion meridionale} nominatur, et sicuti tituli in capitibus tabula-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L035}{}{}{« rum admonent ». Qua re in tabulis latitudinum planetarum superiorum, columna latitudinis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0371-body-L036}{}{}{borealis « \textit{effregion septentrionale} » inscribitur, latitudinis australis « \textit{effregion meridionale} ».}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}

\par\vspace{4.35mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0372-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L002}{}{}{\Indent I. — « \textit{Ob eorum velocem circa Solem cursum} ».— Similiter infra, p. 117, l: 11. Pla-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L003}{}{}{netae inferiores a \Name{Platone Atheniensi} \textgreek{ἰσοδρόμοι ἡλίῳ} « cursum Solis aequantes » appel-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L004}{}{}{lantur; a \Name{Ptolemaeo} IX, 1 (ed. Halma t. II, p. 115, ubi versio parum bona), \textgreek{περὶ αὐτὸν ἀεὶ}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L005}{}{}{\textgreek{φερόμενοι} « circa eum [scil. Solem] semper ducti ». His et similibus scriptorum Graecorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L006}{}{}{Latinorumque locutionibus nonnisi apparentem Veneris et Mercurii motum circa Solem, dex-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L007}{}{}{trorsum et sinistrorsum, indicari, doctissime ostendit G. \Name{Schiaparelli}, \textit{I precursori di Co-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L008}{}{}{\textit{pernico nell’antichità,} Milano 1873. Cfr. quoque \Name{Hultzsch}, s. v. \textit{Astronomie,} in \Name{Pauly}–}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L009}{}{}{\Name{Wissowa}, \textit{Real–Encyclopädie der classischen Altertumswissenschaft,} t. II, col. 1859.}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0372-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L010}{}{}{\Indent Item XIII saec. Chr. \Name{al–Qazwīnī}, \textit{Cosmographia,} tract. I, cap. III (t. I, p. 34 in edit.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L011}{}{}{Kahirensi anni 1311 heg.) Mercurium dicit « multas habere retrogradationes et praegressio-}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L012}{}{}{« nes, atque \textit{circa} Solem revolvi \ArabicRun{يدور حول الشمس} ».}
\par\vspace{-0.14mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0372-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L013}{}{}{\Indent II. — Scribit \Name{Ibn Yūnus}, introd. (\Name{Caussin} p. 70): « [Ex erroribus] quoque sunt quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L014}{}{}{« speciatim evenerunt Muḥammadi ibn Ǵābir ibn Sinān \Name{al–Battānī} in plaga (1) latitudinis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L015}{}{}{« Mercurii [statuenda] ». Errorem non invenio; praecepta p. 115–16 et tabulae non differunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L016}{}{}{a quae Ptolemaeus habet. Fortasse Ibn Yūnus exemplar operis Albateniani hoc loco mendo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L017}{}{}{sum prae manibus habebat, ut ipse cod. Escurialensis (non vero Plato) p. 116.}
\par\fontsize{15}{17}\selectfont
\par\vspace{8.42mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0372-H18}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L018}{}{}{\CenterLine{A\textsc{d} \textsc{caput} XLVIII.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.88mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0372-H19}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L019}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{a}}) — \textit{Pag. 117, l. 28-29.}}}
\par\vspace{4.43mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0372-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L020}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Arcus « aequatoriales » apparitionum} planetarum.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0372-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L021}{}{}{\Indent Iuxta \Name{al–Battānī} hos vere esse \textit{aequatoris} arcus, patet etiam e sequentibus praeceptis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L022}{}{}{ubi arcus comparandi dicuntur cum arcu ascensionis rectae (« temporibus occasuum vel ascen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L023}{}{}{sionum ») respondente differentiae longitudinis inter Solem et planetam datum. Idemque e fine}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L024}{}{}{XLIX capitis, p. 120, apparet.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0372-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L025}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Ptolemaeus} contra arcus \textit{eclipticae,} scilicet ipsam differentiam longitudinis delegit (2):}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L026}{}{}{« Ex ea consideratione observationum ortuum planetarum invenimus prope (\textgreek{περί}) initium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L027}{}{}{« Cancri in universum (\textgreek{ὡς ἐπίπαν}) Saturnum apparere (\textgreek{ἀνατέλλει}) cum a Sole vero 14° distet,}
\par\vspace{0.35mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L028}{}{}{« Iovem 12° $\qfrac{1}{2}$ + $\qfrac{1}{4}$, Martem 14° $\qfrac{1}{2}$, Venerem vespere 5° $\qfrac{2}{3}$, Mercurium vespere 11° $\qfrac{1}{2}$ »}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L029}{}{}{(\textit{Almag.} XIII, 7, ed. Halma t. II, p. 418–19). At cum hos apparitionum limites Ptolemaeus}
\par\vspace{-0.14mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L030}{}{}{prope initium Cancri definivisset, ubi aequator et ecliptica fere sunt paralleli et ascensiones}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L031}{}{}{rectae paulo ab arcubus correspondentibus zodiaci diversae, absque sensibili errore potuit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-body-L032}{}{}{al–Battānī numeros limites Ptolemaicos in aequatorem transferre.}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.72}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.09mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0372-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) \Name{Caussin}: « s’est trompé sur la latitude »;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-notes_left-L002}{}{}{vocis \ArabicRun{جهة} significationem technicam non agnovit,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-notes_left-L003}{}{}{qua re \ArabicRun{في جهة} « in plaga » male interpretatus est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-notes_left-L004}{}{}{« quoad, quod attinet ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0372-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (2) In \textit{Almag.} XIII, 7 (ed. Halma t. II, p. 420-2)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-notes_left-L006}{}{}{supputavit quoque minimam depressionem Solis sub}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-notes_left-L007}{}{}{horizonte necessaria ut hi ortus et occasus heliaci}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-notes_left-L008}{}{}{planetarum fieri possint; invenit pro Saturno 11°,}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.72}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.06mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-notes_right-L001}{}{}{pro Iove 10°, pro Marte 11° $\qfrac{1}{2}$, pro Venere 5°, pro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-notes_right-L002}{}{}{Mercurio 10°. Cfr. R. \Name{Wolf}, \textit{Handbuch der Astro-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\textit{nomie,} Zürich 1890-93, t. I, p. 423-24, § 191,\textit{c}, qui}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-notes_right-L004}{}{}{miro modo se hos numeros in Almagesto non repe-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-notes_right-L005}{}{}{risse fatetur; quae in Almagesto desunt sunt quan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-notes_right-L006}{}{}{titates arcuum stellarum fixarum (cfr. adn. \textbf{\textit{b}} ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0372-notes_right-L007}{}{}{cap. XLIX).}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0373}{284}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 0.23mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0373-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent Iure \Name{al–Battānī} maluit elementa calculi in arcubus aequatoris supputare; nam, si ar-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L002}{}{}{cus essent eclipticae, in eodem climate haud paulum in diversis anni partibus mutarentur ob}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L003}{}{}{variam inclinationem eclipticae respectu horizontis (1).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0373-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L004}{}{}{\Indent Arcum apparitionis \Name{al–Battānī} tantum p. 118, l. 14–28, in \textit{ecliptica} numerat; nam tunc}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L005}{}{}{usum tabularum apparitionum et occultationum planetarum (t. II, p. 142–43) explicat, quas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L006}{}{}{pro latitudine 36° 0′ urbis ar–Raqqah construxit, ad similitudinem tabularum \Name{Ptolemaei},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L007}{}{}{in eorum usum qui calculum directum (meliorem tamen) vitare studeant.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0373-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L008}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Ḥabash}, f. 67,v.–70,v. (tabulae f. 73,r.–76,v.), et \Name{Oloug Beg} p. 142 text., 192 vers.,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L009}{}{}{arcus eclipticae semper considerant. — \Name{Barhebraeus} p. 79–80 text., de depressione Solis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L010}{}{}{sub horizonte (quae in circulo verticali Solis numeratur) iuxta Ptolemaeum loquitur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0373-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L011}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Indi} limites visionis planetae in aequatore, ut al–Battānī, statuerunt; sed eorum numeri}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L012}{}{}{a Graecis et Arabicis differunt; v. \textit{Sūrya–Siddhānta,} vers. Burgess–Whitney, IX, 4–9, p. 365–}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L013}{}{}{–67, et \Name{al–Bīrūnī}, \textit{India,} cap. LVII, p. 246–47 text., t. II, p. 90–92 vers.}
\par\fontsize{15}{17}\selectfont
\par\vspace{9.28mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0373-H14}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L014}{}{}{\CenterLine{A\textsc{d} \textsc{caput} XLIX.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.87mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0373-H15}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L015}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{a}}) — \textit{Pag. 119, l. 3–41.}}}
\par\vspace{4.40mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0373-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L016}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Configurationes stellarum fixarum} cum Sole.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0373-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L017}{}{}{\Indent Vocibus \textit{tashrīq} et \textit{taghrīb} astronomi Arabici plerumque \textit{ortum} et \textit{occasum heliacum}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L018}{}{}{stellarum designant, ut e definitione lexici \textit{Mafātīḥ al–‘ulūm} apparet: « \textit{Tashrīq} fit cum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L019}{}{}{« stella in plaga orientali ascendere cernatur ante Solis ortum; \textit{taghrīb} cum in plaga occi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L020}{}{}{« dentali post occasum Solis occidere conspiciatur » (2). At haec definitio non semper cum usu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L021}{}{}{Albateniano in hoc capite congruit; qua re malui voces \textit{ortum anteriorem} et \textit{occasum an-}}
\par\vspace{-0.06mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L022}{}{}{\textit{teriorem} adhibere.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0373-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L023}{}{}{\Indent Quae in hoc capite describuntur sunt novem genera (\textgreek{τρόποι}) configurationum (\textgreek{σχηματισμοί})}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L024}{}{}{stellarum fixarum respectu Solis, iam descripta a \Name{Ptolemaeo} VIII, 4 (ed. Halma t. II, p. 100–}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L025}{}{}{–104), cuius nomina Graeca, cum versione Halma, referre placet ut cum Albatenianis com-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L026}{}{}{parentur:}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0373-P08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L027}{}{}{\Indent 1. \textgreek{πρωϊνὸς ἀπηλιώτης} subsolaire du matin (vent d’est), tribus modis: \textgreek{ἐπανατολή} lever sub-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L028}{}{}{sequent, \textgreek{συνανατολὴ ἀληθινή} lever simultané vrai, \textgreek{προανατολή} lever antérieur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0373-P09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L029}{}{}{\Indent 2. \textgreek{πρωϊνὸν μεσουράνημα} culmination matutinale; quae esse potest: \textgreek{ἑῷον συμμεσουράνημα μὴ}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L030}{}{}{\textgreek{φαινόμενον} culmination simultanée non apparente, \textgreek{ἑῷον συμμεσουράνημα ἀληθινόν} vrai, \textgreek{ἑῷον προ}-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L031}{}{}{\textgreek{μεσουράνημα} culmination matutinale antérieure.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0373-P10}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L032}{}{}{\Indent 3. \textgreek{πρωϊνὸς λίψ} coucher du matin (du vent d’ouest); cuius species: \textgreek{ἑῷα ἐπικατάδυσις} cou-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L033}{}{}{cher matutinal postérieur, \textgreek{ἑῷα συγκατάδυσις ἀληθινή} c. m. simultané vrai, \textgreek{ἑῷα πρόδυσις} c. m.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L034}{}{}{antérieur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0373-P11}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L035}{}{}{\Indent 4. \textgreek{μεσημβρινὸς ἀπηλιώτης} lever méridional oriental; qui potest esse: \textgreek{ἡμερινός} diurne, et \textgreek{νυκ}-}
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L036}{}{}{\textgreek{τερινός} nocturne.}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0373-P12}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-body-L037}{}{}{\Indent 5. \textgreek{μεσημβρινὸν μεσουράνημα} culmination méridienne.}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.68}\selectfont
\par\vspace{4.01mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0373-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) [Animadvertendum tamen est arcus illos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-notes_left-L002}{}{}{tam aequatoris quam eclipticae diversos esse pro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-notes_left-L003}{}{}{diversis Terrae latitudinibus. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0373-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Indent (2) \textit{Liber Mafâtih al-‘olûm, auctore} \Name{Abû}}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.51}\selectfont
\par\vspace{4.18mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Name{‘Abdallâh Mohammed al-Khowârezmî},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-notes_right-L002}{}{}{edidit G. van Vloten, Lugduni Batavorum 1895,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-notes_right-L003}{}{}{p. 229-230. — Liber intra annos 363 et 381 heg.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0373-notes_right-L004}{}{}{(973 et 991 Chr.) confectus.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0374}{285}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 0.08mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0374-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent 6. \textgreek{μεσημβρινὸς λίψ} coucher méridional–oriental.}
\par\vspace{-0.04mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0374-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L002}{}{}{\Indent 7. \textgreek{ὀψινὸς ἀπηλιώτης} lever du soir; cuius tres species: \textgreek{ἑσπερία ἐπανατολὴ φαινομένη} lever}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L003}{}{}{du soir postérieur et visible; \textgreek{ἑσπερία συνανατολὴ ἀληθινή} l. d. s. simultané vrai, \textgreek{ἑσπερία προα}-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L004}{}{}{\textgreek{νατολὴ μὴ φαινομένη} l. d. s. antérieur et non visible.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0374-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L005}{}{}{\Indent 8. \textgreek{ὀψινὸν μεσουράνημα} culmination du soir; cuius species: \textgreek{ἑσπερινὸν ἐπιμεσουράνημα φαι}-}
\par\vspace{0.06mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L006}{}{}{\textgreek{νόμενον} culmination postérieure du soir, \textgreek{ἑσπερινὸν συμμεσουράνημα ἀληθινόν} culm. vraie simul-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L007}{}{}{tanée du soir, \textgreek{ἑσπερινὸν προμεσουράνημα μὴ φαινόμενον} culm. antérieure du soir non visible.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0374-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L008}{}{}{\Indent 9. \textgreek{ὀψινὸς λίψ} coucher occidental du soir; cuius tres species: \textgreek{ἑσπερία ἐπικατάδυσις φαινομένη}}
\par\vspace{0.04mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L009}{}{}{coucher du soir postérieur et visible, \textgreek{ἑσπερία συγκατάδυσις ἀληθινή} c. d. s. simultané et vrai,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L010}{}{}{\textgreek{ἑσπερία πρόδυσις μὴ φαινομένη} c. d. s. antérieur et invisible.}
\par\vspace{5.22mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0374-H11}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L011}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{b}}) — \textit{Pag. 120, l. 1–7.}}}
\par\vspace{4.47mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0374-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L012}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Arcus aequatoriales apparitionis} stellarum fixarum, iuxta traditionem communiter re-}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L013}{}{}{ceptam (« dicunt »).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0374-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L014}{}{}{\Indent Hi arcus in circulo aequinoctiali supputantur, ut iam p. 283 ostendi. — \Name{Ptolemaeus},}
\par\vspace{-0.05mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L015}{}{}{\textit{Almag.} VIII, 6 (ed. Halma t. II, p. 108–113), de apparitionibus et occultationibus fixarum dis-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L016}{}{}{serit; solutionem problematis perdifficilem esse ostendit, at nullam eorum arcuum quantitatem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L017}{}{}{praebet. Recte L. \Name{Ideler}, \textit{Handbuch der mathematischen und technischen Chronologie,}}
\par\vspace{-0.04mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L018}{}{}{Berlin 1825–26, t. I, p. 55, ait nullo fundamento niti quae in multis astronomicis libris cir-}
\par\vspace{0.04mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L019}{}{}{cumferuntur, \Name{Ptolemaeum} scilicet determinavisse arcum apparitionis stellarum primae ma-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L020}{}{}{gnitudinis 12°, secundae 13°, tertiae 14°, quartae 15°, quintae 16°, sextae demum (quae con-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L021}{}{}{spiciuntur cum omnino desit crepusculum) 18°. Huiusmodi nihil in libris \Name{Ptolemaei} notis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L022}{}{}{reperitur. Praeterea animadvertendum est hos arcus esse \textit{depressionem} Solis sub horizonte,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L023}{}{}{quae in \textit{circulo verticali} supputatur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0374-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Ptolemaeus} ait se opusculum conscripsisse in quo arcus apparitionis stellarum fixarum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L025}{}{}{tam in circulo verticali quam in ecliptica determinavisset. Libellus ad nos non pervenit; ta-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L026}{}{}{men L. \Name{Ideler}, \textit{Ueber den Kalender des Ptolemaeus} (1), a notitiis contentis libro Ptole-}
\par\vspace{-0.04mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L027}{}{}{maico \textit{de apparitionibus fixarum} (\textgreek{φάσεις ἀπλανῶν ἀστέρων}) deduxit astronomum Graecum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L028}{}{}{depressionem Solis 11° et 7° supputasse pro stellis primae magnitudinis, 14° et 8° $\qfrac{1}{2}$ pro stellis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L029}{}{}{secundae magnitudinis; numeros 11 et 14 valere cum stella et Sol in eadem horizontis plaga}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L030}{}{}{ascendant aut occidant, 7 et 8 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ cum in duabus plagis oppositis sint.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0374-P08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L031}{}{}{\Indent Numerus 15° in stellis primae magnitudinis fortasse provenit a libro \Name{Autolyci} \textit{de or-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L032}{}{}{\textit{tibus et occasibus} (\textgreek{περὶ ἐπιτολῶν καὶ δύσεων}), aetate al–Battānī iam Arabibus notissimo; nam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L033}{}{}{\Name{Qosṭā ibn Lūqā} eum Arabice verterat, et \Name{Thābit ibn Qurrah} emendaverat. \Name{Autoly-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L034}{}{}{\Name{cus} (IV saec. ante Chr.) tamen arcum apparitionis in \textit{ecliptica} numerat, et pro stellis fixis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L035}{}{}{omnibus 15° statuit (2).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0374-P09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L036}{}{}{\Indent Sed qua ratione statuerint arcus aequatoriales apparitionis ordinatim tribus gradibus cre-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-body-L037}{}{}{scere, ita ut in stellis sextae magnitudinis 30° fere sint, non liquet. [Probabile est haec omnia}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{1.36mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0374-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) In Schriften der k. Akademie der Wissen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-notes_left-L002}{}{}{schaften zu Berlin, 1816 et 1817. — Versio Gallica}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-notes_left-L003}{}{}{exstat in calce operis \textit{Table chronologique des rè-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\textit{gnes; apparition des étoiles fixes de} C. \Name{Ptolé-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Name{mée}, \Name{Théon} etc., publiées et traduites par Halma,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-notes_left-L006}{}{}{Paris 1819.}

\par\vspace{1.25mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-notes_right-L001}{}{}{Mémoires de la Société des sciences physiques et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-notes_right-L002}{}{}{naturelles de Bordeaux, III série, t. II, fasc. 1, 1885,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-notes_right-L003}{}{}{p. 173-199. — \Name{Eudoxus} in \textit{Parapegmate} arcus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-notes_right-L004}{}{}{differentes praebet, qui a 20° $\qfrac{1}{4}$ vel 21° (Pleiadum,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-notes_right-L005}{}{}{Hyadum, Orionis, Canis) ad 8° $\qfrac{3}{4}$ (Delphini) variant;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0374-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\Name{Tannery}, l. l., p. 190-191.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0375}{286}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 0.08mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L001}{}{}{a rudibus calculis et observationibus provenire; scilicet Arabes ante al–Battānī statuisse si-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L002}{}{}{dera primae magnitudinis apparere 1\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} ($=$ 15°) ante ortum Solis et 1\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} post Solis occasum, sidera}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L003}{}{}{autem sextae magnitudinis 2\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} ($=$ 30°) ante ortum et post occasum Solis. Intra hos limites}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L004}{}{}{reliqui ordines magnitudinis stellarum collocari ordinatim. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0375-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L005}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Oloug Beg} p. 143 text., 193 vers., \textit{depressionem} Solis sub horizonte considerat; eam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L006}{}{}{12° circiter pro stellis primae magnitudinis supputat, 14° pro stellis secundae, et hoc modo,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L007}{}{}{cum regulari incremento 2° in subsequentibus ordinibus, 22° pro stellis sextae magnitudinis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L008}{}{}{statuit. Addit hoc verum esse in stellis quae prope eclipticam sunt (1).}
\par\fontsize{15}{17}\selectfont
\par\vspace{9.39mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0375-H09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L009}{}{}{\CenterLine{A\textsc{d} \textsc{caput} L.}}

\par\vspace{3.76mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0375-H10}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L010}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{a}}) — \textit{Pag. 120, l. 18.}}}
\par\vspace{4.51mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0375-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L011}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Distantiae planetarum a Terra} « iuxta ea quae recentiores sapientes \textit{post} Ptolemaeum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L012}{}{}{« longo labore statuerunt ».}
\par\vspace{-0.04mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0375-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L013}{}{}{\Indent Codex, ut suo loco monui, « ante » habet, quod cum verbis « recentiores sapientes » male}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L014}{}{}{componitur. Dubitatio autem facile dirimitur si consideremus fundamenta omnium horum cal-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L015}{}{}{culorum esse excentricitates et magnitudines epicyclorum a \Name{Ptolemaeo} definitas, ut ex}
\par\vspace{0.05mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L016}{}{}{adn. \textbf{\textit{b}} patebit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0375-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L017}{}{}{\Indent Haec distantiarum theoria nititur principio sphaeras septem planetarum contiguas esse et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L018}{}{}{nullo intervallo ab alteris disiunctas; qua re minimam distantiam planetae superioris aequari}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L019}{}{}{maximae planetae statim inferioris. Quod principium ab omnibus mihi notis Arabicis astro-}
\par\vspace{0.04mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L020}{}{}{nomis receptum est; ipso XV saec. Chr. apud Persicum scriptorem \Name{‘Alī Qūshǵī} invenitur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L021}{}{}{(ap. \Name{Delambre} p. 198). Disputatio philosophica est in epistulis quas X saec. Chr. in urbe}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L022}{}{}{al–Baṣrah de diversis scientiis scripserunt viri \textit{Ikhwān aṣ–ṣafā’} « socii fideles » appellati,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L023}{}{}{quorum doctrina tam ab Aristotele quam a Neopythagoricis et Neoplatonicis proveniebat (2).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0375-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent Iam ante \Name{al–Battānī} haec narrat \Name{al–Farghānī} cap. XXI, p. 80 ed. Golii: « \Name{Ptole-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L025}{}{}{« \Name{maeus} quidem in libro suo exposuit quanta sit distantia duntaxat Solis et Lunae; neque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L026}{}{}{« comperimus quicquam ab illo proditum de distantiis siderum reliquorum, nisi quod docuerit}
\par\vspace{-0.05mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L027}{}{}{« quantum dissideant centra orbium a centro Terrae, quantique sint orbes epicycli. — Quod}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L028}{}{}{« igitur si ponamus distantiam Lunae, respectu utriusque simul orbis (excentrici et epicycli),}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L029}{}{}{« remotissimam esse eandem cum distantia Mercurii proxima, et eadem ratione utamur ut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L030}{}{}{« diximus; procedentes similiter in Mercurio et Venere, inveniemus summam Veneris in utroque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L031}{}{}{« simul orbe distantiam aequari Solis distantiae minimae, quam explicuit \Name{Ptolemaeus}. Cu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L032}{}{}{« ius rei indicium ex eo capimus, quod inter orbes nihil est vacui ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0375-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L033}{}{}{\Indent Addit postea \Name{al–Farghānī}, p. 81–82, distantias in radiis Terrae et in milliariis Arabicis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L034}{}{}{radio Terrae 3250 milliariorum (3) sumpto. Numeros aliquantum diversos exhibet, ante annum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-body-L035}{}{}{301 heg. (inc. 6 Aug. 913), \Name{Ibn Rosteh} p. 18–20, qui Terrae radium 3818 milliariorum}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.62}\selectfont
\par\vspace{5.49mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0375-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Apud reliquos astronomos Arabicos editos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-notes_left-L002}{}{}{nihil invenio. De \Name{Ibn Yūnus}, \Name{Delambre} p. 112}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-notes_left-L003}{}{}{tantum ait: « Chap. XVI. Durée du crepuscule. Abais-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-notes_left-L004}{}{}{« sement 18°. Rien de neuf ». — \Name{Alhazen} ($=$ \Name{Ibn}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Name{al-Haytham}), \textit{De crepusculis,} nonnisi profundi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-notes_left-L006}{}{}{tatem 19° Solis in fine crepusculi indicat.}
\end{minipage}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\textit{Naturphilosophie der Araber im zehnten Jahrhun-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\textit{dert, aus den Schriften der lautern Brüder über-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\textit{setzt,} Posen 1864, p. 27-28.}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0375-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (3) In meo \textit{Il valore metrico del grado di me-}}

\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 0.04mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L001}{}{}{facit. Demum, priore XII saeculi Chr. medietate, Hebraeo–Hispanus \Name{Abrāhām bar Ḥiyyā} (1),}
\par\vspace{-0.04mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L002}{}{}{distantias in radiis ($=$ 3250 milliariorum) milliariisque indicat, et addit: « Hic quem exposui-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L003}{}{}{« mus calculus est secundum opinionem viri appellati \Name{al–Battānī}, unius e sapientibus qui hoc}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L004}{}{}{« argumentum investigaverunt tempore memorati regis al–Ma’mūn [sic!]. Ille fuit qui in hoc}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L005}{}{}{« calculo [distantias] minuit quas ante eum sapientes metiti erant; veteres enim multum his}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L006}{}{}{« quantitatibus addebant ». Distantiae in radiis Terrae iuxta hos tres auctores sunt:}
\par\vspace{4.82mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L007}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{92.31mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{Al–Farghānī}}}\rlap{\hspace*{129.12mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{Ibn Rosteh}}}\rlap{\hspace*{162.02mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{Bar Ḥiyyā}}}}
\par\vspace{0.41mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L008}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{14.53mm}\smash{\rlap{Minima distantia Lunae}}}\rlap{\hspace*{92.77mm}\smash{\llap{33}}}\rlap{\hspace*{94.37mm}\smash{\rlap{$\qfrac{1}{2}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{100.77mm}\smash{\rlap{$\qfrac{1}{20}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{108.46mm}\smash{\rlap{(2)}}}\rlap{\hspace*{119.62mm}\smash{\rlap{. . . . .}}}\rlap{\hspace*{163.40mm}\smash{\llap{33}}}\rlap{\hspace*{165.31mm}\smash{\rlap{$\qfrac{1}{2}$}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L009}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{14.53mm}\smash{\rlap{Maxima \MoonSym{}, minima Mercurii}}}\rlap{\hspace*{92.77mm}\smash{\llap{64}}}\rlap{\hspace*{94.37mm}\smash{\rlap{$\qfrac{1}{6}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{129.29mm}\smash{\llap{64}}}\rlap{\hspace*{131.04mm}\smash{\rlap{$\qfrac{1}{6}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{137.98mm}\smash{\rlap{(3)}}}\rlap{\hspace*{163.40mm}\smash{\llap{64}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L010}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{14.53mm}\smash{\rlap{Maxima {\MoonFont ☿}, minima Veneris}}}\rlap{\hspace*{92.77mm}\smash{\llap{167}}}\rlap{\hspace*{129.29mm}\smash{\llap{166}}}\rlap{\hspace*{163.40mm}\smash{\llap{166}}}}
\par\vspace{-0.04mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L011}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{14.53mm}\smash{\rlap{Maxima {\MoonFont ♀}, minima Solis}}}\rlap{\hspace*{92.77mm}\smash{\llap{1120}}}\rlap{\hspace*{129.29mm}\smash{\llap{1079}}}\rlap{\hspace*{163.40mm}\smash{\llap{1080}}}\rlap{\hspace*{165.95mm}\smash{\rlap{(4)}}}}
\par\vspace{0.05mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L012}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{14.53mm}\smash{\rlap{Maxima \SunSym{}, minima Martis}}}\rlap{\hspace*{92.77mm}\smash{\llap{1220}}}\rlap{\hspace*{129.29mm}\smash{\llap{1260}}}\rlap{\hspace*{163.40mm}\smash{\llap{1146}}}}
\par\vspace{-0.05mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L013}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{14.53mm}\smash{\rlap{Maxima {\MoonFont ♂}, minima Iovis}}}\rlap{\hspace*{92.77mm}\smash{\llap{8876}}}\rlap{\hspace*{129.29mm}\smash{\llap{8820}}}\rlap{\hspace*{131.68mm}\smash{\rlap{(5)}}}\rlap{\hspace*{163.40mm}\smash{\llap{8000}}}\rlap{\hspace*{165.84mm}\smash{\rlap{(6)}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L014}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{14.53mm}\smash{\rlap{Maxima {\MoonFont ♃}, minima Saturni}}}\rlap{\hspace*{92.77mm}\smash{\llap{14405}}}\rlap{\hspace*{129.29mm}\smash{\llap{14187}}}\rlap{\hspace*{131.73mm}\smash{\rlap{(7)}}}\rlap{\hspace*{163.40mm}\smash{\llap{12400}}}\rlap{\hspace*{165.84mm}\smash{\rlap{(8)}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L015}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{14.53mm}\smash{\rlap{Maxima Saturni}}}\rlap{\hspace*{92.77mm}\smash{\llap{20110}}}\rlap{\hspace*{129.29mm}\smash{\llap{19880}}}\rlap{\hspace*{163.40mm}\smash{\llap{18000}}}\rlap{\hspace*{165.85mm}\smash{\rlap{(9)}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L016}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{14.53mm}\smash{\rlap{Stellarum fixarum}}}\rlap{\hspace*{92.77mm}\smash{\llap{20110}}}\rlap{\hspace*{129.29mm}\smash{\llap{20000}}}\rlap{\hspace*{163.40mm}\smash{\llap{18000}}}\rlap{\hspace*{165.85mm}\smash{\rlap{(9)}}}}
\par\vspace{4.42mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0376-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L017}{}{}{\Indent Principium quo haec distantiarum theoria nititur, Arabes seriores quidam ipsi \Name{Ptole-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L018}{}{}{\Name{maeo} tribuunt. Ita, XIII Chr. saec., \Name{al–Qazwīnī}, \textit{Cosmographia,} Kahirae 1311 heg. (in}
\par\vspace{-0.05mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L019}{}{}{marg. ad–Damīrī), t. I, p. 26, tract. I: « \Name{Ptolemaeus} memoravit spissitudinem (10) sphaera-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L020}{}{}{« rum caelestium, et magnitudinem corporis, circumferentiae et diametrorum cuiusque pla-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L021}{}{}{« netae. Id difficillimum ne putes; difficile enim non est nisi ignorantibus scientiam geometri-}
\par\vspace{0.00mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L022}{}{}{« cam. Qui secundum librum Euclidis absolverit, si perspicax fuerit hoc facile inveniet ». Et}
\par\vspace{-0.04mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L023}{}{}{in capitibus sequentibus (II–IX) eiusdem I tractatus indicat quot milliaria spissitudo sphaerae}
\par\vspace{0.04mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L024}{}{}{cuiusque septem planetarum complectatur, « secundum opinionem \Name{Ptolemaei}, observationum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L025}{}{}{« auctoris, qui eam deduxit demonstrationibus geometricis ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0376-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L026}{}{}{\Indent Profecto al–Qazwīnī auctoritas haud magna est in his rebus. Sed memoratu dignum est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L027}{}{}{testimonium celeberrimi astronomi \Name{al–Bīrūnī}, \textit{India,} cap. LV, p. 234–36 text., t. II, p. 68–}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L028}{}{}{–69 vers. Exponit ille doctrinam distantiarum quam, anno 161 heg. (inc. 8 Oct. 777 Chr.), ex}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L029}{}{}{libris Indorum collexerat \Name{Ya‘qūb ibn Ṭāriq}; hoc est inter maximam distantiam planetae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L030}{}{}{inferioris et minimam superioris intercedere spatium aequale diametro planetae inferioris (11).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0376-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-body-L031}{}{}{\Indent Nam, radio Terrae tanquam unitate mensurae sumpto, \Name{Ya‘qūb} ponebat:}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Name{Hispano filio R. Haijae}. \textit{Osw. Schrecken-}}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0376-notes_left-L007}{}{}{milliariorum innumeris mendis scatent.}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L002}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{33.53mm}\smash{\rlap{Luna}}}\rlap{\hspace*{74.47mm}\smash{\llap{35}}}\rlap{\hspace*{76.33mm}\smash{\rlap{$\qfrac{15}{21}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.11mm}\smash{\llap{56}}}\rlap{\hspace*{106.92mm}\smash{\rlap{$\qfrac{4}{21}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{135.91mm}\smash{\llap{4}}}\rlap{\hspace*{138.04mm}\smash{\rlap{$\qfrac{16}{21}$}}}}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L005}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{33.53mm}\smash{\rlap{Sol}}}\rlap{\hspace*{74.42mm}\smash{\llap{1114}}}\rlap{\hspace*{76.23mm}\smash{\rlap{$\qfrac{6}{21}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.22mm}\smash{\llap{2104}}}\rlap{\hspace*{107.35mm}\smash{\rlap{$\qfrac{16}{21}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{136.23mm}\smash{\llap{19}}}\rlap{\hspace*{138.20mm}\smash{\rlap{$\qfrac{1}{21}$}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L006}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{33.53mm}\smash{\rlap{Mars}}}\rlap{\hspace*{74.47mm}\smash{\llap{2123}}}\rlap{\hspace*{76.44mm}\smash{\rlap{$\qfrac{17}{21}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.33mm}\smash{\llap{8000}}}\rlap{\hspace*{136.34mm}\smash{\llap{19}}}\rlap{\hspace*{138.36mm}\smash{\rlap{$\qfrac{1}{21}$}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L007}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{33.53mm}\smash{\rlap{Iupiter}}}\rlap{\hspace*{74.63mm}\smash{\llap{8019}}}\rlap{\hspace*{76.55mm}\smash{\rlap{$\qfrac{1}{21}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.43mm}\smash{\llap{13714}}}\rlap{\hspace*{107.35mm}\smash{\rlap{$\qfrac{6}{21}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{136.34mm}\smash{\llap{19}}}\rlap{\hspace*{138.41mm}\smash{\rlap{$\qfrac{1}{21}$}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L008}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{33.53mm}\smash{\rlap{Saturnus}}}\rlap{\hspace*{74.63mm}\smash{\llap{13733}}}\rlap{\hspace*{76.60mm}\smash{\rlap{$\qfrac{7}{21}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.11mm}\smash{\llap{17161}}}\rlap{\hspace*{107.83mm}\smash{\rlap{$\qfrac{19}{21}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{136.44mm}\smash{\llap{19}}}\rlap{\hspace*{138.52mm}\smash{\rlap{$\qfrac{1}{21}$}}}}
