\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L009}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{33.53mm}\smash{\rlap{Zodiacus}}}\rlap{\hspace*{74.84mm}\smash{\llap{19047}}}\rlap{\hspace*{76.76mm}\smash{\rlap{$\qfrac{13}{21}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{85.96mm}\smash{\rlap{(1)}}}}
\par\vspace{4.85mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0377-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L010}{}{}{\Indent Postea \Name{al–Bīrūnī} addit: « Haec opinio ab ea differt supra quam \Name{Ptolemaeus}, in libro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L011}{}{}{« \textit{al–manshūrāt,} calculum distantiarum condidit, quem veteres et recentiores secuti sunt. Ho-}
\par\vspace{-0.05mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L012}{}{}{« rum enim principium est maximam cuiuslibet planetae distantiam esse minimam planetae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L013}{}{}{« statim superioris, nec inter ambas sphaeras exstare locum actione destitutum. — Sed iuxta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L014}{}{}{« illam [\Name{Indorum}] opinionem inter duas sphaeras est locus ambabus vacuus, et in eo [ali-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L015}{}{}{« quid] prehendens instar axis [rotae], circa quod rotatio fit; quasi ii credidissent aliquid gra-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L016}{}{}{« vitatis in aethere esse, ita ut sphaerae interiori [res] prehendens opus esset quae eam in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L017}{}{}{« medio [sphaerae] exterioris teneret ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0377-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L018}{}{}{\Indent Librum \textit{al–manshūrāt,} aliunde ignotum, memoratum quoque invenio in libello \Name{al–Bī-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L019}{}{}{\Name{rūnī} \textit{de elementis astronomiae,} fol. 33,r., ubi de quaestione simili distantiarum planetarum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L020}{}{}{agitur. « An quantitates corporum planetarum cognoscantur. Diametrus Lunae nota est iuxta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L021}{}{}{« rationem qua semidiametrus Terrae 1 sumitur; eam \Name{Ptolemaeus} a parallaxe deduxit.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L022}{}{}{« Similiter diametrus Solis nota est eadem ratione; eam in V libro \textit{Almagesti} ab eclipsibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L023}{}{}{« deduxit. Rationes autem diametrorum planetarum et fixarum ad diametrum Solis notae quo-}
\par\vspace{-0.03mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L024}{}{}{« que sunt, ex aliorum sententia, ab observatione, ut narratur in libro \textit{al–manshūrāt.} Hoc}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L025}{}{}{« nos in tabula posuimus iuxta opinionem et inventionem \Name{Ptolemaei}, quoniam aliorum sen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L026}{}{}{« tentiae nobis tam firmae non videntur » (2).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0377-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L027}{}{}{\Indent Demum referre oportet locum astrologiae \Name{Ibn Hibintà}, fol. 210,v., cum quo compa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L028}{}{}{randus \Name{al–Battānī} p. 123, l. 4–8. Narrat Ibn Hibintà: « \Name{Ptolemaeus} ait molem omnium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L029}{}{}{« stellarum primae magnitudinis sexies et nonagies Terram complecti; moles postea pro sin-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L030}{}{}{« gulis reliquis stellarum ordinibus minui quantitate sexdecies Terram complectente, usque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L031}{}{}{« quo ad \textit{as–Suhà} (3) perveniatur, quae est omnium minima stella visibilis in zodiaco [sic!];}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L032}{}{}{« eius molem sexdecies complecti Terram, nec ultra eam aliquid visibile esse. — Sed Muḥam-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L033}{}{}{« mad ibn Ǵābir \Name{al–Battānī}, qui anno 295 hegirae in urbe ar–Raqqah observabat, narrat}
\par\vspace{0.06mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L034}{}{}{« stellas omnes primae magnitudinis centies octies Terram complecti; [in sequentibus magni-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-body-L035}{}{}{« tudinis ordinibus] ordinatim decrescere usque ad as–Suhà, quam ponit duodevicies Terram}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{1.61mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Name{ney}, p. 397). Ponit omnes planetas habere eandem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-notes_left-L002}{}{}{mediam velocitatem \textit{linearem}; Lunam 32 diametris}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-notes_left-L003}{}{}{terrestribus abesse; reliquorum planetarum distan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-notes_left-L004}{}{}{tias tanto maiores esse distantia Lunae quanto eo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-notes_left-L005}{}{}{rum revolutiones sidereae maiores revolutione side-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-notes_left-L006}{}{}{rea Lunae.}
\par\vspace{1.16mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0377-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-notes_left-L007}{}{}{\Indent (1) Est igitur inter maximam distantiam Saturni}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-notes_left-L008}{}{}{et zodiacum spatium vacuum 1885 $\qfrac{15}{21}$ radiorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-notes_left-L009}{}{}{terrestrium.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.70}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.93mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0377-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (2) \ArabicRun{وقد وضعنا ذلك في جدول بحسب راي بطلميوس}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\ArabicRun{ووجوده اذ لم يتقرر عندنا راي غيره الى هذه الغاية}. Sequitur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-notes_right-L003}{}{}{tabula diametrorum (respectu Solis) pertinentium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-notes_right-L004}{}{}{ad singulos planetas et ad sex ordines magnitudinis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-notes_right-L005}{}{}{stellarum fixarum; sed numeri haud paulum mendosi.}
\par\vspace{0.05mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0377-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\Indent (3) Prope \textgreek{ζ} Ursae maioris; deest in Catalogo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-notes_right-L007}{}{}{stellarum Ptolemaei, nec conspicitur nisi ab iis qui}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-notes_right-L008}{}{}{magna oculorum acie praediti sint. Cfr. \Name{aṣ-Ṣūfī}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0377-notes_right-L009}{}{}{pag. 50.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0378}{289}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr -0.37mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L001}{}{}{« complecti » (1). — Quae de \Name{Albatenio} narrat haud bene cum textu cod. Escurialensis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L002}{}{}{et Platonis conveniunt; quae de \Name{Ptolemaeo}, in operibus eius cognitis non reperiuntur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0378-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L003}{}{}{\Indent Nunc Graecos interrogemus. \Name{Schiaparelli} mihi locum suppeditat e \Name{Procli} \textit{Hypo-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L004}{}{}{\textit{typosibus} (2), ubi haec distantiarum supputandarum ratio memoratur, Lunam, Mercurium,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L005}{}{}{Venerem et Solem quod attinet. Ait enim « quosdam (\textgreek{τίνες}), ponentes nullum spatium vacuum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L006}{}{}{« in mundo exstare et omnia intervalla impleri sphaeris intermediis », supputavisse eorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L007}{}{}{planetarum distantias. Maxima Lunae 64\textsuperscript{r} 10′ radiorum terrestrium, minima Solis 1160\textsuperscript{r} [ex}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L008}{}{}{Almagesto] sumptis, « invenisse demonstratam [a Ptolemaeo] distantiam apogei epicycli Mer-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L009}{}{}{« curii a centro zodiaci [vel Terrae] esse ad distantiam perigei ut 91\textsuperscript{r} 30′ ad 34\textsuperscript{r} 4′. Facien-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L010}{}{}{« tes postea 34\textsuperscript{r} 4′ ad 91\textsuperscript{r} 30′ ut maxima distantia Lunae (64\textsuperscript{r} 10′) ad quartum terminum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L011}{}{}{« quemdam, ex hac proportione invenisse 277\textsuperscript{r} 33′ quae sint distantia apogea Mercurii ..... Item}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L012}{}{}{« Veneris distantiam perigeam a centro zodiaci sumentes ad apogeam ut 15\textsuperscript{r} 35′ ad 204\textsuperscript{r} 25′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L013}{}{}{« esse, eandemque intercedere rationem inter distantiam apogeam Mercurii (277\textsuperscript{r} 33′) et quar-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L014}{}{}{« tum alium terminum, hunc invenisse 1150\textsuperscript{r}, qui fere sunt distantia perigea Solis (1160) ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0378-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L015}{}{}{\Indent Hi calculi, quorum elementa ab \textit{Almagesto} sumpta sunt, facile potuerunt ab Arabibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L016}{}{}{tribui ipsi Ptolemaeo. Sed hoc iam \Name{Graecos} fecisse me \Name{Schiaparelli} monet. \Name{Simpli-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L017}{}{}{\Name{cius} (3) enim, qui posterior est Proclo, narrat: « Mercurium sub Venere consistere osten-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L018}{}{}{« dunt observationes in quibus Mercurius sub Venere currens deprehenditur. Id etiam de-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L019}{}{}{« monstratur ratione distantiae eorum apogeorum et perigeorum; nam maximam Veneris}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L020}{}{}{« distantiam esse fere distantiam Solis demonstratur, qua re Venerem proximam esse Soli;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L021}{}{}{« item maximam Mercurii aequalem fere minimae Veneris, et maximam Lunae aequalem fere}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L022}{}{}{« minimae Mercurii. Haec enim demonstrantur in \textit{Syntaxi} [$=$ Almagesto] \Name{Ptolemaei}, ratio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L023}{}{}{« nem illam deducendo ab excentricitate horum planetarum respectu centri Terrae » (4).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0378-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Simplicius} summam operam posuerat in studio librorum Procli, quem « magistrum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L025}{}{}{« magistri mei » appellat. Fortasse, ut \Name{Schiaparelli} recte coniicit, doctrinas Almagesti non}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L026}{}{}{e textu originali, intellectu difficillimo, sed ex aliquo compendio, ut Hypotyposes (« Primae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L027}{}{}{« lineae seu rudimenta » motuum planetarum) sunt, cognoverat; itaque facile censere potuit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L028}{}{}{omnia quae Proclus in iis narrat, et etiam calculum distantiarum planetarum, a Ptolemaeo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L029}{}{}{provenire. — Res cum ita sint, haud improbabile puto illum \textit{Kitāb al–manshūrāt,} quem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L030}{}{}{nonnisi in duobus iis locis \Name{al–Bīrūnī} memoratum invenio, « Librum rerum vulgatarum [de}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L031}{}{}{« astronomia] » significare, quae versio esset nominis Graeci \textgreek{Ὑποτυπώσεις}; \Name{Ptolemaei} igitur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L032}{}{}{non esse, sed \Name{Procli}; Arabum quosdam demum in eundem errorem lapsos esse in quo Sim-}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L033}{}{}{plicius iam versatus erat.}
\par\vspace{0.56mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0378-H34}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L034}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{b}}) — \textit{Pag. 120–122.}}}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0378-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L035}{}{}{\Indent [I. \textit{Diametri planetarum respectu Terrae.} Hoc modo supputantur: Sit \textgreek{Δ} diametrus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-body-L036}{}{}{angularis apparens Solis, \textit{R} eius distantia media; diametrus linearis Solis erit R\textgreek{Δ}. Item sit \textgreek{δ}}

\par\vspace{1.04mm}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-notes_left-L005}{}{}{C. \Name{Ptolémée}, \textit{et Hypotyposes de} \Name{Proclus Dia-}}

\par\vspace{5.83mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\textit{mentaria, ed. Heiberg,} Berolini 1894, p. 474 ($=$ Com-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-notes_right-L002}{}{}{mentaria in Aristotelem Graeca edita consilio et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-notes_right-L003}{}{}{auctoritate Academiae litterarum regiae Borussicae,}

\par\vspace{1.12mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0378-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\Indent (4) \textgreek{Ταῦτα γὰρ δέδεικται ἐν τῇ Πτολεμαίου Συντά}-}
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-notes_right-L007}{}{}{\textgreek{ξει μεταφερομένου τοῦ λόγου τῆς τῶν ἀστέρων ἐκκεν}-}
\par\vspace{-0.02mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0378-notes_right-L008}{}{}{\textgreek{τρότητος εἰς τὸν λόγον τῆς ἐκ τοῦ κέντρου τῆς γῆς}.}
\end{minipage}
\par\vspace{5mm}\hfill{\fontsize{8}{10}\selectfont 37}\hspace{10mm}
\DSource{0379}{290}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 0.14mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L001}{}{}{diametrus angularis apparens planetae, \textit{r} eius distantia media; erit \textit{r}\textgreek{δ} diametrus linearis pla-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L002}{}{}{netae. Itaque}
\par\vspace{-1.66mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0379-H03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L003}{}{}{\CenterLine{$\dfrac{\text{diametrus planetae}}{\text{diametrus Solis}} = \dfrac{r\text{\textgreek{δ}}}{R\Delta} = \dfrac{r}{R} \times \dfrac{\text{\textgreek{δ}}}{\Delta}$.}}
\par\vspace{1.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L004}{}{}{Diametrus Solis complectitur 5 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ diametros terrestres; ergo}
\par\vspace{-0.47mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0379-H05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L005}{}{}{\CenterLine{$\dfrac{\text{diametrus planetae}}{\text{diametrus Terrae}} = \text{5 $\qfrac{1}{2}$} \times \dfrac{r}{R} \times \dfrac{\text{\textgreek{δ}}}{\Delta} = r \times \dfrac{\text{\textgreek{δ}}}{\Delta} \times \dfrac{1}{\left(\dfrac{R}{\text{5 $\qfrac{1}{2}$}}\right)}$.}}
\par\vspace{-5.95mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L006}{}{}{Sed $R = 1108$, $\dfrac{R}{\text{5 $\qfrac{1}{2}$}} = \text{201 $\qfrac{5}{11}$}$, ergo}
\par\vspace{-1.72mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0379-H07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L007}{}{}{\CenterLine{$\dfrac{\text{diametrus planetae}}{\text{diametrus Terrae}} = r \times \dfrac{\text{\textgreek{δ}}}{\Delta} \times \dfrac{1}{\text{201 $\qfrac{5}{11}$}}$,}}
\par\vspace{0.05mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L008}{}{}{quae est regula \Name{Albatenii}, seu potius eorum qui ante eum hos calculos instituerant. Mani-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L009}{}{}{feste cum de ratione observata inter diametros planetarum diametrumque Solis loquitur, vult}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L010}{}{}{semper rationem \textit{diametrorum apparentium,} non verarum.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0379-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L011}{}{}{\Indent II. \textit{Arcus quos diametri apparentes epicyclorum in oculo subtendunt.} Diametrus ap-}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{147.92mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L012}{}{}{parens epicycli est angulus contentus duabus lineis tangentibus ductis ab}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L013}{}{}{oculo ad utramque epicycli partem, scilicet angulus AXB. — In \textit{Mercurio}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L014}{}{}{et \textit{Venere} hic angulus manifeste est summa digressionis maximae et mi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L015}{}{}{nimae, ut al–Battānī habet. — Aliter in planetis superioribus. \textit{Martem}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L016}{}{}{quod attinet, \Name{Ptolemaeus} X, 8 (ed. Halma t. II, 236) demonstrat radium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L017}{}{}{excentrici esse ad radium epicycli ut 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} ad 39\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 30′, hoc est ut 1 ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L018}{}{}{0,65833.... Hic numerus (vel 39\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 30′) est sinus anguli OXB, qui in oculo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L019}{}{}{subtendit radium OB epicycli; ergo OXB $=$ 41° 10′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$, et AXB $=$ 82° 21′.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L020}{}{}{— In \textit{Iove} \Name{Ptolemaeus} XI, 2 (ed. Halma t. II, p. 262) statuit radium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L021}{}{}{excentrici esse ad radium epicycli ut 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} ad 11\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 30′, vel ut 1 ad 0,19666.....}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{38.08mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0379-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0379-F01}\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{-0.34mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L022}{}{}{Itaque angulus OXB $=$ 11° 3′, et diametrus angularis epicycli AXB $=$ 22° 6′. — In \textit{Saturno}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L023}{}{}{\Name{Ptolemaeus} XI, 6 (ed. Halma t. II, p. 287) statuit radium excentrici ad radium epicycli ut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L024}{}{}{60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} ad 6\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 30′, vel ut 1 ad 0.10833.... Itaque OXB $=$ 6° 13′, et AXB $=$ 12° 26′.— \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0379-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L025}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Tycho Brahe}, \textit{Astronomiae instauratae progymnasmata,} Uraniburgi et Pragae 1610,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L026}{}{}{t. I, p. 467–71, exponit quae de distantiis magnitudinibusque corporum caelestium al–Farghānī}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L027}{}{}{et al–Battānī dicunt, postea docens « qua Methodo et demonstrationis processu Albategnius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L028}{}{}{« potissimum usus sit, in Stellarum Magnitudinibus praefiniendis ex data eorum distantia et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L029}{}{}{« visibili Diametro in aliquota parte solaris diametri, iuxta apparentiam ».}
\par\vspace{5.21mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0379-H30}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L030}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{c}}) — \textit{Pag. 122, l. 33.}}}
\par\vspace{4.42mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0379-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L031}{}{}{\Indent [« \textit{Diametrus Solis subtendit arcum 39° 48′} ». Quid hoc sibi velit, nescio. Si de \textit{epi-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L032}{}{}{\textit{cyclo} Solis agitur, eius diametrus est duplum maximae aequationis centri, ergo 3° 58′, quod}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L033}{}{}{minime convenit cum 39° 48′ codicis (hic et p. 123, l. 21) et 49° 48′ vel 9° 48′ Platonis. Si de}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L034}{}{}{\textit{diametro} apparenti \textit{Solis} agitur, eius quantitatem mediam \Name{al–Battānī}, pag. 58, statuit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L035}{}{}{32′ 30″. Est igitur stulta interpolatio censenda, ut ea de qua in adnotatione sequenti dicam.}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L036}{}{}{— \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{0.36mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0379-H37}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L037}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{d}}) — \textit{Pag. 123, l. 18–20 et 20–30.}}}
\par\vspace{4.46mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0379-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L038}{}{}{\Indent [Totus hic locus, in quo omnes fere codicis numeri a numeris Platonis differunt, sanari}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L039}{}{}{nequit; attamen antea omnia recte procedebant. Manifeste non \Name{al–Battānī}, sed ineptissimus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0379-body-L040}{}{}{interpolator diametros apparentes maximas, medias, minimas planetarum omnium addere co-}
\end{document}
